function uepManagementRule(group='general'){
  return UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES[group]||UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.general;
}
let __uepManagementFiveGroupCache=null;
function uepManagementFiveGroupKey(avg5){
  const value=Number(avg5);
  return Number.isFinite(value)?value.toFixed(2):null;
}
function uepManagementExpandedFiveGroups(){
  const students=readonlyCache?.students||[], scores=readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[];
  const signature=[students,scores,students.length,scores.length];
  const cached=__uepManagementFiveGroupCache;
  if(cached&&signature.every((v,i)=>cached.signature[i]===v))return cached;
  const rows=students.filter(student=>String(student.status||'재학')==='재학').map(student=>{
    const base=admissionBase(student.id);
    return {actual9:Number(base.actual9),fiveKey:uepManagementFiveGroupKey(base.avg5)};
  });
  const inseoul=new Set(rows.filter(row=>Number.isFinite(row.actual9)&&row.actual9<=UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.inseoul.internal&&row.fiveKey).map(row=>row.fiveKey));
  const regional=new Set(rows.filter(row=>Number.isFinite(row.actual9)&&row.actual9<=UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.regional.internal&&row.fiveKey).map(row=>row.fiveKey));
  __uepManagementFiveGroupCache={signature,inseoul,regional};
  return __uepManagementFiveGroupCache;
}
function uepBaseManagementGroup(actual9,avg5=null){
  const value=Number(actual9),fiveKey=uepManagementFiveGroupKey(avg5);
  if(!Number.isFinite(value))return 'general';
  const expanded=fiveKey?uepManagementExpandedFiveGroups():null;
  if(value<=UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.inseoul.internal||(expanded&&expanded.inseoul.has(fiveKey)))return 'inseoul';
  if(value<=UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.regional.internal||(expanded&&expanded.regional.has(fiveKey)))return 'regional';
  return 'general';
}
function uepMockCoreFor(group,mock={}){
  if(group==='inseoul')return mock.mainLevels?.length>=3&&Number(mock.mainWithin2||0)>=2&&mock.mainLevels.every(v=>v<=3);
  if(group==='regional')return Number(mock.mainWithin4||0)>=2&&Boolean(mock.allWithin5);
  return false;
}
function uepMockPotentialFor(group,mock={}){
  if(group==='inseoul')return mock.mainLevels?.length>=3&&Number(mock.mainWithin3||0)>=2&&mock.mainLevels.every(v=>v<=4);
  if(group==='regional')return mock.mainLevels?.length>=3&&Number(mock.mainWithin4||0)>=1&&mock.mainLevels.every(v=>v<=5);
  return false;
}
function uepManagementStage(internalOk,mockCore,mockPotential){
  if(internalOk&&mockCore)return 'core';
  if(internalOk&&mockPotential)return 'growth';
  if(internalOk||mockCore)return 'interest';
  return 'general';

function dormScoreRows(studentId){return (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(row=>String(row.studentId||'')===String(studentId||''));}
function dormAverage(rows){const values=rows.map(row=>Number(row.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);return values.length?values.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/values.length:null;}
function dormInternalTrend(student,term){
  const rows=dormScoreRows(student.id).filter(row=>row.scoreType==='내신');
  const terms=[['1-1','2026','1'],['1-2','2026','2'],['2-1','2027','1'],['2-2','2027','2'],['3','2028','']];
  const limit=term.grade===3?4:(term.grade-1)*2+(term.semester==='2'?1:0);
  return terms.slice(0,limit+1).map(([label,year,semester])=>({label,value:dormAverage(rows.filter(row=>(!row.schoolYear||String(row.schoolYear)===year)&&(!semester||!row.semester||String(row.semester).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'')===semester)))})).filter(x=>x.value!=null);
}
function dormMockTrend(student,term){
  const order=['3월모의고사','6월모의고사','9월모의고사','10월모의고사','11월모의고사','수능'];
  const rows=dormScoreRows(student.id).filter(row=>row.scoreType==='모의고사');
  const years=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>String(row.schoolYear||'')).filter(Boolean))].sort();
  const allowedYears=years.filter(year=>Number(year)<=Number(term.year));
  const result=[];
  allowedYears.forEach(year=>order.forEach(exam=>{const avg=dormAverage(rows.filter(row=>String(row.schoolYear||'')===year&&row.exam===exam));if(avg!=null)result.push({label:`${year.slice(-2)}.${exam.replace('모의고사','').replace('월','')}`,value:avg});}));
  return result.slice(-5);
}
function dormSparkline(points){
  if(!points.length)return '<span class="dorm-spark-empty">자료 대기</span>';
  const w=118,h=30,p=4,vals=points.map(x=>x.value),min=Math.min(...vals),max=Math.max(...vals),range=Math.max(.4,max-min);
  if(points.length===1){
    return `<svg class="dorm-spark dorm-spark-one" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="${w/2}" cy="${h/2}" r="3.2"/></svg>`;
  }
  const coords=points.map((x,i)=>`${p+i*(w-p*2)/(points.length-1)},${p+(x.value-min)*(h-p*2)/range}`).join(' ');
  const dots=coords.split(' ').map(pair=>{const [x,y]=pair.split(',');return `<circle cx="${x}" cy="${y}" r="2.1"/>`;}).join('');
  return `<svg class="dorm-spark" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="${coords}" fill="none" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/>${dots}</svg>`;
}
function dormManagementBadge(student){
  const candidate=counselPriorityCandidateForStudent(student);if(!candidate)return {label:'',reason:'안정 추적',candidate:null};
  const score=Number(candidate.score||0);const rank=score>=110?'관리 우선 ①':score>=80?'관리 우선 ②':'관리 확인';
  return {label:rank,reason:(candidate.tags||candidate.reasons||[])[0]||'상담 확인',candidate};
}

function printScorePackage(student,mode){
  if(!student)return;
  const all=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(r=>r.studentId===student.id);
  const internal=all.filter(r=>r.scoreType==="내신");
  const mock=all.filter(r=>r.scoreType==="모의고사");
  const gradeRows=internal.filter(r=>Number.isFinite(Number(r.level)));
  const avg=gradeRows.length?(gradeRows.reduce((s,r)=>s+Number(r.level),0)/gradeRows.length).toFixed(2):"-";
  const mockExams=[...new Set(mock.map(r=>r.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b));
  const latestMock=mockExams.at(-1)||"-";
  const subjects=[...new Set(mock.map(r=>r.subject).filter(Boolean))];
  const summary=`<div class="score-print-summary"><div><small>최근 내신 평균</small><b>${avg}</b><span>등급 평균</span></div><div><small>내신 자료</small><b>${internal.length}</b><span>과목·시험 기록</span></div><div><small>최근 모의고사</small><b>${escapeHtml(latestMock)}</b><span>${mock.length}개 성적</span></div><div><small>조회범위</small><b>${mode==="combined"?"종합":mode==="mock"?"모의":"내신"}</b><span>개인별 누적</span></div></div>`;
  const internalExams=[...new Set(internal.map(r=>r.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b));
  const internalSubjects=[...new Set(internal.map(r=>r.subject).filter(Boolean))];
  const internalTable=`<section class="print-section"><h2>내신 누적 성적</h2><table class="score-wide"><thead><tr><th>과목</th>${internalExams.map(e=>`<th>${escapeHtml(e)}<small>등급 / 원점수</small></th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${internalSubjects.map(subject=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(subject)}</td>${internalExams.map(exam=>{const r=internal.find(x=>x.subject===subject&&x.exam===exam);return `<td>${r?`<b>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.level))}등급</b><small>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.rawScore))}점 · ${escapeHtml(r.achievement||"")}</small>`:"-"}</td>`}).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></section>`;
  const mockTable=`<section class="print-section"><h2>모의고사 비교</h2><table class="score-wide mock"><thead><tr><th>영역</th>${mockExams.map(e=>`<th>${escapeHtml(e)}<small>등급 / 원점수 / 표준</small></th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${subjects.map(subject=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(subject)}</td>${mockExams.map(exam=>{const r=mock.find(x=>x.subject===subject&&x.exam===exam);return `<td>${r?`<b>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.level))}등급</b><small>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.rawScore))}점 · 표준 ${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.standardScore))}${r.percentile!==""?` · 백분위 ${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.percentile))}`:""}</small>`:"-"}</td>`}).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></section>`;
  const body=`<header class="print-head score-title"><small>UNHO EDUCATION PLATFORM · 학생상담 성적 종합</small><h1>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</h1><p>진로희망: ${escapeHtml(student.career||"-")} · 전체 누적 성적</p>${studentPrintBadgesMarkup(student)}</header>${summary}${mode!=="mock"?internalTable:""}${mode!=="internal"?mockTable:""}`;
  openPrintDocument(`${student.studentNo}_${student.name}_성적상담자료`,body,"portrait");
}

function scorePeriodLabel(key) {
  if (!key || key === "all") return "전체 누적";
  const [year, semester] = String(key).split("-");
  return `${year}학년도 ${semester || "1"}학기`;
}
function availableScorePeriods(mode = scoreMode) {
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => mode === "combined" || row.scoreType === (mode === "mock" ? "모의고사" : "내신"));
  return [...new Set(rows.map(scorePeriodKey).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b));
}
function scoreExamOrder(exam) {
  const order = ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사","3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사","수능"];
  const index = order.indexOf(exam);
  return index < 0 ? 999 : index;
}
function scoreTrend(row, allRows) {
  const orders = row.scoreType === "모의고사" ? ["3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사"] : ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사"];
  const index = orders.indexOf(row.exam);
  if (index <= 0 || row.level === "" || row.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
  const previous = allRows.find(x => x.studentId === row.studentId && x.scoreType === row.scoreType && x.subject === row.subject && x.exam === orders[index - 1] && scorePeriodKey(x) === period);
  if (!previous || previous.level === "" || previous.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  const diff = Number(previous.level) - Number(row.level);
  if (!Number.isFinite(diff) || diff === 0) return { text:"유지", cls:"" };
  return diff > 0 ? { text:`▲ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"good" } : { text:`▼ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"bad" };
}
function gradeToneClass(value) {
  const grade = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(grade) && grade >= 1 && grade <= 9 ? `grade-${grade}` : "grade-na";
}
function gradeBadgeMarkup(value, suffix = "등급") {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">-</span>`;
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">${escapeHtml(String(value))}</span>`;
  return `<span class="grade-badge ${gradeToneClass(number)}"><b>${scoreDisplay(number,2)}</b><small>${suffix}</small></span>`;
}

  if (!rows.length) return `<span class="mock-grade-empty">자료 없음</span>`;
  const sorted = rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=order.indexOf(a.subject), bi=order.indexOf(b.subject);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi);
-----

  const grade = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(grade) && grade >= 1 && grade <= 9 ? `grade-${grade}` : "grade-na";
}
function gradeBadgeMarkup(value, suffix = "등급") {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">-</span>`;
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">${escapeHtml(String(value))}</span>`;
  return `<span class="grade-badge ${gradeToneClass(number)}"><b>${scoreDisplay(number,2)}</b><small>${suffix}</small></span>`;
}
function mockGradeMiniMarkup(studentId, exam) {
  const order = ["국어", "수학", "영어", "한국사"];
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => row.studentId === studentId && row.scoreType === "모의고사" && row.exam === exam);
  if (!rows.length) return `<span class="mock-grade-empty">자료 없음</span>`;
  const sorted = rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=order.indexOf(a.subject), bi=order.indexOf(b.subject);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi);
    return String(a.subject).localeCompare(String(b.subject),"ko");
  });
  return `<div class="mock-grade-mini-strip">${sorted.map(row=>`<span><small>${escapeHtml(row.subject)}</small><b class="${gradeToneClass(row.level)}">${escapeHtml(String(row.level || "-"))}</b></span>`).join("")}</div>`;
}
function scoreTableMarkup(rows, mode = scoreMode) {
  const coreOrder = mode === "mock" ? ["국어","수학","영어","한국사"] : ["공통국어1","공통수학1","공통영어1","한국사1","통합사회1","통합과학1","한문","과학탐구실험1","체육1","음악"];
  const subjects = [...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=coreOrder.indexOf(a), bi=coreOrder.indexOf(b);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?999:ai)-(bi<0?999:bi);
    return a.localeCompare(b,"ko");
  });
  const groups = new Map();
  rows.forEach(row=>{
    const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
    const key=`${row.studentId}|${period}|${row.exam}`;
    if(!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key,{studentId:row.studentId,studentNo:row.studentNo,name:row.name,period,exam:row.exam,items:new Map()});
    groups.get(key).items.set(row.subject,row);
  });
  const grouped=[...groups.values()].sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.studentNo)) || a.period.localeCompare(b.period) || scoreExamOrder(a.exam)-scoreExamOrder(b.exam) || String(a.exam).localeCompare(String(b.exam),"ko"));
  const cell=(row)=>{
    if(!row) return `<td class="empty-score-cell">-</td>`;
    const trend=scoreTrend(row,readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]);
    const trendMarkup=trend.text!=="-"?`<em class="trend ${trend.cls}">${trend.text}</em>`:"";
    if(row.scoreType==="모의고사") return `<td><div class="score-cell-primary">${gradeBadgeMarkup(row.level)}${trendMarkup}</div><small>원점수 ${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)} · 표준 ${scoreDisplay(row.standardScore)}${row.percentile!==""?` · 백분위 ${scoreDisplay(row.percentile)}`:""}</small></td>`;
    const gradeValue = row.level === "산출제외" ? (row.achievement || "산출제외") : row.level;
    return `<td><div class="score-cell-primary">${gradeBadgeMarkup(gradeValue, Number.isFinite(Number(gradeValue))?"등급":"")}${trendMarkup}</div><small>원점수 ${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)} · 석차 ${escapeHtml(String(row.rank||"-"))}/${escapeHtml(String(row.enrollment||"-"))}${row.achievement?` · ${escapeHtml(row.achievement)}`:""}</small></td>`;
  };
  const title = mode === "mock" ? "모의고사 과목별 등급" : "내신 과목별 등급";
  return `<div class="data-card score-matrix-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>${title}</strong></div><small>전체 누적 또는 선택 학기를 표시합니다.</small></div><div class="score-matrix-scroll"><table class="score-matrix-table"><thead><tr><th class="student-col">학생</th><th class="exam-col">학기·시험</th>${subjects.map(subject=>`<th>${escapeHtml(subject)}</th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${grouped.map(group=>`<tr><th class="student-col"><b>${escapeHtml(group.studentNo)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(group.name)}</small></th><th class="exam-col"><b>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(group.period).replace("학년도 ","· "))}</b><small>${escapeHtml(group.exam)}</small></th>${subjects.map(subject=>cell(group.items.get(subject))).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div></div>`;
}

-----
    return a.localeCompare(b,"ko");
  });
  const groups = new Map();
  rows.forEach(row=>{
    const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
    const key=`${row.studentId}|${period}|${row.exam}`;
    if(!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key,{studentId:row.studentId,studentNo:row.studentNo,name:row.name,period,exam:row.exam,items:new Map()});
    groups.get(key).items.set(row.subject,row);
  });
  const grouped=[...groups.values()].sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.studentNo)) || a.period.localeCompare(b.period) || scoreExamOrder(a.exam)-scoreExamOrder(b.exam) || String(a.exam).localeCompare(String(b.exam),"ko"));
  const cell=(row)=>{
    if(!row) return `<td class="empty-score-cell">-</td>`;
    const trend=scoreTrend(row,readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]);
    const trendMarkup=trend.text!=="-"?`<em class="trend ${trend.cls}">${trend.text}</em>`:"";
    if(row.scoreType==="모의고사") return `<td><div class="score-cell-primary">${gradeBadgeMarkup(row.level)}${trendMarkup}</div><small>원점수 ${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)} · 표준 ${scoreDisplay(row.standardScore)}${row.percentile!==""?` · 백분위 ${scoreDisplay(row.percentile)}`:""}</small></td>`;
    const gradeValue = row.level === "산출제외" ? (row.achievement || "산출제외") : row.level;
    return `<td><div class="score-cell-primary">${gradeBadgeMarkup(gradeValue, Number.isFinite(Number(gradeValue))?"등급":"")}${trendMarkup}</div><small>원점수 ${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)} · 석차 ${escapeHtml(String(row.rank||"-"))}/${escapeHtml(String(row.enrollment||"-"))}${row.achievement?` · ${escapeHtml(row.achievement)}`:""}</small></td>`;
  };
  const title = mode === "mock" ? "모의고사 과목별 등급" : "내신 과목별 등급";
  return `<div class="data-card score-matrix-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>${title}</strong></div><small>전체 누적 또는 선택 학기를 표시합니다.</small></div><div class="score-matrix-scroll"><table class="score-matrix-table"><thead><tr><th class="student-col">학생</th><th class="exam-col">학기·시험</th>${subjects.map(subject=>`<th>${escapeHtml(subject)}</th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${grouped.map(group=>`<tr><th class="student-col"><b>${escapeHtml(group.studentNo)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(group.name)}</small></th><th class="exam-col"><b>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(group.period).replace("학년도 ","· "))}</b><small>${escapeHtml(group.exam)}</small></th>${subjects.map(subject=>cell(group.items.get(subject))).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div></div>`;
}

function scoreDisplay(value, digits = 1) {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return "-";
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return escapeHtml(String(value));
  return Number.isInteger(number) ? String(number) : number.toFixed(digits).replace(/\.0+$/, "");
}
function scoreAverage(rows) {
  const values = rows.filter((row) => row.level !== "" && row.level !== null && row.level !== undefined).map((row) => Number(row.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);
  return values.length ? values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length : null;
}

  const internalSubjects = [...new Set(summaryInternal.map((row) => row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a, b) => {
    const ai = coreInternalOrder.indexOf(a), bi = coreInternalOrder.indexOf(b);
    if (ai >= 0 || bi >= 0) return (ai < 0 ? 999 : ai) - (bi < 0 ? 999 : bi);
    return a.localeCompare(b, "ko");
  });
  const byInternal = (subject, exam) => summaryInternal.find((row) => row.subject === subject && row.exam === exam);

function scoreDisplay(value, digits = 1) {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return "-";
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return escapeHtml(String(value));
  return Number.isInteger(number) ? String(number) : number.toFixed(digits).replace(/\.0+$/, "");
}
function scoreAverage(rows) {
  const values = rows.filter((row) => row.level !== "" && row.level !== null && row.level !== undefined).map((row) => Number(row.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);
  return values.length ? values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length : null;
}
function combinedScoreDashboardMarkup(student, rows) {
  const allRows = readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || [];
  const scopedRows = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows.filter(row=>row.studentId===student.id) : allRows.filter(row=>row.studentId===student.id);
  const internal = scopedRows.filter((row) => row.scoreType === "내신");
  const mock = scopedRows.filter((row) => row.scoreType === "모의고사");
  const internalPeriods = [...new Set(internal.map(scorePeriodKey))].sort();
  const latestInternalPeriod = internalPeriods.at(-1) || "";
  const summaryInternal = scorePeriod === "all" && latestInternalPeriod ? internal.filter(row=>scorePeriodKey(row)===latestInternalPeriod) : internal;
  const periodLabel = scorePeriodLabel(scorePeriod);
  const coreInternalOrder = ["공통국어1","공통수학1","공통영어1","한국사1","통합사회1","통합과학1","한문","과학탐구실험1","체육1","음악"];
  const internalSubjects = [...new Set(summaryInternal.map((row) => row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a, b) => {
    const ai = coreInternalOrder.indexOf(a), bi = coreInternalOrder.indexOf(b);
    if (ai >= 0 || bi >= 0) return (ai < 0 ? 999 : ai) - (bi < 0 ? 999 : bi);
    return a.localeCompare(b, "ko");
  });
  const byInternal = (subject, exam) => summaryInternal.find((row) => row.subject === subject && row.exam === exam);
  const internalRows = internalSubjects.map((subject) => {
    const first = byInternal(subject, "1차고사"), second = byInternal(subject, "2차고사"), final = byInternal(subject, "학기말고사");
    const finalGrade = final?.level === "산출제외" ? (final?.achievement || "산출제외") : final?.level;
    return `<div class="consult-score-row consult-internal-grid"><b>${escapeHtml(subject)}</b><span>${gradeBadgeMarkup(first?.level)}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(first?.rawScore)}</span><span>${gradeBadgeMarkup(second?.level)}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(second?.rawScore)}</span><span>${gradeBadgeMarkup(finalGrade,Number.isFinite(Number(finalGrade))?"등급":"")}${final?.achievement&&Number.isFinite(Number(finalGrade))?`<small>${escapeHtml(final.achievement)}</small>`:""}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(final?.rawScore)}</span></div>`;
  }).join("") || `<div class="consult-empty">선택 범위의 내신 성적이 없습니다.</div>`;

  const mockExamList = [...new Set(mock.map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b)||a.localeCompare(b,"ko"));
  const summaryMockExams = mockExamList.slice(-2);
  const firstMockExam = summaryMockExams[0] || "3월모의고사";
  const secondMockExam = summaryMockExams[1] || summaryMockExams[0] || "6월모의고사";
  const coreMockOrder = ["국어","수학","영어","한국사"];
  const mockSubjects = [...new Set(mock.filter(row=>summaryMockExams.includes(row.exam)).map((row) => row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=coreMockOrder.indexOf(a),bi=coreMockOrder.indexOf(b); return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi)||a.localeCompare(b,"ko");
  });
  const byMock = (subject, exam) => mock.find(row=>row.subject===subject&&row.exam===exam);
  const mockRows = mockSubjects.map(subject=>{
    const first=byMock(subject,firstMockExam), second=byMock(subject,secondMockExam);
    const trend = first && second && Number.isFinite(Number(first.level)) && Number.isFinite(Number(second.level)) ? Number(first.level)-Number(second.level) : null;
    const trendText = trend == null ? "" : trend > 0 ? `<small class="trend good">▲${Math.abs(trend)}등급</small>` : trend < 0 ? `<small class="trend bad">▼${Math.abs(trend)}등급</small>` : `<small class="trend">유지</small>`;
    return `<div class="consult-score-row consult-mock-grid"><b>${escapeHtml(subject)}</b><span>${gradeBadgeMarkup(first?.level)}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(first?.rawScore)}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(first?.standardScore)}</span><span>${gradeBadgeMarkup(second?.level)}${trendText}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(second?.rawScore)}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(second?.standardScore)}</span></div>`;
  }).join("") || `<div class="consult-empty">선택 범위의 모의고사 성적이 없습니다.</div>`;
-----

  const cumulativeTables = `<section class="consult-cumulative-scores"><header><div><small>ALL RECORDS · ${escapeHtml(periodLabel)}</small><h4>누적 성적 전체보기</h4></div><span>학기 전환 버튼으로 범위를 바꿀 수 있습니다.</span></header>${internal.length?scoreTableMarkup(internal,"internal"):""}${mock.length?scoreTableMarkup(mock,"mock"):""}</section>`;
  const actualNineText=baseSecond.actual9==null?"산출 자료 없음":`${baseSecond.actual9.toFixed(2)}등급`;
  const targetRuleMarkup=`<div class="consult-admission-rule"><span><small>현재 9등급 환산 내신</small><b>${escapeHtml(actualNineText)}</b><em>${escapeHtml(baseSecond.preferred||"최근 내신")} 기준</em></span><p><b>판정 규칙</b> 과목별 석차÷수강자 백분위를 9등급 경계(4·11·23·40·60·77·89·96%)로 환산해 평균합니다. 대학 목표 내신 이하이면 ‘충족’, 목표보다 0.7등급 이내 높으면 ‘도전’, 그보다 높으면 ‘상향’으로 표시합니다.</p></div>`;
  return `<section class="consult-dashboard"><header class="consult-student-head"><div><small>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} · ${escapeHtml(student.className)} ${escapeHtml(student.number||"")}번 · consult-grade-metrics"><span><small>${escapeHtml(latestInternalPeriod?scorePeriodLabel(latestInternalPeriod):periodLabel)} · ${escapeHtml(latestInternalExam)}</small><b>${latestInternalAverage==null?"-":latestInternalAverage.toFixed(2)}</b><em>5등급제 평균</em></span>${mockGradeCard(firstMockExam)}${mockGradeCard(secondMockExam)}<span><small>조회범위</small><b>${escapeHtml(periodLabel)}</b><em>전체 누적·학기별 전환</em></span></div><div class="consult-score-columns"><section class="consult-panel"><header><h4>최근 학기 내신 요약</h4><small>등급 우선 · 원점수 참고</small></header><div class="consult-table-scroll"><div class="consult-score-head consult-mock-grid"><span>과목</span><span>${escapeHtml(firstMockExam.replace("모의고사",""))} 등급</span><span>원점수</span><span>표준</span><span>${escapeHtml(secondMockExam.replace("모의고사",""))} 등급</span><span>원점수</span><span>표준</span></div>${mockRows}</div></section></div><section class="consult-panel consult-semester"><header><h4>학기별 누적 평균 등급</h4><small>학기말고사 · 5등급제 기준</small></header><div consult-targets"><header><h4>현재 성적 기준 대학 지원 가능성</h4><small>9등급 환산 내신과 대학별 목표 내신을 비교한 참고 판정</small></header>${targetRuleMarkup}<div class="consult-table-scroll"><div class="consult-target-head 판단</span><span>수능최저</span><span>${escapeHtml(firstMockExam.replace("모의고사",""))}</span><span>${escapeHtml(secondMockExam.replace("모의고사",""))}</span></div>${targetRows}</div></section></section>`;
}
function runScoreQuery() {
  const scope = $("#scoreScope")?.value || "class";
  const classNo = $("#scoreClass")?.value || "";
  const studentId = $("#scoreStudent")?.value || "";
  const exam = $("#scoreExam")?.value || "";
  const target = $("#scoreQueryResult");
  let rows = readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || [];
  if (scoreMode === "internal") rows = rows.filter(x => x.scoreType === "내신");
  if (scoreMode === "mock") rows = rows.filter(x => x.scoreType === "모의고사");
  if (scorePeriod !== "all") rows = rows.filter(x=>scorePeriodKey(x)===scorePeriod);
  if (scope === "individual") {
    if (!studentId) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>학생을 선택하세요.</b><span>반을 먼저 선택한 뒤 학생을 고르고 조회하세요.</span></div>`; return; }
    rows = rows.filter(x => x.studentId === studentId);
  } else {
    if (!classNo) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>반을 선택하세요.</b><span>반을 선택한 뒤 조회 버튼을 눌러야 성적이 표시됩니다.</span></div>`; return; }
    const ids = new Set((readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(s => classNumberOf(s) === String(classNo)).map(s=>s.id));
    rows = rows.filter(x => ids.has(x.studentId));
  }
  if (scoreMode === "combined" && scope !== "individual") { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>종합성적은 개인별로 조회하세요.</b><span>반과 학생을 선택하면 학사상담 대시보드가 표시됩니다.</span></div>`; return; }
  if (scoreMode === "combined" && scope === "individual") {
    const student = (readonlyCache?.students || []).find(item=>item.id===studentId);
    if (!student) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>학생자료를 찾지 못했습니다.</b><span>학생정보 연결 상태를 확인하세요.</span></div>`; return; }
    target.innerHTML = combinedScoreDashboardMarkup(student, rows); return;
  }
  if (exam) rows = rows.filter(x=>x.exam===exam);
  if (!rows.length) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>조회 결과가 없습니다.</b><span>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(scorePeriod))}의 반·시험 자료를 확인하세요.</span></div>`; return; }
  let averageRows=rows, averageLabel='5등급제 평균';
  if(scoreMode==='internal'&&scope==='individual'){
    const periods=[...new Set(rows.map(scorePeriodKey).filter(Boolean))].sort();
    const latestPeriod=periods.at(-1)||'';
    const periodRows=latestPeriod?rows.filter(row=>scorePeriodKey(row)===latestPeriod):rows;
    const preferredExam=['학기말고사','2차고사','1차고사'].find(name=>periodRows.some(row=>row.exam===name));
    averageRows=preferredExam?periodRows.filter(row=>row.exam===preferredExam):periodRows;
    averageLabel=preferredExam==='학기말고사'?'최근 학기말 평균':preferredExam?`최근 ${preferredExam} 임시평균`:'5등급제 평균';
  }
  const gradeValues=averageRows.map(x=>Number(x.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);
  const average=gradeValues.length?(gradeValues.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/gradeValues.length).toFixed(2):"-";
  const persons=new Set(rows.map(x=>x.studentId)).size, subjects=new Set(rows.map(x=>x.subject).filter(Boolean)).size;
  const modeLabel={internal:"내신",mock:"모의고사",combined:"종합"}[scoreMode]||"종합";
  const third=scoreMode==="mock"?`<span><b>${subjects}</b>과목별 등급</span>`:`<span><b>${average}</b>${escapeHtml(averageLabel)}</span>`;
  target.innerHTML=`<div class="query-summary"><span><b>${persons}</b>명</span><span><b>${rows.length}</b>성적행</span>${third}<span><b>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(scorePeriod))}</b>조회범위</span></div>${scoreTableMarkup(rows,scoreMode)}`;
}

}


function scoreStudentInfo(studentId){
  return (readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(student=>student.id===studentId)||{};
}
function scoreNumeric(value){
  const number=Number(String(value??"").replace(/[^0-9.\-]/g,""));
  return Number.isFinite(number)?number:null;
}
function scoreStatisticsRows(type,exam,classNo="all"){
  const wanted=type==="mock"?"모의고사":"내신";
  let rows=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(row=>row.scoreType===wanted&&(!exam||row.exam===exam));
  if(classNo!=="all"){
    const ids=new Set((readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(student=>classNumberOf(student)===String(classNo)).map(student=>student.id));
    rows=rows.filter(row=>ids.has(row.studentId));
  }
  return rows;
}
function scoreStatisticsStudentButton(studentId,content){
  return `<button class="score-stat-student" data-score-stat-student="${escapeHtml(studentId)}">${content}</button>`;
}

function scoreStatisticsView(){
  const rows=readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[];
  const isTrend=scoreStatisticsType==="mocktrend",isCompare=scoreStatisticsType==="mockcompare", wanted=scoreStatisticsType==="internal"?"내신":"모의고사";
  const exams=[...new Set(rows.filter(row=>row.scoreType===wanted).map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b)||String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko"));
  const subjects=[...new Set(rows.filter(row=>row.scoreType==="모의고사").map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko"));
  if(!scoreStatisticsExam||!exams.includes(scoreStatisticsExam))scoreStatisticsExam=exams.at(-1)||"";
  if(!scoreStatisticsTrendSubject||(scoreStatisticsTrendSubject!=="__all__"&&!subjects.includes(scoreStatisticsTrendSubject)))scoreStatisticsTrendSubject="__all__";
  const selector=isTrend?`<label>과목<select id="scoreStatisticsTrendSubject"><option value="__all__" ${scoreStatisticsTrendSubject==="__all__"?"selected":""}>전체 과목</option>${subjects.map(subject=>`<option ${subject===scoreStatisticsTrendSubject?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(subject)}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>`:isCompare?'':`<label>시험<select id="scoreStatisticsExam">${exams.map(exam=>`<option ${exam===scoreStatisticsExam?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(exam)}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>`;
  const result=isTrend?mockTrendStatisticsMarkup(scoreStatisticsClass):isCompare?mockAllExamComparisonMarkup(scoreStatisticsClass):(scoreStatisticsType==="mock"?mockStatisticsMarkup(scoreStatisticsExam,scoreStatisticsClass):internalStatisticsMarkup(scoreStatisticsExam,scoreStatisticsClass));
  const label=isTrend?"시험별 추이":isCompare?"전체 시험 비교":scoreStatisticsType==="mock"?"모의고사 통계":"내신 통계";
  return `<div class="score-statistics-shell"><div class="score-stat-controls"><div class="score-stat-type"><button data-score-stat-type="mock" class="${scoreStatisticsType==="mock"?"active":""}">모의고사 통계</button><button data-score-stat-type="mockcompare" class="${isCompare?"active":""}">전체 시험 비교</button><button data-score-stat-type="mocktrend" class="${isTrend?"active":""}">시험별 추이</button><button data-score-stat-type="internal" class="${scoreStatisticsType==="internal"?"active":""}">내신 통계</button></div>${selector}<label>범위<select id="scoreStatisticsClass"><option value="all">1학년 전체</option>${availableClasses().map(no=>`<option value="${no}" ${scoreStatisticsClass===no?"selected":""}>${no}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label><button class="btn primary" data-score-stat-run>통계 조회</button></div><div class="score-stat-guide"><b>${label}</b><span>기존 시험별 분포·랭킹은 유지하며, 추이 화면은 모든 모의고사를 시간순으로 비교합니다.</span></div><div id="scoreStatisticsResult">${result}</div></div>`;
}
function openStudentCombinedScore(studentId){
  const student=scoreStudentInfo(studentId);
  if(!student)return toast("학생정보를 찾지 못했습니다.");
  scoreMode="combined";
  scorePeriod="all";
  render("scores");
  setTimeout(()=>{
    const scope=$("#scoreScope"),classSelect=$("#scoreClass"),studentSelect=$("#scoreStudent");
    if(scope)scope.value="individual";
    if(classSelect)classSelect.value=classNumberOf(student);
    if(studentSelect)studentSelect.innerHTML=`<option value="">학생 선택</option>${optionStudents(classNumberOf(student),studentId)}`;
    if(studentSelect)studentSelect.value=studentId;
    $("#scoreStudentLabel")?.classList.remove("hidden");
    runScoreQuery();
  },0);
}

const UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES=Object.freeze({
  inseoul:Object.freeze({key:'inseoul',label:'인서울',groupLabel:'인서울 관리군',internal:2.5,boundary:0.35,
    mockText:'국·수·영 중 2개 영역 2등급 이내, 나머지 3등급 이내'}),
  regional:Object.freeze({key:'regional',label:'지거국',groupLabel:'지거국 관리군',internal:4.0,boundary:0.35,
    mockText:'국·수·영 중 2개 영역 4등급 이내, 전체 5등급 이내'}),
  general:Object.freeze({key:'general',label:'일반',groupLabel:'일반 관리군',internal:null,boundary:0.35,
    mockText:'최근 모의 성적의 상승 흐름 확인'})
});
function uepManagementRule(group='general'){
  return UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES[group]||UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.general;
}
let __uepManagementFiveGroupCache=null;
function uepManagementFiveGroupKey(avg5){
  const value=Number(avg5);
  return Number.isFinite(value)?value.toFixed(2):null;
}
function uepManagementExpandedFiveGroups(){
  const students=readonlyCache?.students||[], scores=readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[];
  const signature=[students,scores,students.length,scores.length];
  const cached=__uepManagementFiveGroupCache;
  if(cached&&signature.every((v,i)=>cached.signature[i]===v))return cached;
  const rows=students.filter(student=>String(student.status||'재학')==='재학').map(student=>{
    const base=admissionBase(student.id);
    return {actual9:Number(base.actual9),fiveKey:uepManagementFiveGroupKey(base.avg5)};
  });
  const inseoul=new Set(rows.filter(row=>Number.isFinite(row.actual9)&&row.actual9<=UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.inseoul.internal&&row.fiveKey).map(row=>row.fiveKey));
  const regional=new Set(rows.filter(row=>Number.isFinite(row.actual9)&&row.actual9<=UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.regional.internal&&row.fiveKey).map(row=>row.fiveKey));
  __uepManagementFiveGroupCache={signature,inseoul,regional};
  return __uepManagementFiveGroupCache;
}
function uepBaseManagementGroup(actual9,avg5=null){
  const value=Number(actual9),fiveKey=uepManagementFiveGroupKey(avg5);
  if(!Number.isFinite(value))return 'general';
  const expanded=fiveKey?uepManagementExpandedFiveGroups():null;
  if(value<=UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.inseoul.internal||(expanded&&expanded.inseoul.has(fiveKey)))return 'inseoul';
  if(value<=UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.regional.internal||(expanded&&expanded.regional.has(fiveKey)))return 'regional';
  return 'general';
}

  const expandedOk=groupKey==='seoul'?expandedGroup==='inseoul':groupKey==='regional'?['inseoul','regional'].includes(expandedGroup):false;
  if(internalLimit==null) internal=hasActual&&actual<=4.5?'near':'bad';
  else if(expandedOk) internal='ok';
  else if(hasActual&&actual<=internalLimit+0.35) internal='near';
  const seoulMock=uepMockCoreFor('inseoul',mock);
  const regionalMock=uepMockCoreFor('regional',mock);
  let mockState=groupKey==='seoul'?(seoulMock?'ok':Number(mock.mainWithin2||0)>=1?'near':'bad'):
    groupKey==='regional'?(regionalMock?'ok':Number(mock.mainWithin4||0)>=1?'near':'bad'):
    (mock.levels?.length?(Number(mock.best||99)<=4?'near':'bad'):'bad');
  const subjectState=Number(prep.subjects||0)>0?'ok':'bad';
  const activityTotal=Number(prep.selected||0)+Number(prep.reports||0);
  const activityState=activityTotal>=2?'ok':activityTotal===1?'near':'bad';
  const reportState=Number(prep.reports||0)>=2?'ok':Number(prep.reports||0)===1?'near':'bad';
  return {internal,mock:mockState,subjects:subjectState,activity:activityState,reports:reportState,internalLimit};
}
function studentCounselDetailMarkup(candidate,groupKey){
  if(!candidate)return `<section class="counsel-side-card empty"><div class="query-empty"><b>학생을 선택해 주세요.</b><span>목록에서 학생을 선택하면 현재 위치·충족 기준·부족한 부분·앞으로 준비할 내용을 이 화면에서 바로 확인할 수 있습니다.</span></div></section>`;
  const s=candidate.student, c=studentCounselCriteria(candidate,groupKey), base=candidate.base||{}, mock=candidate.mock||{}, prep=candidate.prep||{};
  const actual=Number.isFinite(Number(base.actual9))?Number(base.actual9).toFixed(2):'-';
-----
===== PATTERN: 모의고사 =====
const addDateDays = (dateText, days) => {
  const date = new Date(`${dateText || dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "";
  date.setDate(date.getDate() + Number(days || 0));
  return dateKey(date);
};
const daysFromToday = (dateText) => {
  const due = new Date(`${dateText}T12:00:00`), now = new Date(`${dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  return Number.isNaN(due.getTime()) ? null : Math.round((due-now)/86400000);
};

function ensureDevelopmentTasks(){
  const existing=new Map((state.tasks||[]).map(task=>[String(task.id||""),task]));
  for(const template of UEP_DEVELOPMENT_TASKS){
    const current=existing.get(template.id);
    if(current){
      current.project="UEP";
      current.order=template.order;
      current.status=current.done?"done":(current.status||template.status);
      current.date=current.date||dateKey(today);
      current.type=current.type||template.type;
    }else{
      state.tasks.push({...template,project:"UEP",date:dateKey(today),done:false,memo:"UEP v0.73.9 기준 개발 체크리스트"});
    }
  }

function studentCounselDetailMarkup(candidate,groupKey){
  if(!candidate)return `<section class="counsel-side-card empty"><div class="query-empty"><b>학생을 선택해 주세요.</b><span>목록에서 학생을 선택하면 현재 위치·충족 기준·부족한 부분·앞으로 준비할 내용을 이 화면에서 바로 확인할 수 있습니다.</span></div></section>`;
  const s=candidate.student, c=studentCounselCriteria(candidate,groupKey), base=candidate.base||{}, mock=candidate.mock||{}, prep=candidate.prep||{};
  const actual=Number.isFinite(Number(base.actual9))?Number(base.actual9).toFixed(2):'-';
  const avg5=Number.isFinite(Number(base.avg5))?Number(base.avg5).toFixed(2):'-';
  const groupName=groupKey==='seoul'?'인서울 관리군':groupKey==='regional'?'지거국 관리군':'일반 관리군';
  const mockRule=groupKey==='seoul'?'국·수·영 2개≤2, 전체≤3':groupKey==='regional'?'국·수·영 2개≤4, 전체≤5':'최근 모의 성적의 상승 흐름 확인';
  const row=(label,state,detail)=>`<div class="counsel-criterion-row"><span>${escapeHtml(label)}</span><i class="${studentCounselStatusClass(state)}">${studentCounselStatusLabel(state)}</i><b>${escapeHtml(detail)}</b></div>`;
  const guide=[];
  if(c.internal==='ok')guide.push(`현재 내신 9등급 환산 ${actual}로 ${groupName} 내신 기준을 충족하고 있습니다.`);
  else if(c.internal==='near')guide.push(`현재 내신 9등급 환산 ${actual}로 목표 기준에 근접해 있어 다음 학기 내신 유지·상승이 중요합니다.`);
  else guide.push(`현재 내신 9등급 환산 ${actual}이며 성적 향상 가능성과 강점 교과를 먼저 확인할 필요가 있습니다.`);
  if(c.mock!=='ok')guide.push(groupKey==='seoul'?'모의고사는 국·수·영 중 2개 영역 2등급 이내와 나머지 3등급 이내를 목표로 보완합니다.':groupKey==='regional'?'모의고사는 국·수·영 중 2개 영역 4등급 이내와 전체 5등급 이내를 우선 확인합니다.':'최근 모의고사에서 상승 가능한 영역을 정해 단기 목표를 세웁니다.');
  if(c.subjects!=='ok')guide.push('진로희망과 연결되는 선택과목 이수계획을 점검합니다.');
  if(c.activity!=='ok')guide.push('희망계열과 연결되는 선택활동과 프로그램 참여를 보완합니다.');
  if(c.reports!=='ok')guide.push('참여 활동을 보고서·탐구 결과로 남겨 학생부 연결성을 강화합니다.');
  const manage=(candidate.reasons||candidate.tags||[]).slice(0,3);
  return `<section class="counsel-side-card counsel-student-detail"><header><div><small>선택 학생 상세</small><h3>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo||'')} ${escapeHtml(s.name||'')} · ${escapeHtml(classNumberOf(s))}반</h3></div><button class="text-button" data-counsel-student-clear>학생 변경</button></header><div class="counsel-student-metrics"><span><small>내신 5등급</small><b>${avg5}</b><em>9등급 환산 ${actual}</em></span><span><small>최근 모의</small><b>${escapeHtml(mock.exam||'-')}</b><em>${escapeHtml(mock.detail||'자료 확인')}</em></span><span><small>대입관리</small><b>${escapeHtml(groupName)}</b><em>${c.internalLimit?`핵심선 ${c.internalLimit.toFixed(2)}`:'성장관리'}</em></span><span><small>관리포인트</small><b>${escapeHtml(manage[0]||'안정 추적')}</b><em>${escapeHtml(manage[1]||'학생부 연계 확인')}</em></span></div><div class="counsel-criteria-card"><div class="card-head"><strong>핵심기준 충족 현황</strong><small>${escapeHtml(groupName)} 기준</small></div>${row('내신 핵심기준',c.internal,c.internalLimit?`${actual} / ${c.internalLimit.toFixed(2)} 이내`:`현재 ${actual}`)}${row('모의고사 핵심기준',c.mock,mockRule)}${row('선택과목 연계',c.subjects,prep.subjects?`선택과목 ${prep.subjects}건 확인`:'선택과목 연계 확인 필요')}${row('선택활동·프로그램',c.activity,`선택활동 ${prep.selected||0} · 방과후 ${prep.after||0} · 야간심화 ${prep.night||0}`)}${row('보고서·탐구',c.reports,`보고서 ${prep.reports||0}건`)}</div></section><section class="counsel-side-card counsel-guide-v07718"><div class="card-head"><strong>상담 가이드</strong><small>읽어주며 상담할 수 있는 핵심 문장</small></div><div class="counsel-guide-tabs"><span class="active">현재 진단</span><span>부족 분석</span><span>준비 방향</span></div><div class="counsel-guide-lines">${guide.map((g,i)=>`<p><i>${i+1}</i>${escapeHtml(g)}</p>`).join('')}</div><div class="counsel-next-actions"><b>다음 상담 시 확인할 사항</b>${guide.slice(-3).map(g=>`<span>• ${escapeHtml(g)}</span>`).join('')}</div><div class="counsel-detail-actions"><button class="btn primary" data-counsel-write="${escapeHtml(s.id||'')}">상담기록 작성</button><button class="btn secondary" data-counsel-open-dashboard="${escapeHtml(s.id||'')}">학생 전체보기</button></div></section>`;
}
function studentCounselCenterView(studentPageTabs){
  dashboardCounselCandidates();
  const cached=__uepDashboardCounselCache||{};
  const all=(cached.all||[]).filter(x=>x?.student && String(x.student.status||'재학')==='재학');
  // 0.81.43: "1-5"에서 학년 숫자까지 합치지 않고 마지막 반 번호(5)만 사용합니다.
  const isHead=currentUserIsGradeHead(), mine=String(currentTeacherHomeroomClass()||'').split('-').pop().replace(/\D/g,'');
  if(!isHead && mine) studentCounselClassFilter=mine;
  const selectedClass=isHead?String(studentCounselClassFilter||''):mine;
  const visible=all.filter(x=>!selectedClass||String(classNumberOf(x.student))===selectedClass);
  const groupOf=x=>uepBaseManagementGroup(x?.base?.actual9,x?.base?.avg5)==='inseoul'?'seoul':uepBaseManagementGroup(x?.base?.actual9,x?.base?.avg5)==='regional'?'regional':'general';
  const defs={seoul:{label:uepManagementRule('inseoul').groupLabel,rule:`9등급 환산 ≤ ${uepManagementRule('inseoul').internal.toFixed(2)}`,desc:'내신·모의 핵심기준 충족 여부와 학생부 연계를 함께 관리',cls:'seoul'},regional:{label:uepManagementRule('regional').groupLabel,rule:`9등급 환산 ≤ ${uepManagementRule('regional').internal.toFixed(2)}`,desc:'내신·모의 상승과 선택과목·학생부 활동 연계를 강화',cls:'regional'},general:{label:uepManagementRule('general').groupLabel,rule:`9등급 환산 > ${uepManagementRule('regional').internal.toFixed(2)}`,desc:'성적 향상 가능성·진로탐색·학생부 기반 형성을 우선',cls:'general'}};
  const groups={seoul:[],regional:[],general:[]}; visible.forEach(x=>groups[groupOf(x)].push(x));
  Object.values(groups).forEach(rows=>rows.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||(a.base.actual9??99)-(b.base.actual9??99)||String(a.student.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.student.studentNo),'ko')));
  const status=x=>{const c=studentCounselCriteria(x,groupOf(x));const vals=[c.internal,c.mock,c.subjects,c.activity,c.reports];return vals.includes('bad')?'부족':vals.includes('near')?'근접':'충족'};
  let groupKey=String(window.__uepCounselGroupFilter||'seoul'); if(!defs[groupKey])groupKey='seoul';
  const summary=Object.entries(defs).map(([key,d])=>{const rows=groups[key], ok=rows.filter(x=>status(x)==='충족').length, near=rows.filter(x=>status(x)==='근접').length, bad=rows.length-ok-near;return `<button class="counsel-group-summary ${d.cls} ${groupKey===key?'active':''}" data-counsel-group="${key}"><header><b>${d.label}</b><small>${d.rule}</small></header><strong>${rows.length}명</strong><div><span>충족 <b>${ok}</b></span><span>근접 <b>${near}</b></span><span>부족 <b>${bad}</b></span></div><p>${d.desc}</p><em>${groupKey===key?'선택됨':'이 그룹 보기'} ›</em></button>`}).join('');
  const classOptions=isHead?`<option value="">전체</option>${Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>`<option value="${i+1}" ${selectedClass===String(i+1)?'selected':''}>${i+1}반</option>`).join('')}`:`<option value="${mine}">${mine}반</option>`;
  const d=defs[groupKey], rows=groups[groupKey]||[];
  if(studentCounselSelectedStudentId && !visible.some(x=>String(x.student.id)===String(studentCounselSelectedStudentId))) studentCounselSelectedStudentId='';
  const selectedCandidate=visible.find(x=>String(x.student.id)===String(studentCounselSelectedStudentId))||null;
  const list=rows.length?rows.map((x,i)=>{const st=status(x), c=studentCounselCriteria(x,groupKey), active=String(x.student.id)===String(studentCounselSelectedStudentId);return `<button class="counsel-manage-row ${active?'active':''}" data-counsel-student="${escapeHtml(x.student.id||'')}"><em>${i+1}</em><span class="student"><b>${escapeHtml(x.student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(x.student.name)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(classNumberOf(x.student))}반 · 내신 ${x.base.actual9==null?'-':Number(x.base.actual9).toFixed(2)} (9등급)</small></span><span class="criterion"><i class="${studentCounselStatusClass(c.internal)}">${studentCounselStatusLabel(c.internal)}</i><small>내신</small></span><span class="criterion"><i class="${studentCounselStatusClass(c.mock)}">${studentCounselStatusLabel(c.mock)}</i><small>모의</small></span><span class="criterion"><i class="${studentCounselStatusClass(c.subjects)}">${studentCounselStatusLabel(c.subjects)}</i><small>선택과목</small></span><span class="criterion"><i class="${studentCounselStatusClass(c.activity)}">${studentCounselStatusLabel(c.activity)}</i><small>선택활동</small></span><span class="criterion"><i class="${studentCounselStatusClass(c.reports)}">${studentCounselStatusLabel(c.reports)}</i><small>보고서·탐구</small></span><strong>${escapeHtml((x.reasons||x.tags||[])[0]||'학생부·성적 연계 확인')}</strong><u>${active?'선택됨':'보기'}</u></button>`}).join(''):'<div class="query-empty compact"><b>해당 관리군 학생이 없습니다.</b><span>다른 관리군 또는 반을 선택해 주세요.</span></div>';
  const history=(Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.counselRecords)?readonlyCache.counselRecords:[]).filter(r=>(!selectedClass||String(r.className||'').replace(/\D/g,'')===selectedClass)&&(!selectedCandidate||String(r.studentId||'')===String(selectedCandidate.student.id||''))).sort((a,b)=>String(b.createdAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt||''))).slice(0,8);
  const historyMarkup=`<section class="student-counsel-history-panel counsel-history-v07718"><div class="card-head"><strong>상담이력</strong><small>${selectedCandidate?'선택 학생 기록':'반 상담 기록'}</small></div>${history.length?history.map(row=>`<button data-student-counsel-history="${escapeHtml(row.studentId||'')}"><b>${escapeHtml(row.studentNo||'')} ${escapeHtml(row.name||'')}</b><span>${escapeHtml((row.createdAt||'').replace('T',' ').slice(0,16))}</span><small>${escapeHtml(row.memo||'')}</small></button>`).join(''):'<div class="query-empty compact"><b>상담이력이 없습니다.</b><span>상담기록을 작성하면 이곳에 누적됩니다.</span></div>'}</section>`;
  return `<div class="module-page student-master-page student-counsel-center counsel-v07718">${studentPageTabs}<div class="counsel-v07718-head counsel-head-with-groups"><div class="counsel-head-copy"><small>학생정보 › 상담</small><h2>1학년 2학기 학생상담 관리</h2><p>관리군을 먼저 선택하고, 학생의 충족·근접·부족을 확인한 뒤 우측 상담 가이드를 그대로 활용합니다.</p></div><div class="counsel-group-summaries counsel-group-summaries-inline">${summary}</div></div><div class="counsel-filter-top"><select data-counsel-class-select>${classOptions}</select><button class="${!selectedClass?'active':''}" data-counsel-class-all ${!isHead?'disabled':''}>전체 학생</button><input data-counsel-search placeholder="학생명 또는 학번 검색"><small>※ 관리군 기준: 9등급 환산 내신</small></div><div class="student-counsel-center-grid"><section class="student-counsel-class-section counsel-list-v07718"><header><div><b>${d.label} 학생 목록</b><small>${d.rule} · ${rows.length}명 · 관리 필요도 순</small></div><span>학생을 누르면 우측 상세·상담가이드가 열립니다.</span></header><div class="counsel-manage-list">${list}</div></section><aside class="counsel-right-column">${studentCounselDetailMarkup(selectedCandidate,selectedCandidate?groupOf(selectedCandidate):groupKey)}${historyMarkup}</aside></div></div>`;
}

  const row=(label,state,detail)=>`<div class="counsel-criterion-row"><span>${escapeHtml(label)}</span><i class="${studentCounselStatusClass(state)}">${studentCounselStatusLabel(state)}</i><b>${escapeHtml(detail)}</b></div>`;
  const guide=[];
  if(c.internal==='ok')guide.push(`현재 내신 9등급 환산 ${actual}로 ${groupName} 내신 기준을 충족하고 있습니다.`);
  else if(c.internal==='near')guide.push(`현재 내신 9등급 환산 ${actual}로 목표 기준에 근접해 있어 다음 학기 내신 유지·상승이 중요합니다.`);
  else guide.push(`현재 내신 9등급 환산 ${actual}이며 성적 향상 가능성과 강점 교과를 먼저 확인할 필요가 있습니다.`);
  if(c.mock!=='ok')guide.push(groupKey==='seoul'?'모의고사는 국·수·영 중 2개 영역 2등급 이내와 나머지 3등급 이내를 목표로 보완합니다.':groupKey==='regional'?'모의고사는 국·수·영 중 2개 영역 4등급 이내와 전체 5등급 이내를 우선 확인합니다.':'최근 모의고사에서 상승 가능한 영역을 정해 단기 목표를 세웁니다.');
  if(c.subjects!=='ok')guide.push('진로희망과 연결되는 선택과목 이수계획을 점검합니다.');
  if(c.activity!=='ok')guide.push('희망계열과 연결되는 선택활동과 프로그램 참여를 보완합니다.');
  if(c.reports!=='ok')guide.push('참여 활동을 보고서·탐구 결과로 남겨 학생부 연결성을 강화합니다.');
  const manage=(candidate.reasons||candidate.tags||[]).slice(0,3);
  return `<section class="counsel-side-card counsel-student-detail"><header><div><small>선택 학생 상세</small><h3>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo||'')} ${escapeHtml(s.name||'')} · ${escapeHtml(classNumberOf(s))}반</h3></div><button class="text-button" data-counsel-student-clear>학생 변경</button></header><div class="counsel-student-metrics"><span><small>내신 5등급</small><b>${avg5}</b><em>9등급 환산 ${actual}</em></span><span><small>최근 모의</small><b>${escapeHtml(mock.exam||'-')}</b><em>${escapeHtml(mock.detail||'자료 확인')}</em></span><span><small>대입관리</small><b>${escapeHtml(groupName)}</b><em>${c.internalLimit?`핵심선 ${c.internalLimit.toFixed(2)}`:'성장관리'}</em></span><span><small>관리포인트</small><b>${escapeHtml(manage[0]||'안정 추적')}</b><em>${escapeHtml(manage[1]||'학생부 연계 확인')}</em></span></div><div class="counsel-criteria-card"><div class="card-head"><strong>핵심기준 충족 현황</strong><small>${escapeHtml(groupName)} 기준</small></div>${row('내신 핵심기준',c.internal,c.internalLimit?`${actual} / ${c.internalLimit.toFixed(2)} 이내`:`현재 ${actual}`)}${row('모의고사 핵심기준',c.mock,mockRule)}${row('선택과목 연계',c.subjects,prep.subjects?`선택과목 ${prep.subjects}건 확인`:'선택과목 연계 확인 필요')}${row('선택활동·프로그램',c.activity,`선택활동 ${prep.selected||0} · 방과후 ${prep.after||0} · 야간심화 ${prep.night||0}`)}${row('보고서·탐구',c.reports,`보고서 ${prep.reports||0}건`)}</div></section><section class="counsel-side-card counsel-guide-v07718"><div class="card-head"><strong>상담 가이드</strong><small>읽어주며 상담할 수 있는 핵심 문장</small></div><div class="counsel-guide-tabs"><span class="active">현재 진단</span><span>부족 분석</span><span>준비 방향</span></div><div class="counsel-guide-lines">${guide.map((g,i)=>`<p><i>${i+1}</i>${escapeHtml(g)}</p>`).join('')}</div><div class="counsel-next-actions"><b>다음 상담 시 확인할 사항</b>${guide.slice(-3).map(g=>`<span>• ${escapeHtml(g)}</span>`).join('')}</div><div class="counsel-detail-actions"><button class="btn primary" data-counsel-write="${escapeHtml(s.id||'')}">상담기록 작성</button><button class="btn secondary" data-counsel-open-dashboard="${escapeHtml(s.id||'')}">학생 전체보기</button></div></section>`;
}
function studentCounselCenterView(studentPageTabs){
  dashboardCounselCandidates();
  const cached=__uepDashboardCounselCache||{};
  const all=(cached.all||[]).filter(x=>x?.student && String(x.student.status||'재학')==='재학');
  // 0.81.43: "1-5"에서 학년 숫자까지 합치지 않고 마지막 반 번호(5)만 사용합니다.
  const isHead=currentUserIsGradeHead(), mine=String(currentTeacherHomeroomClass()||'').split('-').pop().replace(/\D/g,'');
  if(!isHead && mine) studentCounselClassFilter=mine;
  const selectedClass=isHead?String(studentCounselClassFilter||''):mine;
  const visible=all.filter(x=>!selectedClass||String(classNumberOf(x.student))===selectedClass);
  const groupOf=x=>uepBaseManagementGroup(x?.base?.actual9,x?.base?.avg5)==='inseoul'?'seoul':uepBaseManagementGroup(x?.base?.actual9,x?.base?.avg5)==='regional'?'regional':'general';
  const defs={seoul:{label:uepManagementRule('inseoul').groupLabel,rule:`9등급 환산 ≤ ${uepManagementRule('inseoul').internal.toFixed(2)}`,desc:'내신·모의 핵심기준 충족 여부와 학생부 연계를 함께 관리',cls:'seoul'},regional:{label:uepManagementRule('regional').groupLabel,rule:`9등급 환산 ≤ ${uepManagementRule('regional').internal.toFixed(2)}`,desc:'내신·모의 상승과 선택과목·학생부 활동 연계를 강화',cls:'regional'},general:{label:uepManagementRule('general').groupLabel,rule:`9등급 환산 > ${uepManagementRule('regional').internal.toFixed(2)}`,desc:'성적 향상 가능성·진로탐색·학생부 기반 형성을 우선',cls:'general'}};
  const groups={seoul:[],regional:[],general:[]}; visible.forEach(x=>groups[groupOf(x)].push(x));
  Object.values(groups).forEach(rows=>rows.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||(a.base.actual9??99)-(b.base.actual9??99)||String(a.student.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.student.studentNo),'ko')));
  const status=x=>{const c=studentCounselCriteria(x,groupOf(x));const vals=[c.internal,c.mock,c.subjects,c.activity,c.reports];return vals.includes('bad')?'부족':vals.includes('near')?'근접':'충족'};
  let groupKey=String(window.__uepCounselGroupFilter||'seoul'); if(!defs[groupKey])groupKey='seoul';
  const summary=Object.entries(defs).map(([key,d])=>{const rows=groups[key], ok=rows.filter(x=>status(x)==='충족').length, near=rows.filter(x=>status(x)==='근접').length, bad=rows.length-ok-near;return `<button class="counsel-group-summary ${d.cls} ${groupKey===key?'active':''}" data-counsel-group="${key}"><header><b>${d.label}</b><small>${d.rule}</small></header><strong>${rows.length}명</strong><div><span>충족 <b>${ok}</b></span><span>근접 <b>${near}</b></span><span>부족 <b>${bad}</b></span></div><p>${d.desc}</p><em>${groupKey===key?'선택됨':'이 그룹 보기'} ›</em></button>`}).join('');
  const classOptions=isHead?`<option value="">전체</option>${Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>`<option value="${i+1}" ${selectedClass===String(i+1)?'selected':''}>${i+1}반</option>`).join('')}`:`<option value="${mine}">${mine}반</option>`;
  const d=defs[groupKey], rows=groups[groupKey]||[];
  if(studentCounselSelectedStudentId && !visible.some(x=>String(x.student.id)===String(studentCounselSelectedStudentId))) studentCounselSelectedStudentId='';
  const selectedCandidate=visible.find(x=>String(x.student.id)===String(studentCounselSelectedStudentId))||null;
  const list=rows.length?rows.map((x,i)=>{const st=status(x), c=studentCounselCriteria(x,groupKey), active=String(x.student.id)===String(studentCounselSelectedStudentId);return `<button class="counsel-manage-row ${active?'active':''}" data-counsel-student="${escapeHtml(x.student.id||'')}"><em>${i+1}</em><span class="student"><b>${escapeHtml(x.student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(x.student.name)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(classNumberOf(x.student))}반 · 내신 ${x.base.actual9==null?'-':Number(x.base.actual9).toFixed(2)} (9등급)</small></span><span class="criterion"><i class="${studentCounselStatusClass(c.internal)}">${studentCounselStatusLabel(c.internal)}</i><small>내신</small></span><span class="criterion"><i class="${studentCounselStatusClass(c.mock)}">${studentCounselStatusLabel(c.mock)}</i><small>모의</small></span><span class="criterion"><i class="${studentCounselStatusClass(c.subjects)}">${studentCounselStatusLabel(c.subjects)}</i><small>선택과목</small></span><span class="criterion"><i class="${studentCounselStatusClass(c.activity)}">${studentCounselStatusLabel(c.activity)}</i><small>선택활동</small></span><span class="criterion"><i class="${studentCounselStatusClass(c.reports)}">${studentCounselStatusLabel(c.reports)}</i><small>보고서·탐구</small></span><strong>${escapeHtml((x.reasons||x.tags||[])[0]||'학생부·성적 연계 확인')}</strong><u>${active?'선택됨':'보기'}</u></button>`}).join(''):'<div class="query-empty compact"><b>해당 관리군 학생이 없습니다.</b><span>다른 관리군 또는 반을 선택해 주세요.</span></div>';
  const history=(Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.counselRecords)?readonlyCache.counselRecords:[]).filter(r=>(!selectedClass||String(r.className||'').replace(/\D/g,'')===selectedClass)&&(!selectedCandidate||String(r.studentId||'')===String(selectedCandidate.student.id||''))).sort((a,b)=>String(b.createdAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt||''))).slice(0,8);
  const historyMarkup=`<section class="student-counsel-history-panel counsel-history-v07718"><div class="card-head"><strong>상담이력</strong><small>${selectedCandidate?'선택 학생 기록':'반 상담 기록'}</small></div>${history.length?history.map(row=>`<button data-student-counsel-history="${escapeHtml(row.studentId||'')}"><b>${escapeHtml(row.studentNo||'')} ${escapeHtml(row.name||'')}</b><span>${escapeHtml((row.createdAt||'').replace('T',' ').slice(0,16))}</span><small>${escapeHtml(row.memo||'')}</small></button>`).join(''):'<div class="query-empty compact"><b>상담이력이 없습니다.</b><span>상담기록을 작성하면 이곳에 누적됩니다.</span></div>'}</section>`;
  return `<div class="module-page student-master-page student-counsel-center counsel-v07718">${studentPageTabs}<div class="counsel-v07718-head counsel-head-with-groups"><div class="counsel-head-copy"><small>학생정보 › 상담</small><h2>1학년 2학기 학생상담 관리</h2><p>관리군을 먼저 선택하고, 학생의 충족·근접·부족을 확인한 뒤 우측 상담 가이드를 그대로 활용합니다.</p></div><div class="counsel-group-summaries counsel-group-summaries-inline">${summary}</div></div><div class="counsel-filter-top"><select data-counsel-class-select>${classOptions}</select><button class="${!selectedClass?'active':''}" data-counsel-class-all ${!isHead?'disabled':''}>전체 학생</button><input data-counsel-search placeholder="학생명 또는 학번 검색"><small>※ 관리군 기준: 9등급 환산 내신</small></div><div class="student-counsel-center-grid"><section class="student-counsel-class-section counsel-list-v07718"><header><div><b>${d.label} 학생 목록</b><small>${d.rule} · ${rows.length}명 · 관리 필요도 순</small></div><span>학생을 누르면 우측 상세·상담가이드가 열립니다.</span></header><div class="counsel-manage-list">${list}</div></section><aside class="counsel-right-column">${studentCounselDetailMarkup(selectedCandidate,selectedCandidate?groupOf(selectedCandidate):groupKey)}${historyMarkup}</aside></div></div>`;
}

const STUDENT_CARD_HEAT_STORE='uep-student-card-heat-v2';
const STUDENT_CARD_HEAT_PERIOD_STORE='uep-student-card-heat-period-v1';
function studentCardHeatId(student){return String(student?.id||student?.studentNo||'').trim();}
function studentCardHeatPeriodDays(){try{return localStorage.getItem(STUDENT_CARD_HEAT_PERIOD_STORE)==='7'?7:30;}catch{return 30;}}
function setStudentCardHeatPeriodDays(days){const value=Number(days)===7?7:30;try{localStorage.setItem(STUDENT_CARD_HEAT_PERIOD_STORE,String(value));}catch{}return value;}
function readStudentCardHeatStore(){try{const raw=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STUDENT_CARD_HEAT_STORE)||'{}');return raw&&typeof raw==='object'&&!Array.isArray(raw)?raw:{};}catch{return {};}}
function writeStudentCardHeatStore(store){try{localStorage.setItem(STUDENT_CARD_HEAT_STORE,JSON.stringify(store||{}));}catch{}}
function studentCardHeat(student){const id=studentCardHeatId(student);if(!id)return 0;const store=readStudentCardHeatStore(),cutoff=Date.now()-studentCardHeatPeriodDays()*86400000;const recent=(Array.isArray(store[id])?store[id]:[]).map(Number).filter(t=>Number.isFinite(t)&&t>=cutoff);return recent.length;}
-----
    const key=`${row.studentId||row.studentNo}|${term.label}`;if(seen.has(key))return false;seen.add(key);return true;
  }).sort(dormStudentNoSort);
}
function dormScoreRows(studentId){return (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(row=>String(row.studentId||'')===String(studentId||''));}
function dormAverage(rows){const values=rows.map(row=>Number(row.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);return values.length?values.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/values.length:null;}
function dormInternalTrend(student,term){
  const rows=dormScoreRows(student.id).filter(row=>row.scoreType==='내신');
  const terms=[['1-1','2026','1'],['1-2','2026','2'],['2-1','2027','1'],['2-2','2027','2'],['3','2028','']];
  const limit=term.grade===3?4:(term.grade-1)*2+(term.semester==='2'?1:0);
  return terms.slice(0,limit+1).map(([label,year,semester])=>({label,value:dormAverage(rows.filter(row=>(!row.schoolYear||String(row.schoolYear)===year)&&(!semester||!row.semester||String(row.semester).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'')===semester)))})).filter(x=>x.value!=null);
}
function dormMockTrend(student,term){
  const order=['3월모의고사','6월모의고사','9월모의고사','10월모의고사','11월모의고사','수능'];
  const rows=dormScoreRows(student.id).filter(row=>row.scoreType==='모의고사');
  const years=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>String(row.schoolYear||'')).filter(Boolean))].sort();
  const allowedYears=years.filter(year=>Number(year)<=Number(term.year));
  const result=[];
  allowedYears.forEach(year=>order.forEach(exam=>{const avg=dormAverage(rows.filter(row=>String(row.schoolYear||'')===year&&row.exam===exam));if(avg!=null)result.push({label:`${year.slice(-2)}.${exam.replace('모의고사','').replace('월','')}`,value:avg});}));
  return result.slice(-5);
}
function dormSparkline(points){
  if(!points.length)return '<span class="dorm-spark-empty">자료 대기</span>';
  const w=118,h=30,p=4,vals=points.map(x=>x.value),min=Math.min(...vals),max=Math.max(...vals),range=Math.max(.4,max-min);
  if(points.length===1){
    return `<svg class="dorm-spark dorm-spark-one" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="${w/2}" cy="${h/2}" r="3.2"/></svg>`;
  }
  const coords=points.map((x,i)=>`${p+i*(w-p*2)/(points.length-1)},${p+(x.value-min)*(h-p*2)/range}`).join(' ');
  const dots=coords.split(' ').map(pair=>{const [x,y]=pair.split(',');return `<circle cx="${x}" cy="${y}" r="2.1"/>`;}).join('');
  return `<svg class="dorm-spark" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="${coords}" fill="none" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/>${dots}</svg>`;
}
function dormManagementBadge(student){
  const candidate=counselPriorityCandidateForStudent(student);if(!candidate)return {label:'',reason:'안정 추적',candidate:null};
  const score=Number(candidate.score||0);const rank=score>=110?'관리 우선 ①':score>=80?'관리 우선 ②':'관리 확인';
  return {label:rank,reason:(candidate.tags||candidate.reasons||[])[0]||'상담 확인',candidate};
}
function dormAdmissionLine(student){
  const rows=studentAdmissionStageRows(student)||[];
  const inseoul=rows.find(x=>x.group==='inseoul'),regional=rows.find(x=>x.group==='regional');
  const label=x=>!x?'일반':x.stage==='core'?'핵심':x.stage==='growth'?'성장':x.stage==='interest'?'관심':'일반';
  return `인서울 ${label(inseoul)} · 지거국 ${label(regional)}`;
}

  }).sort(dormStudentNoSort);
}

function dormSparkline(points){
  if(!points.length)return '<span class="dorm-spark-empty">자료 대기</span>';
  const w=118,h=30,p=4,vals=points.map(x=>x.value),min=Math.min(...vals),max=Math.max(...vals),range=Math.max(.4,max-min);
  if(points.length===1){

  }
  const coords=points.map((x,i)=>`${p+i*(w-p*2)/(points.length-1)},${p+(x.value-min)*(h-p*2)/range}`).join(' ');
  const dots=coords.split(' ').map(pair=>{const [x,y]=pair.split(',');return `<circle cx="${x}" cy="${y}" r="2.1"/>`;}).join('');
  return `<svg class="dorm-spark" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="${coords}" fill="none" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/>${dots}</svg>`;
}

function dormAxisChart(points,maxGrade=9){
  if(!points.length)return '<div class="dorm-axis-empty">연결된 성적 자료가 없습니다.</div>';
  const w=620,h=132,left=48,right=18,top=14,bottom=30,plotW=w-left-right,plotH=h-top-bottom;

function dormInternalTrend(student,term){
  const rows=dormScoreRows(student.id).filter(row=>row.scoreType==='내신');
  const terms=[['1-1','2026','1'],['1-2','2026','2'],['2-1','2027','1'],['2-2','2027','2'],['3','2028','']];
  const limit=term.grade===3?4:(term.grade-1)*2+(term.semester==='2'?1:0);
  return terms.slice(0,limit+1).map(([label,year,semester])=>({label,value:dormAverage(rows.filter(row=>(!row.schoolYear||String(row.schoolYear)===year)&&(!semester||!row.semester||String(row.semester).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'')===semester)))})).filter(x=>x.value!=null);
}
function dormMockTrend(student,term){
  const order=['3월모의고사','6월모의고사','9월모의고사','10월모의고사','11월모의고사','수능'];
  const rows=dormScoreRows(student.id).filter(row=>row.scoreType==='모의고사');
  const years=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>String(row.schoolYear||'')).filter(Boolean))].sort();
  const allowedYears=years.filter(year=>Number(year)<=Number(term.year));
  const result=[];
  allowedYears.forEach(year=>order.forEach(exam=>{const avg=dormAverage(rows.filter(row=>String(row.schoolYear||'')===year&&row.exam===exam));if(avg!=null)result.push({label:`${year.slice(-2)}.${exam.replace('모의고사','').replace('월','')}`,value:avg});}));
  return result.slice(-5);
}
function dormSparkline(points){
  if(!points.length)return '<span class="dorm-spark-empty">자료 대기</span>';
  const w=118,h=30,p=4,vals=points.map(x=>x.value),min=Math.min(...vals),max=Math.max(...vals),range=Math.max(.4,max-min);
  if(points.length===1){
    return `<svg class="dorm-spark dorm-spark-one" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="${w/2}" cy="${h/2}" r="3.2"/></svg>`;
  }
  const coords=points.map((x,i)=>`${p+i*(w-p*2)/(points.length-1)},${p+(x.value-min)*(h-p*2)/range}`).join(' ');
  const dots=coords.split(' ').map(pair=>{const [x,y]=pair.split(',');return `<circle cx="${x}" cy="${y}" r="2.1"/>`;}).join('');
  return `<svg class="dorm-spark" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="${coords}" fill="none" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/>${dots}</svg>`;
}
function dormManagementBadge(student){
  const candidate=counselPriorityCandidateForStudent(student);if(!candidate)return {label:'',reason:'안정 추적',candidate:null};
  const score=Number(candidate.score||0);const rank=score>=110?'관리 우선 ①':score>=80?'관리 우선 ②':'관리 확인';
  return {label:rank,reason:(candidate.tags||candidate.reasons||[])[0]||'상담 확인',candidate};
}
function dormAdmissionLine(student){
  const rows=studentAdmissionStageRows(student)||[];
  const inseoul=rows.find(x=>x.group==='inseoul'),regional=rows.find(x=>x.group==='regional');
  const label=x=>!x?'일반':x.stage==='core'?'핵심':x.stage==='growth'?'성장':x.stage==='interest'?'관심':'일반';
  return `인서울 ${label(inseoul)} · 지거국 ${label(regional)}`;
}

function dormAxisChart(points,maxGrade=9){
  if(!points.length)return '<div class="dorm-axis-empty">연결된 성적 자료가 없습니다.</div>';
  const w=620,h=132,left=48,right=18,top=14,bottom=30,plotW=w-left-right,plotH=h-top-bottom;
  const max=Math.max(2,Number(maxGrade)||9), minGrade=1;
  const x=(i)=>points.length===1?left+plotW/2:left+i*plotW/(points.length-1);
  const y=(v)=>top+(Math.max(minGrade,Math.min(max,Number(v)))-minGrade)*plotH/(max-minGrade);
  const actualValues=[...new Set(points.map(p=>Number(p.value)).filter(Number.isFinite).map(v=>Number(v.toFixed(2))))].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  const grid=actualValues.map(value=>{const yy=y(value);return `<line x1="${left}" y1="${yy}" x2="${w-right}" y2="${yy}" class="grid actual"/><text x="${left-8}" y="${yy+3}" text-anchor="end">${value.toFixed(value%1?2:0)}</text>`;}).join('');
  const path=points.length>1?`<polyline points="${points.map((p,i)=>`${x(i)},${y(p.value)}`).join(' ')}" class="line"/>`:'';
  const dots=points.map((p,i)=>`<circle cx="${x(i)}" cy="${y(p.value)}" r="4" class="dot"/><text x="${x(i)}" y="${Math.max(10,y(p.value)-8)}" text-anchor="middle" class="value-label">${Number(p.value).toFixed(2)}</text><text x="${x(i)}" y="${h-10}" text-anchor="middle" class="xlabel">${escapeHtml(p.label)}</text>`).join('');
  return `<svg class="dorm-axis-chart" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" role="img" aria-label="성적 추세 그래프"><line x1="${left}" y1="${top}" x2="${left}" y2="${h-bottom}" class="axis"/><line x1="${left}" y1="${h-bottom}" x2="${w-right}" y2="${h-bottom}" class="axis"/>${grid}${path}${dots}</svg>`;
-----
  const x=(i)=>points.length===1?left+plotW/2:left+i*plotW/(points.length-1);
  const y=(v)=>top+(Math.max(minGrade,Math.min(max,Number(v)))-minGrade)*plotH/(max-minGrade);
  const actualValues=[...new Set(points.map(p=>Number(p.value)).filter(Number.isFinite).map(v=>Number(v.toFixed(2))))].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  const grid=actualValues.map(value=>{const yy=y(value);return `<line x1="${left}" y1="${yy}" x2="${w-right}" y2="${yy}" class="grid actual"/><text x="${left-8}" y="${yy+3}" text-anchor="end">${value.toFixed(value%1?2:0)}</text>`;}).join('');
  const path=points.length>1?`<polyline points="${points.map((p,i)=>`${x(i)},${y(p.value)}`).join(' ')}" class="line"/>`:'';
  const dots=points.map((p,i)=>`<circle cx="${x(i)}" cy="${y(p.value)}" r="4" class="dot"/><text x="${x(i)}" y="${Math.max(10,y(p.value)-8)}" text-anchor="middle" class="value-label">${Number(p.value).toFixed(2)}</text><text x="${x(i)}" y="${h-10}" text-anchor="middle" class="xlabel">${escapeHtml(p.label)}</text>`).join('');
  return `<svg class="dorm-axis-chart" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" role="img" aria-label="성적 추세 그래프"><line x1="${left}" y1="${top}" x2="${left}" y2="${h-bottom}" class="axis"/><line x1="${left}" y1="${h-bottom}" x2="${w-right}" y2="${h-bottom}" class="axis"/>${grid}${path}${dots}</svg>`;
}

function dormInternalTermRows(student,year,semester){
  const rows=dormScoreRows(student.id).filter(row=>row.scoreType==='내신'&&(!year||!row.schoolYear||String(row.schoolYear)===String(year))&&(!semester||!row.semester||String(row.semester).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'')===String(semester)));
  const exams=[...new Set(rows.map(r=>String(r.exam||'')).filter(Boolean))];
  const preferred=['학기말고사','2차고사','1차고사'].find(exam=>exams.includes(exam))||exams[exams.length-1]||'';
  return preferred?rows.filter(r=>String(r.exam||'')===preferred):rows;
}
function dormScoreValueMarkup(row){
  const achievement=String(row.achievement||'').trim();
  const level=Number(row.level);
  if(Number.isFinite(level))return `<b>${level}등급</b>${achievement?`<small>성취도 ${escapeHtml(achievement)}</small>`:''}`;
  if(achievement)return `<b>성취도 ${escapeHtml(achievement)}</b><small>등급 미산출 과목</small>`;
  return '<b>-</b>';
}
function dormInternalDetailMarkup(student){
  const defs=[['1학년 1학기','2026','1'],['1학년 2학기','2026','2'],['2학년 1학기','2027','1'],['2학년 2학기','2027','2'],['3학년','2028','']];
  const sections=defs.map(([label,year,sem])=>{
    const rows=dormInternalTermRows(student,year,sem);
    if(!rows.length)return '';
    return `<article class="dorm-detail-block"><header><b>${label} 내신 (5등급 기준)</b><span>${escapeHtml(rows[0]?.exam||'내신')}</span></header><div class="dorm-subject-grid">${rows.map(row=>`<span><small>${escapeHtml(row.subject||'과목')}</small>${dormScoreValueMarkup(row)}</span>`).join('')}</div></article>`;
  }).filter(Boolean).join('');
  return sections||'<div class="empty-inline">연결된 내신 과목별 자료가 없습니다.</div>';
}

    const rows=dormInternalTermRows(student,year,sem);
    if(!rows.length)return '';
    return `<article class="dorm-detail-block"><header><b>${label} 내신 (5등급 기준)</b><span>${escapeHtml(rows[0]?.exam||'내신')}</span></header><div class="dorm-subject-grid">${rows.map(row=>`<span><small>${escapeHtml(row.subject||'과목')}</small>${dormScoreValueMarkup(row)}</span>`).join('')}</div></article>`;
  }).filter(Boolean).join('');
  return sections||'<div class="empty-inline">연결된 내신 과목별 자료가 없습니다.</div>';
}

function dormSelectedSubjectsMarkup(student,bundle){
  const career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle);
  const rows=(bundle.selected||[]).filter(r=>String(r.final||'Y')!=='N');
  const grouped=new Map();

    const term=String(row.term||row.semester||'선택과목').trim()||'선택과목';

  });

}
function dormProgramTermKey(program){
  const explicit=String(program.semester||program.term||'');
  if(/2학년\s*1|2-1/.test(explicit))return 'g2s1';
  if(/2학년\s*2|2-2/.test(explicit))return 'g2s2';
  if(/3학년/.test(explicit))return 'g3';
  const date=String(program.date||program.startDate||'');
  const year=date.slice(0,4),month=Number(date.slice(5,7));
  if(year==='2026')return month&&month<=7?'g1s1':'g1s2';
  if(year==='2027')return month&&month<=7?'g2s1':'g2s2';
  if(year==='2028')return 'g3';
  return '';
}

function dormProgramTermKey(program){

  if(/3학년/.test(explicit))return 'g3';
  const date=String(program.date||program.startDate||'');
  const year=date.slice(0,4),month=Number(date.slice(5,7));
  if(year==='2026')return month&&month<=7?'g1s1':'g1s2';
  if(year==='2027')return month&&month<=7?'g2s1':'g2s2';
  if(year==='2028')return 'g3';
  return '';
}

function dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode){
  const view=studentActivityView(student);
  const order=['g1s1','g1s2','g2s1','g2s2','g3'];
  const cutoff=Math.max(0,order.indexOf(termMode));
  const allowed=new Set(order.slice(0,cutoff+1));
-----
  const standard=row?.standard||{internal:uepManagementRule('inseoul').internal,mockText:uepManagementRule('inseoul').mockText};
  const actual=row?.base?.actual9;
  const internalGap=actual==null?null:Math.max(0,(actual-Number(standard.internal))/Number(standard.internal)*100);
  const levels=(row?.mock?.mainLevels||[]).filter(Number.isFinite);
  const within3=levels.filter(v=>v<=3).length,within2=levels.filter(v=>v<=2).length;
  const mockScore=levels.length?Math.min(100,((Math.min(within3,3)+Math.min(within2,2))/5)*100):null;
  const mockGap=mockScore==null?null:100-mockScore;
  const overall=[internalGap,mockGap].filter(v=>v!=null).length?Math.max(...[internalGap,mockGap].filter(v=>v!=null)):null;
  const gaps=[];
  if(actual==null)gaps.push('내신 9등급 환산자료 확인 필요');
  else if(actual>standard.internal)gaps.push(`내신 ${actual.toFixed(2)} → 핵심기준 ${Number(standard.internal).toFixed(2)} 이내 보완`);
  else gaps.push(`내신 핵심기준 충족 (${actual.toFixed(2)})`);
  if(levels.length<3)gaps.push('국·수·영 모의고사 자료 보완');
  else{
    levels.forEach((v,i)=>{if(v>3)gaps.push(`${['국어','수학','영어'][i]||'주요영역'} ${v}등급 → 3등급 이내`);});
    if(within2<2)gaps.push(`국·수·영 2등급 이내 ${within2}개 → 2개 필요`);
    if(within3===3&&within2>=2)gaps.push('모의고사 인서울 핵심기준 충족');
  }
  return {row,standard,actual,internalGap,mockGap,overall,gaps};
}
function dormComprehensiveCounselMarkup(student,termMode,bundle){
  const gap=dormInseoulGap(student),career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle),stats=dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode),cohort=dormCohortAverage(termMode);
  const selected=(bundle.selected||[]).filter(r=>String(r.final||'Y')!=='N');
  const points=[...gap.gaps];
  if(!selected.length)points.push('희망계열에 맞는 선택과목 이수계획 확인 필요');
  if(stats.programs.length<cohort.programs)points.push(`계열 연계 프로그램: 본인 ${stats.programs.length}개 / 학사생 평균 ${cohort.programs.toFixed(1)}개`);
  if(stats.reportRate+1<cohort.reportRate)points.push(`보고서 제출률: 본인 ${Math.round(stats.reportRate)}% / 학사생 평균 ${Math.round(cohort.reportRate)}%`);
  points.push(`진로희망 ${career.hopeTrack} · ${career.hopeMajor}와 선택과목·탐구활동의 연속성 확인`);
  return `<section class="dorm-counsel-priority"><header><div><small>SEOUL · CORE GAP</small><h4>인서울 핵심관리 괴리 최우선</h4></div><strong>${gap.overall==null?'자료 확인':gap.overall<=0?'핵심기준 충족':`최대 괴리 ${gap.overall.toFixed(0)}%`}</strong></header><div class="dorm-gap-metrics"><span><small>내신 핵심기준</small><b>9등급 ${Number(gap.standard.internal).toFixed(2)} 이내</b></span><span><small>현재 내신</small><b>${gap.actual==null?'-':gap.actual.toFixed(2)}</b><em>${gap.internalGap==null?'자료 없음':gap.internalGap===0?'충족':`괴리 +${gap.internalGap.toFixed(0)}%`}</em></span><span><small>모의 핵심기준</small><b>국·수·영 2개≤2, 전체≤3</b></span><span><small>모의 충족 괴리</small><b>${gap.mockGap==null?'-':`${gap.mockGap.toFixed(0)}%`}</b></span></div><ol>${points.map(p=>`<li>${escapeHtml(p)}</li>`).join('')}</ol><p class="dorm-holistic-note"><b>학생부종합전형 관점</b> 성적만으로 순위를 매기지 않고 진로희망–선택과목–계열연계 프로그램–보고서 탐구의 연결성과 지속성을 함께 상담합니다.</p></section>`;
}

function dormSeoulUniversityRows(student){
  const seoul=/서울대|연세대|고려대|서강대|성균관대|한양대|중앙대|경희대|한국외대|서울시립대|건국대|동국대|홍익대|숙명여대|이화여대|광운대|국민대|숭실대|세종대|서울과기대/;
  const base=admissionBase(student.id), actual=base.actual9;
  const cuts=(readonlyCache?.admissionCuts||[]).filter(x=>seoul.test(String(x.university||''))&&Number.isFinite(Number(x.cut)));
  const grouped=new Map();
  cuts.forEach(row=>{const key=String(row.university||'').trim();if(!key)return;const diff=actual==null?null:actual-Number(row.cut);const old=grouped.get(key);if(!old||Math.abs(diff??99)<Math.abs(old.diff??99))grouped.set(key,{...row,diff});});
  return [...grouped.values()].sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(a.diff??99)-Math.abs(b.diff??99)).slice(0,9);
}
function dormUniversityFitMarkup(student,bundle){
  const rows=dormSeoulUniversityRows(student), career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle), gap=dormInseoulGap(student);
  const selectedCount=(bundle.selected||[]).filter(r=>String(r.final||'Y')!=='N').length;
  const holistic=selectedCount>=6?'적합':selectedCount>=3?'보완':'확인';
  const rules=readonlyCache?.admissionMinimums||[];
  const html=rows.map(row=>{const rule=rules.find(r=>String(r.university||'')===String(row.university||''));const latest=latestMockSnapshot(student.id), min=rule?minimumCheck(rule,admissionBase(student.id,latest.exam||'6월모의고사').mock):null;let state='자료확인';if(row.diff!=null)state=row.diff<=0?'가능권':row.diff<=.45?'경계':row.diff<=.9?'도전':'상향';const cls=state==='가능권'?'good':state==='경계'?'warn':state==='도전'?'warn':'bad';const internal=row.diff==null?'-':row.diff<=0?'충족':`+${row.diff.toFixed(2)}`;const mock=min?.result|| (gap.mockGap===0?'핵심충족':'보완');const point=row.diff==null?'입결 연결 확인':row.diff<=0?'학종 서류 연속성 강화':row.diff<=.45?'내신 경계 관리':row.diff<=.9?'내신·모의 동시 보완':'상향 지원 관찰';return `<div class="dorm-univ-row"><b>${escapeHtml(row.university)}</b><span class="fit ${cls}">${state}</span><span><small>내신</small>${escapeHtml(internal)}</span><span><small>모의/최저</small>${escapeHtml(mock)}</span><span><small>학종·계열</small>${holistic}</span><em>${escapeHtml(point)}</em></div>`;}).join('');
  return `<section class="dorm-university-fit"><header><div><small>SEOUL · UNIVERSITY FIT</small><h4>인서울 대학별 적합도</h4></div><span>${escapeHtml(career.hopeTrack)} · ${escapeHtml(career.hopeMajor)}</span></header><div class="dorm-univ-head"><span>대학</span><span>현재판정</span><span>내신</span><span>모의/최저</span><span>학종·계열</span><span>핵심 보완</span></div>${html||'<div class="empty-inline">연결된 인서울 대학 입결 자료가 없습니다.</div>'}<small class="dorm-univ-note">대학별 입결·수능최저 연결자료를 활용한 상담 참고값이며 합격 가능성을 확정하지 않습니다.</small></section>`;
}
