    ["01","학부모 안내","프로그램·학교일정·활동 결과를 학부모용 안내자료로 구성합니다."],

}

      preview=`<div class="preview-student-head"><b>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</b><span>입시 종합</span></div><div class="preview-metrics"><span><small>내신 5등급 평균</small><b>${base?.avg5==null?'-':Number(base.avg5).toFixed(2)}</b></span><span><small>실제 9등급 참고</small><b>${base?.actual9==null?'-':Number(base.actual9).toFixed(2)}</b></span><span><small>진로희망</small><b>${escapeHtml(student.career||'-')}</b></span></div>`;

}
function printScorePackage(student,mode){
  if(!student)return;
  const all=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(r=>r.studentId===student.id);
  const internal=all.filter(r=>r.scoreType==="내신");
  const mock=all.filter(r=>r.scoreType==="모의고사");
  const gradeRows=internal.filter(r=>Number.isFinite(Number(r.level)));
  const avg=gradeRows.length?(gradeRows.reduce((s,r)=>s+Number(r.level),0)/gradeRows.length).toFixed(2):"-";
  const mockExams=[...new Set(mock.map(r=>r.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b));
  const latestMock=mockExams.at(-1)||"-";
  const subjects=[...new Set(mock.map(r=>r.subject).filter(Boolean))];
  const summary=`<div class="score-print-summary"><div><small>최근 내신 평균</small><b>${avg}</b><span>등급 평균</span></div><div><small>내신 자료</small><b>${internal.length}</b><span>과목·시험 기록</span></div><div><small>최근 모의고사</small><b>${escapeHtml(latestMock)}</b><span>${mock.length}개 성적</span></div><div><small>조회범위</small><b>${mode==="combined"?"종합":mode==="mock"?"모의":"내신"}</b><span>개인별 누적</span></div></div>`;
  const internalExams=[...new Set(internal.map(r=>r.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b));
  const internalSubjects=[...new Set(internal.map(r=>r.subject).filter(Boolean))];
  const internalTable=`<section class="print-section"><h2>내신 누적 성적</h2><table class="score-wide"><thead><tr><th>과목</th>${internalExams.map(e=>`<th>${escapeHtml(e)}<small>등급 / 원점수</small></th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${internalSubjects.map(subject=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(subject)}</td>${internalExams.map(exam=>{const r=internal.find(x=>x.subject===subject&&x.exam===exam);return `<td>${r?`<b>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.level))}등급</b><small>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.rawScore))}점 · ${escapeHtml(r.achievement||"")}</small>`:"-"}</td>`}).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></section>`;
  const mockTable=`<section class="print-section"><h2>모의고사 비교</h2><table class="score-wide mock"><thead><tr><th>영역</th>${mockExams.map(e=>`<th>${escapeHtml(e)}<small>등급 / 원점수 / 표준</small></th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${subjects.map(subject=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(subject)}</td>${mockExams.map(exam=>{const r=mock.find(x=>x.subject===subject&&x.exam===exam);return `<td>${r?`<b>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.level))}등급</b><small>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.rawScore))}점 · 표준 ${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.standardScore))}${r.percentile!==""?` · 백분위 ${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.percentile))}`:""}</small>`:"-"}</td>`}).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></section>`;
  const body=`<header class="print-head score-title"><small>UNHO EDUCATION PLATFORM · 학생상담 성적 종합</small><h1>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</h1><p>진로희망: ${escapeHtml(student.career||"-")} · 전체 누적 성적</p>${studentPrintBadgesMarkup(student)}</header>${summary}${mode!=="mock"?internalTable:""}${mode!=="internal"?mockTable:""}`;
  openPrintDocument(`${student.studentNo}_${student.name}_성적상담자료`,body,"portrait");
}


function printAdmissionSummaryPackage(student){
  if(!student)return;
  const base=admissionBase(student.id);
  const body=`<header class="print-head score-title"><small>UNHO EDUCATION PLATFORM</small><h1>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)} · 입시 종합</h1><p>진로희망: ${escapeHtml(student.career||'-')}</p>${studentPrintBadgesMarkup(student)}</header><section class="print-section"><h2>입시 종합 요약</h2><div class="score-print-summary"><div><small>내신 5등급 평균</small><b>${base?.avg5==null?'-':Number(base.avg5).toFixed(2)}</b></div><div><small>실제 9등급 참고</small><b>${base?.actual9==null?'-':Number(base.actual9).toFixed(2)}</b></div><div><small>최근 모의</small><b>${escapeHtml(base?.mockExam||'-')}</b></div><div><small>관리 구분</small><b>${studentAdmissionPrintLabel(student)||'일반'}</b></div></div></section><section class="print-section"><h2>상담 메모</h2><div class="record-box">UEP 입시 메뉴의 종합 분석을 상담자료로 활용하세요.</div></section>`;
  openPrintDocument(`${student.studentNo}_${student.name}_입시종합`,body,'portrait');
}

  return `<div class="uep-hub-tabs output-hub-tabs"><button class="${outputHubMode==="alerts"?"active":""}" data-output-hub="alerts"><b>알림</b><small>학생 안내·제출·출결</small></button><button class="${outputHubMode==="print"?"active":""}" data-output-hub="print"><b>출력</b><small>보고서·출석부·상담자료</small></button></div>`;
}

function availableClasses() {
  const found = [...new Set((readonlyCache?.students || []).map(classNumberOf).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>Number(a)-Number(b));
  return found.length ? found : Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1));
}
function optionStudents(classNo, selectedId = "") {
  return (readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(s => !classNo || classNumberOf(s) === String(classNo)).map(s => `<option value="${escapeHtml(s.id)}" ${s.id === selectedId ? "selected" : ""}>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(s.name)}</option>`).join("");
}

function scorePeriodKey(row) {
  const year = String(row?.schoolYear || today.getFullYear()).replace(/[^0-9]/g, "") || String(today.getFullYear());
  let semester = String(row?.semester || "").match(/[12]/)?.[0] || "";
  if (!semester && row?.scoreType === "모의고사") {
    const month = Number(String(row.exam || "").match(/(\d{1,2})\s*월/)?.[1] || 0);
    semester = month && month >= 8 ? "2" : "1";
  }
  return `${year}-${semester || "1"}`;
}
function scorePeriodLabel(key) {
  if (!key || key === "all") return "전체 누적";
  const [year, semester] = String(key).split("-");
  return `${year}학년도 ${semester || "1"}학기`;
}
function availableScorePeriods(mode = scoreMode) {
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => mode === "combined" || row.scoreType === (mode === "mock" ? "모의고사" : "내신"));
  return [...new Set(rows.map(scorePeriodKey).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b));
}
function scoreExamOrder(exam) {
  const order = ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사","3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사","수능"];
  const index = order.indexOf(exam);
  return index < 0 ? 999 : index;
}
function scoreTrend(row, allRows) {
  const orders = row.scoreType === "모의고사" ? ["3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사"] : ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사"];
  const index = orders.indexOf(row.exam);
  if (index <= 0 || row.level === "" || row.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
  const previous = allRows.find(x => x.studentId === row.studentId && x.scoreType === row.scoreType && x.subject === row.subject && x.exam === orders[index - 1] && scorePeriodKey(x) === period);
  if (!previous || previous.level === "" || previous.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  const diff = Number(previous.level) - Number(row.level);
  if (!Number.isFinite(diff) || diff === 0) return { text:"유지", cls:"" };
  return diff > 0 ? { text:`▲ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"good" } : { text:`▼ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"bad" };
}
function gradeToneClass(value) {
  const grade = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(grade) && grade >= 1 && grade <= 9 ? `grade-${grade}` : "grade-na";
}
function gradeBadgeMarkup(value, suffix = "등급") {

function scoreTrend(row, allRows) {
  const orders = row.scoreType === "모의고사" ? ["3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사"] : ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사"];
  const index = orders.indexOf(row.exam);
  if (index <= 0 || row.level === "" || row.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
  const previous = allRows.find(x => x.studentId === row.studentId && x.scoreType === row.scoreType && x.subject === row.subject && x.exam === orders[index - 1] && scorePeriodKey(x) === period);
  if (!previous || previous.level === "" || previous.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  const diff = Number(previous.level) - Number(row.level);
  if (!Number.isFinite(diff) || diff === 0) return { text:"유지", cls:"" };
  return diff > 0 ? { text:`▲ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"good" } : { text:`▼ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"bad" };
}
function gradeToneClass(value) {

  return Number.isFinite(grade) && grade >= 1 && grade <= 9 ? `grade-${grade}` : "grade-na";
}
function gradeBadgeMarkup(value, suffix = "등급") {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">-</span>`;
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">${escapeHtml(String(value))}</span>`;
  return `<span class="grade-badge ${gradeToneClass(number)}"><b>${scoreDisplay(number,2)}</b><small>${suffix}</small></span>`;
}
function mockGradeMiniMarkup(studentId, exam) {
  const order = ["국어", "수학", "영어", "한국사"];
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => row.studentId === studentId && row.scoreType === "모의고사" && row.exam === exam);
  if (!rows.length) return `<span class="mock-grade-empty">자료 없음</span>`;
  const sorted = rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=order.indexOf(a.subject), bi=order.indexOf(b.subject);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi);
-----
  return `${year}학년도 ${semester || "1"}학기`;
}

function gradeBadgeMarkup(value, suffix = "등급") {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">-</span>`;
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">${escapeHtml(String(value))}</span>`;

function mockGradeMiniMarkup(studentId, exam) {
  const order = ["국어", "수학", "영어", "한국사"];
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => row.studentId === studentId && row.scoreType === "모의고사" && row.exam === exam);
  if (!rows.length) return `<span class="mock-grade-empty">자료 없음</span>`;
  const sorted = rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=order.indexOf(a.subject), bi=order.indexOf(b.subject);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi);
    return String(a.subject).localeCompare(String(b.subject),"ko");
  });
  return `<div class="mock-grade-mini-strip">${sorted.map(row=>`<span><small>${escapeHtml(row.subject)}</small><b class="${gradeToneClass(row.level)}">${escapeHtml(String(row.level || "-"))}</b></span>`).join("")}</div>`;
}
function scoreTableMarkup(rows, mode = scoreMode) {
  const coreOrder = mode === "mock" ? ["국어","수학","영어","한국사"] : ["공통국어1","공통수학1","공통영어1","한국사1","통합사회1","통합과학1","한문","과학탐구실험1","체육1","음악"];
  const subjects = [...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=coreOrder.indexOf(a), bi=coreOrder.indexOf(b);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?999:ai)-(bi<0?999:bi);
-----
  return [...new Set(rows.map(scorePeriodKey).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b));
}

  const order = ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사","3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사","수능"];
  const index = order.indexOf(exam);
  return index < 0 ? 999 : index;

function scoreTrend(row, allRows) {
  const orders = row.scoreType === "모의고사" ? ["3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사"] : ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사"];
  const index = orders.indexOf(row.exam);
  if (index <= 0 || row.level === "" || row.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
  const previous = allRows.find(x => x.studentId === row.studentId && x.scoreType === row.scoreType && x.subject === row.subject && x.exam === orders[index - 1] && scorePeriodKey(x) === period);
  if (!previous || previous.level === "" || previous.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  const diff = Number(previous.level) - Number(row.level);
  if (!Number.isFinite(diff) || diff === 0) return { text:"유지", cls:"" };
  return diff > 0 ? { text:`▲ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"good" } : { text:`▼ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"bad" };
}
function gradeToneClass(value) {
  const grade = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(grade) && grade >= 1 && grade <= 9 ? `grade-${grade}` : "grade-na";
}
function gradeBadgeMarkup(value, suffix = "등급") {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">-</span>`;
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">${escapeHtml(String(value))}</span>`;
  return `<span class="grade-badge ${gradeToneClass(number)}"><b>${scoreDisplay(number,2)}</b><small>${suffix}</small></span>`;
}
function mockGradeMiniMarkup(studentId, exam) {
  const order = ["국어", "수학", "영어", "한국사"];
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => row.studentId === studentId && row.scoreType === "모의고사" && row.exam === exam);
  if (!rows.length) return `<span class="mock-grade-empty">자료 없음</span>`;
  const sorted = rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=order.indexOf(a.subject), bi=order.indexOf(b.subject);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi);
    return String(a.subject).localeCompare(String(b.subject),"ko");
  });
  return `<div class="mock-grade-mini-strip">${sorted.map(row=>`<span><small>${escapeHtml(row.subject)}</small><b class="${gradeToneClass(row.level)}">${escapeHtml(String(row.level || "-"))}</b></span>`).join("")}</div>`;
}

  const subjects = [...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=coreOrder.indexOf(a), bi=coreOrder.indexOf(b);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?999:ai)-(bi<0?999:bi);
    return a.localeCompare(b,"ko");
  });

function dormRuleHistory(){
  const sheetRules=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.dormRules)?readonlyCache.dormRules:[];
  if(sheetRules.length) return sheetRules.map(x=>({...x,active:x.active!==false}));
  return dormDefaultRules().map(x=>({...x,active:x.active!==false,source:'UEP 기본값(시트 연결 대기)'}));
}
function setDormRuleCache(rules){
  readonlyCache=readonlyCache||{};
  readonlyCache.dormRules=(Array.isArray(rules)?rules:[]).map(x=>({...x}));
}

// v0.76.97: 학사외출 규칙 읽기는 일반 UEP readonly 동기화 경로 하나만 사용합니다.
function refreshDormRulesFromReadonly({rerender=true,silent=true}={}){
  const rules=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.dormRules)?readonlyCache.dormRules:[];
  if(rerender&&dormMode==='outing')render('dorm');
  if(!silent)toast(rules.length?`학사운영규칙 ${rules.length}건이 연결되어 있습니다.`:'학사운영규칙을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  return rules.length>0;
}

}

function dormRuleHistory(){
  const sheetRules=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.dormRules)?readonlyCache.dormRules:[];
  if(sheetRules.length) return sheetRules.map(x=>({...x,active:x.active!==false}));
  return dormDefaultRules().map(x=>({...x,active:x.active!==false,source:'UEP 기본값(시트 연결 대기)'}));
}
function setDormRuleCache(rules){
  readonlyCache=readonlyCache||{};
  readonlyCache.dormRules=(Array.isArray(rules)?rules:[]).map(x=>({...x}));
}

// v0.76.97: 학사외출 규칙 읽기는 일반 UEP readonly 동기화 경로 하나만 사용합니다.
function refreshDormRulesFromReadonly({rerender=true,silent=true}={}){
  const rules=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.dormRules)?readonlyCache.dormRules:[];
  if(rerender&&dormMode==='outing')render('dorm');
  if(!silent)toast(rules.length?`학사운영규칙 ${rules.length}건이 연결되어 있습니다.`:'학사운영규칙을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  return rules.length>0;
}

function dormRuleCategoryLabel(category){
  return ({period:'학교 운영기간',closure:'학사 미운영기간',exception:'입·퇴소 변경',exam:'시험 외출허용',vacation:'방학 중 외출허용',base:'기본 입·퇴소'})[category]||'기타 규칙';
}
function dormRuleNormalizeDateTime(value){
  if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='') return '';
  // Google Sheets 날짜 직렬값은 시트의 '현지 날짜/시간' 값이다.
  // UTC instant로 해석한 뒤 Asia/Seoul을 다시 적용하면 +9시간이 생기므로,
  // 직렬값의 날짜/시간 성분을 그대로 추출한다.
  if(typeof value==='number'||/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(String(value).trim())){
    const serial=Number(value);
    if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000){
      const d=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+Math.round(serial*86400000));
-----
===== PATTERN: grades =====
      const rowClass=classNumberOf(found||{}) || (String(row.className||"").match(/(?:^|\D)(\d+)\s*반?\s*$/)?.[1] || String(row.className||"").match(/\d+/)?.[0] || "");
      return rowClass===no && ([String(row.studentId||""),String(row.studentNo||"")].some(id=>id&&rosterIds.has(id)) || Boolean(found));
    }).length;
    return {classNo:no,roster:roster.length,participants,checks};
  });
  const selectedSummary=classSummaries.find(item=>item.classNo===classNo)||{classNo,roster:students.length,participants:0,checks:0};
  const classCards=`<div class="night-class-overview daily-class-overview">${classSummaries.map(item=>`<button data-night-class="${item.classNo}" class="${classNo===item.classNo?"active":""}"><small>${item.classNo}반 · 재적 ${item.roster}명</small><b>${item.participants}<em>당일 참여</em></b><span>출석체크 ${item.checks}건</span></button>`).join("")}</div>`;

  const dateHead=dates.map(day=>`<th colspan="3" class="${day===attendanceViewDate?"selected-day":""}" data-night-date="${escapeHtml(day)}"><b>${escapeHtml(day.slice(5).replace("-","/"))}</b><small>${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(day))}</small></th>`).join("");
  const slotHead=dates.map(day=>slots.map(slot=>`<th class="${day===attendanceViewDate?"selected-day-slot":""}">${escapeHtml(slot)}</th>`).join("")).join("");
  const bodyRows=students.map(student=>`<tr><th class="student-name-cell"><button data-night-student="${escapeHtml(attendanceStudentKey(student))}"><b>${escapeHtml(String(student.number||"").padStart(2,"0"))}번</b><span>${escapeHtml(student.name)}</span></button></th>${dates.map(day=>slots.map(slot=>{const status=resolveStatus(student,day,slot);const programAttrs=status.programId?` data-night-program="${escapeHtml(status.programId)}" data-night-program-kind="${escapeHtml(status.programKind||"general")}"`:"";return `<td class="${day===attendanceViewDate?"selected-day-cell":""}"><button class="night-status-cell ${status.cls}" title="${escapeHtml(status.title)}" data-night-student="${escapeHtml(attendanceStudentKey(student))}" data-night-day="${escapeHtml(day)}" data-night-slot="${escapeHtml(slot)}"${programAttrs}><span>${escapeHtml(status.label)}</span></button></td>`;}).join("")).join("")}</tr>`).join("");
  const matrix=dates.length?`<div class="night-month-scroll-hint">선택일 열은 강조 표시됩니다. 월간 운영일은 항상 유지됩니다. ← 좌우로 스크롤하여 월 전체 확인 →</div><div class="night-month-matrix"><table><thead><tr><th rowspan="2" class="student-name-head">학생</th>${dateHead}</tr><tr>${slotHead}</tr></thead><tbody>${bodyRows}</tbody></table></div>`:`<div class="empty-state compact-empty data-card"><b>${nightStudyOperationStatus(attendanceViewDate).operates?`${nightViewMonth} 야자출결 자료가 없습니다.`:`${attendanceViewDate} · ${escapeHtml(nightStudyOperationStatus(attendanceViewDate).reason)}`}</b><span>${nightStudyOperationStatus(attendanceViewDate).operates?'운영일인데 자료가 없다면 데이터 처리시트의 30_야자출결_정규화를 확인하세요.':'미운영일은 결석·미입력으로 집계하지 않습니다.'}</span></div>`;
  const gradeSummary=classSummaries.reduce((sum,item)=>({roster:sum.roster+item.roster,participants:sum.participants+item.participants,checks:sum.checks+item.checks}),{roster:0,participants:0,checks:0});
  return `<div class="module-page">${tabs}${attendanceDateControls("야자 조회일")}<section class="attendance-dashboard-head night"><div><small>NIGHT STUDY · ${attendanceViewDate} ${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(attendanceViewDate,true))}</small><h3>1학년 선택일 참여현황</h3><p>1학년 전체 재적을 기준으로, 오후자습·야자1·야자2 중 출결 기록이 확인된 학생을 참여로 집계합니다.</p></div><div class="attendance-head-metrics"><span><b>${gradeSummary.roster}</b>1학년 재적인원</span><span><b>${gradeSummary.participants}</b>학년 참여인원</span><span><b>${gradeSummary.checks}</b>선택일 출석체크</span><span><b>${dates.length}</b>${nightViewMonth} 운영일</span></div></section>${classCards}<div class="section-title-row monthly-night-title"><div><small>MONTHLY · ${nightViewMonth}</small><h3>${classNo}반 월간 참여현황</h3></div><span class="state">${attendanceViewDate} ${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(attendanceViewDate,true))} 선택</span></div><div class="night-matrix-legend"><span class="present">출석</span><span class="dorm">학사</span><span class="after">방과후 강좌명</span><span class="program">프로그램명</span><span class="late">지각</span><span class="pending">확인</span><span class="none">기록 없음</span></div>${matrix}</div>`;
}
function openNightAttendanceDetail(studentKey, day, slot) {
  const students = readonlyCache?.students || [];
  const student = students.find(item => attendanceStudentKey(item) === studentKey) || {};
  const rows = readonlyCache?.nightAttendance || readonlyCache?.attendance || [];
  const row = rows.find(item => item.date === day && nightSlotName(item) === slot && (attendanceStudentKey(item) === studentKey || String(item.studentNo||"") === String(student.studentNo||"")));
  openDrawer(`${student.name || "학생"} · ${day} ${slot}`);
  $("#drawerTitle")?.classList.add("privacy-sensitive-text");
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="history-summary positive"><span><small>학번</small><b class="privacy-sensitive-text">${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||"-")}</b></span><span><small>학급</small><b>${escapeHtml(student.className||"-")}반</b></span><span><small>일자</small><b>${escapeHtml(day)}</b></span><span><small>시간</small><b>${escapeHtml(slot)}</b></span></div><div class="drawer-section"><h4>야자출결 상세</h4><dl class="detail-list"><dt>결과</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.result||"출석 기록 없음")}</dd><dt>소속·유형</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.affiliation||row?.type||"일반 야자")}</dd><dt>상세</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.detail||"-")}</dd><dt>원본 시간</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.time||row?.period||slot)}</dd></dl></div><p class="drawer-note">방과후·야간심화·추가 프로그램 상태는 해당 프로그램 상세 화면으로 바로 연결됩니다.</p>`;
}

function subjectsView() {
  return `<div class="module-page">${toolbar("학생·희망과목·오류 검색", "신청 입력")}<div class="data-card"><div class="data-head student-grid"><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>1차 신청</span><span>상담</span><span>상태</span></div>${samples.students.map((r, i) => `<div class="data-row student-grid"><b>${r[0]}</b><span>${r[1]}</span><span>${i === 2 ? "미신청" : "완료"}</span><span>${i === 2 ? "필요" : "-"}</span><span class="state ${i === 2 ? "warn" : ""}">${i === 2 ? "확인 필요" : "정상"}</span></div>`).join("")}</div></div>`;
}
function schoolScheduleTimetableMarkup(){
  const anchor=new Date(`${calendarSelectedDate||dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  const monday=new Date(anchor); monday.setDate(anchor.getDate()-((anchor.getDay()+6)%7));
  const days=Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const d=new Date(monday);d.setDate(monday.getDate()+i);return d;});
  const dayEvents=days.map(d=>schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(d)).filter(calendarEventMatchesFilter).sort((a,b)=>String(a.time||"99:99").localeCompare(String(b.time||"99:99"))));
  const maxRows=Math.max(1,...dayEvents.map(rows=>rows.length));
  const rows=Array.from({length:maxRows},(_,rowIndex)=>`<div class="school-schedule-row"><div class="school-schedule-slot">${rowIndex+1}</div>${dayEvents.map(events=>{const event=events[rowIndex];if(!event)return '<div class="school-schedule-cell empty"><span>—</span></div>';const kind=calendarEventVisualKind(event);return `<button class="school-schedule-cell kind-${kind} ${calendarEventIsPending(event)?"pending":""}" data-school-event="${escapeHtml(event.id)}"><time>${escapeHtml(event.time||"종일")}</time><b>${escapeHtml(calendarShortTitle(calendarDisplayTitle(event),26))}</b><small>${escapeHtml([event.target,event.place].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")||event.owner||"학교일정")}</small></button>`;}).join("")}</div>`).join("");
  return `<section class="school-schedule-timetable"><div class="school-schedule-head"><div class="school-schedule-corner">순서</div>${days.map(d=>`<button data-calendar-date="${dateKey(d)}" class="${dateKey(d)===dateKey(today)?"today":""}"><b>${["일","월","화","수","목","금","토"][d.getDay()]}</b><span>${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}</span><em>${schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(d)).length}건</em></button>`).join("")}</div>${rows}</section>`;
}
function calendarView() {
  const hubTabs=`<div class="uep-hub-tabs calendar-hub-tabs"><button class="${calendarHubMode==="schedule"?"active":""}" data-calendar-hub="schedule"><b>학교일정</b><small>월간·주간 일정</small></button><button class="${calendarHubMode==="timetable"?"active":""}" data-calendar-hub="timetable"><b>시간표</b><small>개인·학급·학생·교사</small></button></div>`;
  if(calendarHubMode==="timetable") return `<div class="module-page calendar-timetable-hub">${hubTabs}${timetableView()}</div>`;
  const anchor=new Date(`${calendarSelectedDate||dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  return `<div class="module-page calendar-page-v654 school-schedule-page">${hubTabs}<div class="calendar-page-toolbar"><div><small>SCHOOL SCHEDULE · 데이터처리시트 41_학교캘린더</small><h3>학교일정</h3><p>월간계획을 기본으로 확인하고 월간·주간 보기를 자유롭게 전환합니다.</p></div><div><button class="btn secondary" data-calendar-today>${state.calendarMode==="week"?"이번 주":"오늘"}</button><button class="btn primary" data-reference="calendar-add">＋ 개인 일정</button></div></div><div class="school-schedule-summary"><b>${anchor.getFullYear()}년 ${anchor.getMonth()+1}월</b><span>선택일 ${anchor.getMonth()+1}/${anchor.getDate()} · ${schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(anchor)).length}건</span></div><div class="card calendar-page-card school-schedule-card">${calendarMarkup(true)}<div class="widget-source-footer dashboard-source">출처: 데이터처리시트 41_학교캘린더 · 대시보드 월간/주간과 동일 DB</div></div></div>`;
}

  const matrix=dates.length?`<div class="night-month-scroll-hint">선택일 열은 강조 표시됩니다. 월간 운영일은 항상 유지됩니다. ← 좌우로 스크롤하여 월 전체 확인 →</div><div class="night-month-matrix"><table><thead><tr><th rowspan="2" class="student-name-head">학생</th>${dateHead}</tr><tr>${slotHead}</tr></thead><tbody>${bodyRows}</tbody></table></div>`:`<div class="empty-state compact-empty data-card"><b>${nightStudyOperationStatus(attendanceViewDate).operates?`${nightViewMonth} 야자출결 자료가 없습니다.`:`${attendanceViewDate} · ${escapeHtml(nightStudyOperationStatus(attendanceViewDate).reason)}`}</b><span>${nightStudyOperationStatus(attendanceViewDate).operates?'운영일인데 자료가 없다면 데이터 처리시트의 30_야자출결_정규화를 확인하세요.':'미운영일은 결석·미입력으로 집계하지 않습니다.'}</span></div>`;
  const gradeSummary=classSummaries.reduce((sum,item)=>({roster:sum.roster+item.roster,participants:sum.participants+item.participants,checks:sum.checks+item.checks}),{roster:0,participants:0,checks:0});
  return `<div class="module-page">${tabs}${attendanceDateControls("야자 조회일")}<section class="attendance-dashboard-head night"><div><small>NIGHT STUDY · ${attendanceViewDate} ${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(attendanceViewDate,true))}</small><h3>1학년 선택일 참여현황</h3><p>1학년 전체 재적을 기준으로, 오후자습·야자1·야자2 중 출결 기록이 확인된 학생을 참여로 집계합니다.</p></div><div class="attendance-head-metrics"><span><b>${gradeSummary.roster}</b>1학년 재적인원</span><span><b>${gradeSummary.participants}</b>학년 참여인원</span><span><b>${gradeSummary.checks}</b>선택일 출석체크</span><span><b>${dates.length}</b>${nightViewMonth} 운영일</span></div></section>${classCards}<div class="section-title-row monthly-night-title"><div><small>MONTHLY · ${nightViewMonth}</small><h3>${classNo}반 월간 참여현황</h3></div><span class="state">${attendanceViewDate} ${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(attendanceViewDate,true))} 선택</span></div><div class="night-matrix-legend"><span class="present">출석</span><span class="dorm">학사</span><span class="after">방과후 강좌명</span><span class="program">프로그램명</span><span class="late">지각</span><span class="pending">확인</span><span class="none">기록 없음</span></div>${matrix}</div>`;
}
function openNightAttendanceDetail(studentKey, day, slot) {
  const students = readonlyCache?.students || [];
  const student = students.find(item => attendanceStudentKey(item) === studentKey) || {};
  const rows = readonlyCache?.nightAttendance || readonlyCache?.attendance || [];
  const row = rows.find(item => item.date === day && nightSlotName(item) === slot && (attendanceStudentKey(item) === studentKey || String(item.studentNo||"") === String(student.studentNo||"")));
  openDrawer(`${student.name || "학생"} · ${day} ${slot}`);
  $("#drawerTitle")?.classList.add("privacy-sensitive-text");
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="history-summary positive"><span><small>학번</small><b class="privacy-sensitive-text">${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||"-")}</b></span><span><small>학급</small><b>${escapeHtml(student.className||"-")}반</b></span><span><small>일자</small><b>${escapeHtml(day)}</b></span><span><small>시간</small><b>${escapeHtml(slot)}</b></span></div><div class="drawer-section"><h4>야자출결 상세</h4><dl class="detail-list"><dt>결과</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.result||"출석 기록 없음")}</dd><dt>소속·유형</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.affiliation||row?.type||"일반 야자")}</dd><dt>상세</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.detail||"-")}</dd><dt>원본 시간</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.time||row?.period||slot)}</dd></dl></div><p class="drawer-note">방과후·야간심화·추가 프로그램 상태는 해당 프로그램 상세 화면으로 바로 연결됩니다.</p>`;
}

function subjectsView() {
  return `<div class="module-page">${toolbar("학생·희망과목·오류 검색", "신청 입력")}<div class="data-card"><div class="data-head student-grid"><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>1차 신청</span><span>상담</span><span>상태</span></div>${samples.students.map((r, i) => `<div class="data-row student-grid"><b>${r[0]}</b><span>${r[1]}</span><span>${i === 2 ? "미신청" : "완료"}</span><span>${i === 2 ? "필요" : "-"}</span><span class="state ${i === 2 ? "warn" : ""}">${i === 2 ? "확인 필요" : "정상"}</span></div>`).join("")}</div></div>`;
}
function schoolScheduleTimetableMarkup(){
  const anchor=new Date(`${calendarSelectedDate||dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  const monday=new Date(anchor); monday.setDate(anchor.getDate()-((anchor.getDay()+6)%7));
  const days=Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const d=new Date(monday);d.setDate(monday.getDate()+i);return d;});
  const dayEvents=days.map(d=>schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(d)).filter(calendarEventMatchesFilter).sort((a,b)=>String(a.time||"99:99").localeCompare(String(b.time||"99:99"))));
  const maxRows=Math.max(1,...dayEvents.map(rows=>rows.length));
  const rows=Array.from({length:maxRows},(_,rowIndex)=>`<div class="school-schedule-row"><div class="school-schedule-slot">${rowIndex+1}</div>${dayEvents.map(events=>{const event=events[rowIndex];if(!event)return '<div class="school-schedule-cell empty"><span>—</span></div>';const kind=calendarEventVisualKind(event);return `<button class="school-schedule-cell kind-${kind} ${calendarEventIsPending(event)?"pending":""}" data-school-event="${escapeHtml(event.id)}"><time>${escapeHtml(event.time||"종일")}</time><b>${escapeHtml(calendarShortTitle(calendarDisplayTitle(event),26))}</b><small>${escapeHtml([event.target,event.place].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")||event.owner||"학교일정")}</small></button>`;}).join("")}</div>`).join("");
  return `<section class="school-schedule-timetable"><div class="school-schedule-head"><div class="school-schedule-corner">순서</div>${days.map(d=>`<button data-calendar-date="${dateKey(d)}" class="${dateKey(d)===dateKey(today)?"today":""}"><b>${["일","월","화","수","목","금","토"][d.getDay()]}</b><span>${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}</span><em>${schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(d)).length}건</em></button>`).join("")}</div>${rows}</section>`;
}
function calendarView() {
  const hubTabs=`<div class="uep-hub-tabs calendar-hub-tabs"><button class="${calendarHubMode==="schedule"?"active":""}" data-calendar-hub="schedule"><b>학교일정</b><small>월간·주간 일정</small></button><button class="${calendarHubMode==="timetable"?"active":""}" data-calendar-hub="timetable"><b>시간표</b><small>개인·학급·학생·교사</small></button></div>`;
  if(calendarHubMode==="timetable") return `<div class="module-page calendar-timetable-hub">${hubTabs}${timetableView()}</div>`;
  const anchor=new Date(`${calendarSelectedDate||dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  return `<div class="module-page calendar-page-v654 school-schedule-page">${hubTabs}<div class="calendar-page-toolbar"><div><small>SCHOOL SCHEDULE · 데이터처리시트 41_학교캘린더</small><h3>학교일정</h3><p>월간계획을 기본으로 확인하고 월간·주간 보기를 자유롭게 전환합니다.</p></div><div><button class="btn secondary" data-calendar-today>${state.calendarMode==="week"?"이번 주":"오늘"}</button><button class="btn primary" data-reference="calendar-add">＋ 개인 일정</button></div></div><div class="school-schedule-summary"><b>${anchor.getFullYear()}년 ${anchor.getMonth()+1}월</b><span>선택일 ${anchor.getMonth()+1}/${anchor.getDate()} · ${schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(anchor)).length}건</span></div><div class="card calendar-page-card school-schedule-card">${calendarMarkup(true)}<div class="widget-source-footer dashboard-source">출처: 데이터처리시트 41_학교캘린더 · 대시보드 월간/주간과 동일 DB</div></div></div>`;
}

function inputCenterBatchStudents(){
  const students=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(s=>!String(s.status||s.enrollmentStatus||'').includes('전출'));
  const scope=inputCenterConfig.batchScope||'학급';
  if(scope==='학급') return students.filter(s=>String(s.grade||String(s.studentNo||'').slice(0,1))===String(inputCenterConfig.batchGrade||'1') && String(s.className||classNumberOf(s)||'').replace(/반/g,'')===String(inputCenterConfig.batchClass||'1'));
  if(scope==='학년전체') return students.filter(s=>String(s.grade||String(s.studentNo||'').slice(0,1))===String(inputCenterConfig.batchGrade||'1'));

function mockAllExamComparisonMarkup(classNo){
  const rows=scoreStatisticsRows("mock","",classNo);
  const exams=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b)||String(a).localeCompare(String(b),'ko'));
  const preferred=['국어','수학','영어','한국사','통합사회','통합과학'];
  const subjects=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{const ai=preferred.indexOf(a),bi=preferred.indexOf(b);return(ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi)||String(a).localeCompare(String(b),'ko');});
  if(!rows.length)return '<div class="query-empty"><b>모의고사 자료가 없습니다.</b></div>';
  const head='<div class="score-grade-head"><span>과목·시험</span>'+Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>'<span>'+(i+1)+'등급</span>').join('')+'<span>1~2</span><span>1~4</span></div>';
  const body=subjects.map(subject=>'<section class="mock-compare-subject"><h4>'+escapeHtml(subject)+'</h4>'+exams.map(exam=>{const scope=rows.filter(row=>row.subject===subject&&row.exam===exam),counts=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>scope.filter(row=>Number(row.level)===i+1).length),values=[...counts,counts[0]+counts[1],counts.slice(0,4).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)];return '<div class="score-grade-row mock-compare-row"><b>'+escapeHtml(exam)+'</b>'+values.slice(0,9).map((v,i)=>'<span><small>'+(i+1)+'등급</small><strong>'+v+'</strong></span>').join('')+'<em><small>1~2등급</small><strong>'+values[9]+'</strong></em><em><small>1~4등급</small><strong>'+values[10]+'</strong></em></div>';}).join('')+'</section>').join('');
  return '<section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>전체 모의고사 과목별 등급 비교</h3><p>왼쪽은 과목, 과목 안의 행은 3월·6월·9월·11월 시험이며 위쪽 등급별 인원을 동시에 비교합니다.</p></div><span>'+exams.length+'회</span></div>'+head+body+'</section>';
}

function mockTrendOverviewMarkup(rows,exams,subjects){
  const grades=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>i+1),categories=[...grades.map(x=>x+'등급'),'1~2','1~4'];
  const values=(subject,exam)=>{const basic=grades.map(grade=>rows.filter(row=>row.subject===subject&&row.exam===exam&&Number(row.level)===grade).length);return[...basic,basic[0]+basic[1],basic.slice(0,4).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)];};
  const matrix=new Map(subjects.flatMap(subject=>exams.map(exam=>[subject+'|'+exam,values(subject,exam)])));
  const max=Math.max(1,...[...matrix.values()].flat()),chartMax=Math.max(max+4,Math.ceil(max*1.12)),W=1180,H=410,left=52,right=18,top=42,bottom=86,plotW=W-left-right,plotH=H-top-bottom,groupW=plotW/categories.length;
  const colors=['#0b8a78','#2f80ed','#f2994a','#9b51e0','#eb5757','#219653'],pairCount=Math.max(1,subjects.length*exams.length),gap=1,barW=Math.max(2,Math.min(8,(groupW-10-gap*(pairCount-1))/pairCount)),y=v=>top+plotH-(v/chartMax)*plotH;
  const grid=Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const value=Math.round(chartMax*(4-i)/4),yy=top+plotH*i/4;return '<line x1="'+left+'" y1="'+yy+'" x2="'+(W-right)+'" y2="'+yy+'" stroke="#dfe9ec"/><text x="'+(left-8)+'" y="'+(yy+4)+'" text-anchor="end" font-size="11" fill="#718690">'+value+'</text>';}).join('');
  const bars=categories.map((category,ci)=>{const used=barW*pairCount+gap*(pairCount-1),start=left+ci*groupW+(groupW-used)/2;let n=0,markup='';subjects.forEach((subject,si)=>exams.forEach((exam,ei)=>{const v=matrix.get(subject+'|'+exam)[ci],x=start+n*(barW+gap),yy=y(v),opacity=Math.max(.32,1-ei*.18);markup+='<rect x="'+x+'" y="'+yy+'" width="'+barW+'" height="'+(top+plotH-yy)+'" rx="1.5" fill="'+colors[si%colors.length]+'" opacity="'+opacity+'"><title>'+escapeHtml(subject)+' · '+escapeHtml(exam)+' · '+category+' '+v+'명</title></rect>';n++;}));return markup+'<text x="'+(left+ci*groupW+groupW/2)+'" y="'+(H-42)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" font-weight="700" fill="#315667">'+category+'</text>';}).join('');
  const subjectLegend=subjects.map((s,i)=>'<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px"><i style="width:11px;height:11px;border-radius:2px;background:'+colors[i%colors.length]+'"></i>'+escapeHtml(s)+'</span>').join('');
  const examLegend=exams.map((e,i)=>'<span>'+escapeHtml(e)+(i===0?' 진하게':'')+'</span>').join(' · ');
  return '<section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>전 과목 시험별 등급 인원 통합 비교</h3><p>등급마다 과목별·시험별 막대를 쌓지 않고 각각 나란히 표시합니다.</p></div><span>'+subjects.length+'과목 · '+exams.length+'회</span></div><div style="padding:16px;overflow-x:auto"><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:14px;margin-bottom:6px;font-weight:700">'+subjectLegend+'</div><small>'+examLegend+'</small><svg viewBox="0 0 '+W+' '+H+'" style="width:100%;min-width:1050px">'+grid+bars+'</svg></div></section>';
}
function mockTrendStatisticsMarkup(classNo){
  const rows=scoreStatisticsRows("mock","",classNo);
  const exams=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b)||String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko"));
  const preferred=["국어","수학","영어","한국사"];
  const subjects=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{const ai=preferred.indexOf(a),bi=preferred.indexOf(b);return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi)||String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko");});
  if(!scoreStatisticsTrendSubject||(scoreStatisticsTrendSubject!=="__all__"&&!subjects.includes(scoreStatisticsTrendSubject)))scoreStatisticsTrendSubject="__all__";
  const subject=scoreStatisticsTrendSubject;
  if(exams.length<2)return '<div class="query-empty"><b>비교할 모의고사가 부족합니다.</b><span>서로 다른 시험 자료가 2회 이상 연결되면 추이를 표시합니다.</span></div>';
  const renderSubject=subject=>{
  const grades=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>i+1),categories=[...grades.map(x=>x+'등급'),'1~2등급','1~4등급'];
  const counts=exams.map(exam=>{const basic=grades.map(grade=>rows.filter(row=>row.exam===exam&&row.subject===subject&&Number(row.level)===grade).length);return [...basic,basic[0]+basic[1],basic.slice(0,4).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)];});
  const colors=['#0b8a78','#2f80ed','#f2994a','#9b51e0','#eb5757','#219653'];
  const rawMax=Math.max(1,...counts.flat()),chartMax=Math.max(rawMax+4,Math.ceil(rawMax*1.12)),W=1120,H=380,left=52,right=22,top=38,bottom=72,plotW=W-left-right,plotH=H-top-bottom;
  const groupW=plotW/categories.length,gap=Math.max(2,Math.min(7,groupW*.05)),barW=Math.max(5,Math.min(28,(groupW-gap*(exams.length+1))/exams.length));
  const y=v=>top+plotH-(v/chartMax)*plotH;
  const grid=Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const value=Math.round(chartMax*(4-i)/4),yy=top+plotH*i/4;return '<line x1="'+left+'" y1="'+yy+'" x2="'+(W-right)+'" y2="'+yy+'" stroke="#dfe9ec"/><text x="'+(left-9)+'" y="'+(yy+4)+'" text-anchor="end" font-size="11" fill="#718690">'+value+'</text>';}).join('');
  const bars=categories.map((category,gi)=>{const used=barW*exams.length+gap*(exams.length-1),start=left+gi*groupW+(groupW-used)/2;return exams.map((exam,ei)=>{const v=counts[ei][gi],x=start+ei*(barW+gap),yy=y(v),h=top+plotH-yy,color=colors[ei%colors.length];return '<rect x="'+x+'" y="'+yy+'" width="'+barW+'" height="'+h+'" rx="3" fill="'+color+'"><title>'+escapeHtml(exam)+' · '+category+' '+v+'명</title></rect><text x="'+(x+barW/2)+'" y="'+Math.max(top+10,yy-5)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" font-weight="700" fill="'+color+'">'+v+'</text>';}).join('')+'<text x="'+(left+gi*groupW+groupW/2)+'" y="'+(H-27)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="12" font-weight="800" fill="#315667">'+category+'</text>';}).join('');
  const legend=exams.map((exam,i)=>'<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-weight:800"><i style="width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:3px;background:'+colors[i%colors.length]+'"></i>'+escapeHtml(exam)+'</span>').join('');
  const tableHead='<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:140px repeat(11,minmax(70px,1fr));background:#28566f;color:white;font-weight:800"><span style="padding:11px">시험</span>'+categories.map(c=>'<span style="padding:11px;text-align:center">'+c+'</span>').join('')+'</div>';
  const tableRows=exams.map((exam,i)=>'<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:140px repeat(11,minmax(70px,1fr));border-top:1px solid #e2ebee"><b style="padding:11px;color:'+colors[i%colors.length]+'">'+escapeHtml(exam)+'</b>'+counts[i].map(v=>'<span style="padding:11px;text-align:center">'+v+'명</span>').join('')+'</div>').join('');
  return '<section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>'+escapeHtml(subject)+' 시험별 등급 분포 비교</h3><p>X축은 1~9등급과 누적 1~2·1~4등급, Y축은 인원입니다. 각 항목에서 시험별 막대를 나란히 비교합니다.</p></div><span>'+exams.length+'회</span></div><div style="padding:16px;overflow-x:auto"><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:16px;margin:0 0 8px">'+legend+'</div><svg viewBox="0 0 '+W+' '+H+'" style="width:100%;min-width:980px">'+grid+bars+'</svg><div style="min-width:940px">'+tableHead+tableRows+'</div></div></section>';
  };
  const visibleSubjects=subject==="__all__"?subjects:[subject];
  const detail=visibleSubjects.map(renderSubject).join('')||'<div class="query-empty"><b>표시할 과목 자료가 없습니다.</b></div>';
  return (subject==='__all__'?mockTrendOverviewMarkup(rows,exams,subjects):'')+detail;

  if(!rows.length)return '<div class="query-empty"><b>모의고사 자료가 없습니다.</b></div>';
  const head='<div class="score-grade-head"><span>과목·시험</span>'+Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>'<span>'+(i+1)+'등급</span>').join('')+'<span>1~2</span><span>1~4</span></div>';
  const body=subjects.map(subject=>'<section class="mock-compare-subject"><h4>'+escapeHtml(subject)+'</h4>'+exams.map(exam=>{const scope=rows.filter(row=>row.subject===subject&&row.exam===exam),counts=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>scope.filter(row=>Number(row.level)===i+1).length),values=[...counts,counts[0]+counts[1],counts.slice(0,4).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)];return '<div class="score-grade-row mock-compare-row"><b>'+escapeHtml(exam)+'</b>'+values.slice(0,9).map((v,i)=>'<span><small>'+(i+1)+'등급</small><strong>'+v+'</strong></span>').join('')+'<em><small>1~2등급</small><strong>'+values[9]+'</strong></em><em><small>1~4등급</small><strong>'+values[10]+'</strong></em></div>';}).join('')+'</section>').join('');
  return '<section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>전체 모의고사 과목별 등급 비교</h3><p>왼쪽은 과목, 과목 안의 행은 3월·6월·9월·11월 시험이며 위쪽 등급별 인원을 동시에 비교합니다.</p></div><span>'+exams.length+'회</span></div>'+head+body+'</section>';
}

function mockTrendOverviewMarkup(rows,exams,subjects){
  const grades=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>i+1),categories=[...grades.map(x=>x+'등급'),'1~2','1~4'];
  const values=(subject,exam)=>{const basic=grades.map(grade=>rows.filter(row=>row.subject===subject&&row.exam===exam&&Number(row.level)===grade).length);return[...basic,basic[0]+basic[1],basic.slice(0,4).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)];};
  const matrix=new Map(subjects.flatMap(subject=>exams.map(exam=>[subject+'|'+exam,values(subject,exam)])));
  const max=Math.max(1,...[...matrix.values()].flat()),chartMax=Math.max(max+4,Math.ceil(max*1.12)),W=1180,H=410,left=52,right=18,top=42,bottom=86,plotW=W-left-right,plotH=H-top-bottom,groupW=plotW/categories.length;
  const colors=['#0b8a78','#2f80ed','#f2994a','#9b51e0','#eb5757','#219653'],pairCount=Math.max(1,subjects.length*exams.length),gap=1,barW=Math.max(2,Math.min(8,(groupW-10-gap*(pairCount-1))/pairCount)),y=v=>top+plotH-(v/chartMax)*plotH;
  const grid=Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const value=Math.round(chartMax*(4-i)/4),yy=top+plotH*i/4;return '<line x1="'+left+'" y1="'+yy+'" x2="'+(W-right)+'" y2="'+yy+'" stroke="#dfe9ec"/><text x="'+(left-8)+'" y="'+(yy+4)+'" text-anchor="end" font-size="11" fill="#718690">'+value+'</text>';}).join('');
  const bars=categories.map((category,ci)=>{const used=barW*pairCount+gap*(pairCount-1),start=left+ci*groupW+(groupW-used)/2;let n=0,markup='';subjects.forEach((subject,si)=>exams.forEach((exam,ei)=>{const v=matrix.get(subject+'|'+exam)[ci],x=start+n*(barW+gap),yy=y(v),opacity=Math.max(.32,1-ei*.18);markup+='<rect x="'+x+'" y="'+yy+'" width="'+barW+'" height="'+(top+plotH-yy)+'" rx="1.5" fill="'+colors[si%colors.length]+'" opacity="'+opacity+'"><title>'+escapeHtml(subject)+' · '+escapeHtml(exam)+' · '+category+' '+v+'명</title></rect>';n++;}));return markup+'<text x="'+(left+ci*groupW+groupW/2)+'" y="'+(H-42)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" font-weight="700" fill="#315667">'+category+'</text>';}).join('');
  const subjectLegend=subjects.map((s,i)=>'<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px"><i style="width:11px;height:11px;border-radius:2px;background:'+colors[i%colors.length]+'"></i>'+escapeHtml(s)+'</span>').join('');
  const examLegend=exams.map((e,i)=>'<span>'+escapeHtml(e)+(i===0?' 진하게':'')+'</span>').join(' · ');
  return '<section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>전 과목 시험별 등급 인원 통합 비교</h3><p>등급마다 과목별·시험별 막대를 쌓지 않고 각각 나란히 표시합니다.</p></div><span>'+subjects.length+'과목 · '+exams.length+'회</span></div><div style="padding:16px;overflow-x:auto"><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:14px;margin-bottom:6px;font-weight:700">'+subjectLegend+'</div><small>'+examLegend+'</small><svg viewBox="0 0 '+W+' '+H+'" style="width:100%;min-width:1050px">'+grid+bars+'</svg></div></section>';
}
function mockTrendStatisticsMarkup(classNo){
  const rows=scoreStatisticsRows("mock","",classNo);
  const exams=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b)||String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko"));
  const preferred=["국어","수학","영어","한국사"];
  const subjects=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{const ai=preferred.indexOf(a),bi=preferred.indexOf(b);return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi)||String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko");});
  if(!scoreStatisticsTrendSubject||(scoreStatisticsTrendSubject!=="__all__"&&!subjects.includes(scoreStatisticsTrendSubject)))scoreStatisticsTrendSubject="__all__";
  const subject=scoreStatisticsTrendSubject;
  if(exams.length<2)return '<div class="query-empty"><b>비교할 모의고사가 부족합니다.</b><span>서로 다른 시험 자료가 2회 이상 연결되면 추이를 표시합니다.</span></div>';
  const renderSubject=subject=>{
  const grades=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>i+1),categories=[...grades.map(x=>x+'등급'),'1~2등급','1~4등급'];
  const counts=exams.map(exam=>{const basic=grades.map(grade=>rows.filter(row=>row.exam===exam&&row.subject===subject&&Number(row.level)===grade).length);return [...basic,basic[0]+basic[1],basic.slice(0,4).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)];});
  const colors=['#0b8a78','#2f80ed','#f2994a','#9b51e0','#eb5757','#219653'];
  const rawMax=Math.max(1,...counts.flat()),chartMax=Math.max(rawMax+4,Math.ceil(rawMax*1.12)),W=1120,H=380,left=52,right=22,top=38,bottom=72,plotW=W-left-right,plotH=H-top-bottom;
  const groupW=plotW/categories.length,gap=Math.max(2,Math.min(7,groupW*.05)),barW=Math.max(5,Math.min(28,(groupW-gap*(exams.length+1))/exams.length));
  const y=v=>top+plotH-(v/chartMax)*plotH;
  const grid=Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const value=Math.round(chartMax*(4-i)/4),yy=top+plotH*i/4;return '<line x1="'+left+'" y1="'+yy+'" x2="'+(W-right)+'" y2="'+yy+'" stroke="#dfe9ec"/><text x="'+(left-9)+'" y="'+(yy+4)+'" text-anchor="end" font-size="11" fill="#718690">'+value+'</text>';}).join('');
  const bars=categories.map((category,gi)=>{const used=barW*exams.length+gap*(exams.length-1),start=left+gi*groupW+(groupW-used)/2;return exams.map((exam,ei)=>{const v=counts[ei][gi],x=start+ei*(barW+gap),yy=y(v),h=top+plotH-yy,color=colors[ei%colors.length];return '<rect x="'+x+'" y="'+yy+'" width="'+barW+'" height="'+h+'" rx="3" fill="'+color+'"><title>'+escapeHtml(exam)+' · '+category+' '+v+'명</title></rect><text x="'+(x+barW/2)+'" y="'+Math.max(top+10,yy-5)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" font-weight="700" fill="'+color+'">'+v+'</text>';}).join('')+'<text x="'+(left+gi*groupW+groupW/2)+'" y="'+(H-27)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="12" font-weight="800" fill="#315667">'+category+'</text>';}).join('');
  const legend=exams.map((exam,i)=>'<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-weight:800"><i style="width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:3px;background:'+colors[i%colors.length]+'"></i>'+escapeHtml(exam)+'</span>').join('');
  const tableHead='<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:140px repeat(11,minmax(70px,1fr));background:#28566f;color:white;font-weight:800"><span style="padding:11px">시험</span>'+categories.map(c=>'<span style="padding:11px;text-align:center">'+c+'</span>').join('')+'</div>';
  const tableRows=exams.map((exam,i)=>'<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:140px repeat(11,minmax(70px,1fr));border-top:1px solid #e2ebee"><b style="padding:11px;color:'+colors[i%colors.length]+'">'+escapeHtml(exam)+'</b>'+counts[i].map(v=>'<span style="padding:11px;text-align:center">'+v+'명</span>').join('')+'</div>').join('');
  return '<section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>'+escapeHtml(subject)+' 시험별 등급 분포 비교</h3><p>X축은 1~9등급과 누적 1~2·1~4등급, Y축은 인원입니다. 각 항목에서 시험별 막대를 나란히 비교합니다.</p></div><span>'+exams.length+'회</span></div><div style="padding:16px;overflow-x:auto"><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:16px;margin:0 0 8px">'+legend+'</div><svg viewBox="0 0 '+W+' '+H+'" style="width:100%;min-width:980px">'+grid+bars+'</svg><div style="min-width:940px">'+tableHead+tableRows+'</div></div></section>';
  };
  const visibleSubjects=subject==="__all__"?subjects:[subject];
  const detail=visibleSubjects.map(renderSubject).join('')||'<div class="query-empty"><b>표시할 과목 자료가 없습니다.</b></div>';
  return (subject==='__all__'?mockTrendOverviewMarkup(rows,exams,subjects):'')+detail;
}

  const examLegend=exams.map((e,i)=>'<span>'+escapeHtml(e)+(i===0?' 진하게':'')+'</span>').join(' · ');

}

  const rows=scoreStatisticsRows("mock","",classNo);
  const exams=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b)||String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko"));
  const preferred=["국어","수학","영어","한국사"];

  if(!scoreStatisticsTrendSubject||(scoreStatisticsTrendSubject!=="__all__"&&!subjects.includes(scoreStatisticsTrendSubject)))scoreStatisticsTrendSubject="__all__";
  const subject=scoreStatisticsTrendSubject;
  if(exams.length<2)return '<div class="query-empty"><b>비교할 모의고사가 부족합니다.</b><span>서로 다른 시험 자료가 2회 이상 연결되면 추이를 표시합니다.</span></div>';
  const renderSubject=subject=>{
  const grades=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>i+1),categories=[...grades.map(x=>x+'등급'),'1~2등급','1~4등급'];
  const counts=exams.map(exam=>{const basic=grades.map(grade=>rows.filter(row=>row.exam===exam&&row.subject===subject&&Number(row.level)===grade).length);return [...basic,basic[0]+basic[1],basic.slice(0,4).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)];});
  const colors=['#0b8a78','#2f80ed','#f2994a','#9b51e0','#eb5757','#219653'];
  const rawMax=Math.max(1,...counts.flat()),chartMax=Math.max(rawMax+4,Math.ceil(rawMax*1.12)),W=1120,H=380,left=52,right=22,top=38,bottom=72,plotW=W-left-right,plotH=H-top-bottom;
  const groupW=plotW/categories.length,gap=Math.max(2,Math.min(7,groupW*.05)),barW=Math.max(5,Math.min(28,(groupW-gap*(exams.length+1))/exams.length));

  const bars=categories.map((category,gi)=>{const used=barW*exams.length+gap*(exams.length-1),start=left+gi*groupW+(groupW-used)/2;return exams.map((exam,ei)=>{const v=counts[ei][gi],x=start+ei*(barW+gap),yy=y(v),h=top+plotH-yy,color=colors[ei%colors.length];return '<rect x="'+x+'" y="'+yy+'" width="'+barW+'" height="'+h+'" rx="3" fill="'+color+'"><title>'+escapeHtml(exam)+' · '+category+' '+v+'명</title></rect><text x="'+(x+barW/2)+'" y="'+Math.max(top+10,yy-5)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" font-weight="700" fill="'+color+'">'+v+'</text>';}).join('')+'<text x="'+(left+gi*groupW+groupW/2)+'" y="'+(H-27)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="12" font-weight="800" fill="#315667">'+category+'</text>';}).join('');
  const legend=exams.map((exam,i)=>'<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-weight:800"><i style="width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:3px;background:'+colors[i%colors.length]+'"></i>'+escapeHtml(exam)+'</span>').join('');
  const tableHead='<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:140px repeat(11,minmax(70px,1fr));background:#28566f;color:white;font-weight:800"><span style="padding:11px">시험</span>'+categories.map(c=>'<span style="padding:11px;text-align:center">'+c+'</span>').join('')+'</div>';

  };
  const visibleSubjects=subject==="__all__"?subjects:[subject];
  const detail=visibleSubjects.map(renderSubject).join('')||'<div class="query-empty"><b>표시할 과목 자료가 없습니다.</b></div>';
  return (subject==='__all__'?mockTrendOverviewMarkup(rows,exams,subjects):'')+detail;
}
function internalNineGradeCut(percentile){
  if(percentile<=4)return 1;
  if(percentile<=11)return 2;
  if(percentile<=23)return 3;
  if(percentile<=40)return 4;
  if(percentile<=60)return 5;
  if(percentile<=77)return 6;
  if(percentile<=89)return 7;
  if(percentile<=96)return 8;
  return 9;
}
function internalAchievementSummary(items){
  const achievements=items
    .map(row=>({subject:String(row.subject||"").replace(/\d+$/,"").trim(),achievement:String(row.achievement||row.performance||row.level||"").trim()}))
    .filter(item=>item.subject&&item.achievement&&/^[A-E]$/i.test(item.achievement));
  if(!achievements.length)return {summary:"-",detail:""};
  const unique=[...new Map(achievements.map(item=>[`${item.subject}|${item.achievement.toUpperCase()}`,{subject:item.subject,achievement:item.achievement.toUpperCase()}])).values()];
  const detail=unique.map(item=>`${item.subject} ${item.achievement}`).join(" · ");
  return {summary:detail,detail};
-----
  return '<section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>전 과목 시험별 등급 인원 통합 비교</h3><p>등급마다 과목별·시험별 막대를 쌓지 않고 각각 나란히 표시합니다.</p></div><span>'+subjects.length+'과목 · '+exams.length+'회</span></div><div style="padding:16px;overflow-x:auto"><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:14px;margin-bottom:6px;font-weight:700">'+subjectLegend+'</div><small>'+examLegend+'</small><svg viewBox="0 0 '+W+' '+H+'" style="width:100%;min-width:1050px">'+grid+bars+'</svg></div></section>';
}

  return '<section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>'+escapeHtml(subject)+' 시험별 등급 분포 비교</h3><p>X축은 1~9등급과 누적 1~2·1~4등급, Y축은 인원입니다. 각 항목에서 시험별 막대를 나란히 비교합니다.</p></div><span>'+exams.length+'회</span></div><div style="padding:16px;overflow-x:auto"><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:16px;margin:0 0 8px">'+legend+'</div><svg viewBox="0 0 '+W+' '+H+'" style="width:100%;min-width:980px">'+grid+bars+'</svg><div style="min-width:940px">'+tableHead+tableRows+'</div></div></section>';
  };
  const visibleSubjects=subject==="__all__"?subjects:[subject];
  const detail=visibleSubjects.map(renderSubject).join('')||'<div class="query-empty"><b>표시할 과목 자료가 없습니다.</b></div>';
  return (subject==='__all__'?mockTrendOverviewMarkup(rows,exams,subjects):'')+detail;
}
function internalNineGradeCut(percentile){
  if(percentile<=4)return 1;
  if(percentile<=11)return 2;
  if(percentile<=23)return 3;
  if(percentile<=40)return 4;
  if(percentile<=60)return 5;
  if(percentile<=77)return 6;
  if(percentile<=89)return 7;
  if(percentile<=96)return 8;
  return 9;
}
function internalAchievementSummary(items){
  const achievements=items
    .map(row=>({subject:String(row.subject||"").replace(/\d+$/,"").trim(),achievement:String(row.achievement||row.performance||row.level||"").trim()}))
    .filter(item=>item.subject&&item.achievement&&/^[A-E]$/i.test(item.achievement));
  if(!achievements.length)return {summary:"-",detail:""};
  const unique=[...new Map(achievements.map(item=>[`${item.subject}|${item.achievement.toUpperCase()}`,{subject:item.subject,achievement:item.achievement.toUpperCase()}])).values()];
  const detail=unique.map(item=>`${item.subject} ${item.achievement}`).join(" · ");
  return {summary:detail,detail};
}

function scoreStatisticsView(){
  const rows=readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[];
  const isTrend=scoreStatisticsType==="mocktrend",isCompare=scoreStatisticsType==="mockcompare", wanted=scoreStatisticsType==="internal"?"내신":"모의고사";
  const exams=[...new Set(rows.filter(row=>row.scoreType===wanted).map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b)||String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko"));
  const subjects=[...new Set(rows.filter(row=>row.scoreType==="모의고사").map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko"));
  if(!scoreStatisticsExam||!exams.includes(scoreStatisticsExam))scoreStatisticsExam=exams.at(-1)||"";
  if(!scoreStatisticsTrendSubject||(scoreStatisticsTrendSubject!=="__all__"&&!subjects.includes(scoreStatisticsTrendSubject)))scoreStatisticsTrendSubject="__all__";
  const selector=isTrend?`<label>과목<select id="scoreStatisticsTrendSubject"><option value="__all__" ${scoreStatisticsTrendSubject==="__all__"?"selected":""}>전체 과목</option>${subjects.map(subject=>`<option ${subject===scoreStatisticsTrendSubject?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(subject)}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>`:isCompare?'':`<label>시험<select id="scoreStatisticsExam">${exams.map(exam=>`<option ${exam===scoreStatisticsExam?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(exam)}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>`;
  const result=isTrend?mockTrendStatisticsMarkup(scoreStatisticsClass):isCompare?mockAllExamComparisonMarkup(scoreStatisticsClass):(scoreStatisticsType==="mock"?mockStatisticsMarkup(scoreStatisticsExam,scoreStatisticsClass):internalStatisticsMarkup(scoreStatisticsExam,scoreStatisticsClass));
  const label=isTrend?"시험별 추이":isCompare?"전체 시험 비교":scoreStatisticsType==="mock"?"모의고사 통계":"내신 통계";
  return `<div class="score-statistics-shell"><div class="score-stat-controls"><div class="score-stat-type"><button data-score-stat-type="mock" class="${scoreStatisticsType==="mock"?"active":""}">모의고사 통계</button><button data-score-stat-type="mockcompare" class="${isCompare?"active":""}">전체 시험 비교</button><button data-score-stat-type="mocktrend" class="${isTrend?"active":""}">시험별 추이</button><button data-score-stat-type="internal" class="${scoreStatisticsType==="internal"?"active":""}">내신 통계</button></div>${selector}<label>범위<select id="scoreStatisticsClass"><option value="all">1학년 전체</option>${availableClasses().map(no=>`<option value="${no}" ${scoreStatisticsClass===no?"selected":""}>${no}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label><button class="btn primary" data-score-stat-run>통계 조회</button></div><div class="score-stat-guide"><b>${label}</b><span>기존 시험별 분포·랭킹은 유지하며, 추이 화면은 모든 모의고사를 시간순으로 비교합니다.</span></div><div id="scoreStatisticsResult">${result}</div></div>`;
}

function fiveGradeBoundaryEnrollment() {
  const internal = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => row.scoreType === "내신");
  const counts = new Map();
  internal.forEach(row => {
-----
}
function fiveGradeBoundaryData(enrollment) {
  const total = Math.max(1, Math.round(Number(enrollment) || 1));
  const cumulativeRates = [0.10, 0.34, 0.66, 0.90, 1];
  const cumulative = cumulativeRates.map(rate => Math.round(total * rate));
  cumulative[cumulative.length - 1] = total;
  const counts = cumulative.map((value, index) => value - (index ? cumulative[index - 1] : 0));
  return { total, cumulative, counts };
}
function fiveGradeBoundaryMarkup() {
  if (scoreMode === "mock") return "";
  const data = fiveGradeBoundaryData(fiveGradeBoundaryEnrollment());
  const grades = data.counts.map((count, index) => `<span><small>${index + 1}등급</small><b>${count}명</b><em>${index ? data.cumulative[index - 1] + 1 : 1}~${data.cumulative[index]}등</em></span>`).join("");
  return `<details class="five-grade-boundary"><summary><div><b>5등급 경계 보정</b><span>수강자 ${data.total}명 기준 · 1등급 ${data.cumulative[0]}등까지</span></div><em>기준 보기</em></summary><div class="five-grade-boundary-body"><div class="five-grade-boundary-control"><label>수강자 수<input id="fiveGradeEnrollment" type="number" min="1" max="999" value="${data.total}"></label><button class="btn secondary" id="fiveGradeApply" type="button">경계 다시 계산</button><p><b>나이스 등급을 본 정보로 사용</b><span>아래 경계표는 수강자 수에 따른 확인용 보조정보입니다. 과목별 수강자 수가 다르면 해당 인원으로 다시 계산하세요.</span></p></div><div class="five-grade-boundary-grid" id="fiveGradeBoundaryGrid">${grades}</div><small>누적 비율 10%·34%·66%·90%에 수강자 수를 곱한 뒤 반올림하여 경계를 산출합니다.</small></div></details>`;
}

function scoresView() {
  const classes = availableClasses();
  const periods = availableScorePeriods(scoreMode);
  if (scorePeriod !== "all" && !periods.includes(scorePeriod)) scorePeriod = "all";
  const modeRows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row=>scoreMode==="combined" || row.scoreType===(scoreMode==="mock"?"모의고사":"내신")).filter(row=>scorePeriod==="all"||scorePeriodKey(row)===scorePeriod);
  const exams = [...new Set(modeRows.map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b)||a.localeCompare(b,"ko"));
  const label={internal:"내신",mock:"모의고사",combined:"종합",statistics:"통계"}[scoreMode];
  const tabs=`<div class="module-tabs"><button data-score-mode="internal" class="${scoreMode==="internal"?"active":""}">내신</button><button data-score-mode="mock" class="${scoreMode==="mock"?"active":""}">모의고사</button><button data-score-mode="combined" class="${scoreMode==="combined"?"active":""}">종합성적</button><button data-score-mode="statistics" class="${scoreMode==="statistics"?"active":""}">통계</button></div>`;
  if(scoreMode==="statistics")return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-blue"><span>02</span><div><h3>성적 통계</h3><p>시험별 등급 분포와 학생 랭킹을 조회합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}${scoreStatisticsView()}</div>`;
  const guide = scoreMode === "combined" ? "개인별 조회 시 최신 요약과 전체 누적 성적을 함께 표시합니다." : "전체 누적 또는 학기별로 범위를 바꿔 조회합니다.";
  const periodTabs = `<div class="score-period-tabs"><button data-score-period="all" class="${scorePeriod==="all"?"active":""}">전체 누적</button>${periods.map(key=>`<button data-score-period="${escapeHtml(key)}" class="${scorePeriod===key?"active":""}">${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(key))}</button>`).join("")}</div>`;
  const gradeBoundary = fiveGradeBoundaryMarkup();
  return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-blue"><span>01</span><div><h3>성적 조회</h3><p>내신·모의고사·종합을 나누어 조회합니다. ${guide}</p></div></div>${tabs}${periodTabs}${gradeBoundary}<div class="query-panel"><label>조회단위<select class="filter" id="scoreScope">${scoreMode==="combined"?`<option value="individual" selected>개인별</option>`:`<option value="class">반별</option><option value="individual">개인별</option>`}</select></label><label>반<select class="filter" id="scoreClass"><option value="">반 선택</option>${classes.map(c=>`<option value="${c}">${c}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label><label id="scoreStudentLabel" class="${scoreMode==="combined"?"":"hidden"}">학생<select class="filter" id="scoreStudent"><option value="">학생 선택</option></select></label><label class="${scoreMode==="combined"?"score-exam-muted":""}">시험<select class="filter" id="scoreExam" ${scoreMode==="combined"?"disabled":""}><option value="">${scoreMode==="combined"?"선택 범위 전체 자동 통합":`${label} 전체`}</option>${exams.map(x=>`<option>${escapeHtml(x)}</option>`).join("")}</select></label><button class="btn primary" id="scoreQueryButton">조회</button><button class="btn secondary" id="scoreCounselButton">상담 기록</button><button class="btn secondary" id="scoreQueryReset">초기화</button></div><div id="scoreQueryResult"><div class="query-empty"><span>⌁</span><b>${label} 성적 조회 대상을 선택하세요.</b><small>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(scorePeriod))} 기준 · 조회 버튼을 눌렀을 때만 표시됩니다.</small></div></div></div>`;
}

function actualNineGrade(row) {
  let rank = Number(String(row?.rank ?? "").replace(/[^0-9.]/g,""));
  let enrollment = Number(String(row?.enrollment ?? "").replace(/[^0-9.]/g,""));
  let derived=false;
  if((!Number.isFinite(rank)||rank<=0)&&Number.isFinite(scoreNumeric(row?.rawScore))){
    const raw=scoreNumeric(row.rawScore);
    const peers=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(peer=>
      peer?.scoreType==="내신"&&String(peer.exam||"")===String(row?.exam||"")&&String(peer.subject||"")===String(row?.subject||"")&&
      String(peer.year||"")===String(row?.year||"")&&String(peer.semester||"")===String(row?.semester||"")&&Number.isFinite(scoreNumeric(peer.rawScore))
    );
    if(peers.length){
      rank=peers.filter(peer=>scoreNumeric(peer.rawScore)>raw).length+1;
      if(!Number.isFinite(enrollment)||enrollment<=0)enrollment=peers.length;
      derived=true;
    }
  }
  if (!Number.isFinite(rank) || !Number.isFinite(enrollment) || rank <= 0 || enrollment <= 0) return null;

function fiveGradeBoundaryData(enrollment) {
  const total = Math.max(1, Math.round(Number(enrollment) || 1));
  const cumulativeRates = [0.10, 0.34, 0.66, 0.90, 1];
  const cumulative = cumulativeRates.map(rate => Math.round(total * rate));
  cumulative[cumulative.length - 1] = total;
  const counts = cumulative.map((value, index) => value - (index ? cumulative[index - 1] : 0));
  return { total, cumulative, counts };
}
function fiveGradeBoundaryMarkup() {
  if (scoreMode === "mock") return "";
  const data = fiveGradeBoundaryData(fiveGradeBoundaryEnrollment());
  const grades = data.counts.map((count, index) => `<span><small>${index + 1}등급</small><b>${count}명</b><em>${index ? data.cumulative[index - 1] + 1 : 1}~${data.cumulative[index]}등</em></span>`).join("");
  return `<details class="five-grade-boundary"><summary><div><b>5등급 경계 보정</b><span>수강자 ${data.total}명 기준 · 1등급 ${data.cumulative[0]}등까지</span></div><em>기준 보기</em></summary><div class="five-grade-boundary-body"><div class="five-grade-boundary-control"><label>수강자 수<input id="fiveGradeEnrollment" type="number" min="1" max="999" value="${data.total}"></label><button class="btn secondary" id="fiveGradeApply" type="button">경계 다시 계산</button><p><b>나이스 등급을 본 정보로 사용</b><span>아래 경계표는 수강자 수에 따른 확인용 보조정보입니다. 과목별 수강자 수가 다르면 해당 인원으로 다시 계산하세요.</span></p></div><div class="five-grade-boundary-grid" id="fiveGradeBoundaryGrid">${grades}</div><small>누적 비율 10%·34%·66%·90%에 수강자 수를 곱한 뒤 반올림하여 경계를 산출합니다.</small></div></details>`;
}

function scoresView() {
  const classes = availableClasses();
  const periods = availableScorePeriods(scoreMode);
  if (scorePeriod !== "all" && !periods.includes(scorePeriod)) scorePeriod = "all";
  const modeRows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row=>scoreMode==="combined" || row.scoreType===(scoreMode==="mock"?"모의고사":"내신")).filter(row=>scorePeriod==="all"||scorePeriodKey(row)===scorePeriod);
  const exams = [...new Set(modeRows.map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b)||a.localeCompare(b,"ko"));
  const label={internal:"내신",mock:"모의고사",combined:"종합",statistics:"통계"}[scoreMode];
  const tabs=`<div class="module-tabs"><button data-score-mode="internal" class="${scoreMode==="internal"?"active":""}">내신</button><button data-score-mode="mock" class="${scoreMode==="mock"?"active":""}">모의고사</button><button data-score-mode="combined" class="${scoreMode==="combined"?"active":""}">종합성적</button><button data-score-mode="statistics" class="${scoreMode==="statistics"?"active":""}">통계</button></div>`;
  if(scoreMode==="statistics")return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-blue"><span>02</span><div><h3>성적 통계</h3><p>시험별 등급 분포와 학생 랭킹을 조회합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}${scoreStatisticsView()}</div>`;
  const guide = scoreMode === "combined" ? "개인별 조회 시 최신 요약과 전체 누적 성적을 함께 표시합니다." : "전체 누적 또는 학기별로 범위를 바꿔 조회합니다.";
  const periodTabs = `<div class="score-period-tabs"><button data-score-period="all" class="${scorePeriod==="all"?"active":""}">전체 누적</button>${periods.map(key=>`<button data-score-period="${escapeHtml(key)}" class="${scorePeriod===key?"active":""}">${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(key))}</button>`).join("")}</div>`;
  const gradeBoundary = fiveGradeBoundaryMarkup();
  return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-blue"><span>01</span><div><h3>성적 조회</h3><p>내신·모의고사·종합을 나누어 조회합니다. ${guide}</p></div></div>${tabs}${periodTabs}${gradeBoundary}<div class="query-panel"><label>조회단위<select class="filter" id="scoreScope">${scoreMode==="combined"?`<option value="individual" selected>개인별</option>`:`<option value="class">반별</option><option value="individual">개인별</option>`}</select></label><label>반<select class="filter" id="scoreClass"><option value="">반 선택</option>${classes.map(c=>`<option value="${c}">${c}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label><label id="scoreStudentLabel" class="${scoreMode==="combined"?"":"hidden"}">학생<select class="filter" id="scoreStudent"><option value="">학생 선택</option></select></label><label class="${scoreMode==="combined"?"score-exam-muted":""}">시험<select class="filter" id="scoreExam" ${scoreMode==="combined"?"disabled":""}><option value="">${scoreMode==="combined"?"선택 범위 전체 자동 통합":`${label} 전체`}</option>${exams.map(x=>`<option>${escapeHtml(x)}</option>`).join("")}</select></label><button class="btn primary" id="scoreQueryButton">조회</button><button class="btn secondary" id="scoreCounselButton">상담 기록</button><button class="btn secondary" id="scoreQueryReset">초기화</button></div><div id="scoreQueryResult"><div class="query-empty"><span>⌁</span><b>${label} 성적 조회 대상을 선택하세요.</b><small>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(scorePeriod))} 기준 · 조회 버튼을 눌렀을 때만 표시됩니다.</small></div></div></div>`;
}

function filterRowsByCurrentTeacherScope(rows){
  const scope=currentTeacherAccessScope();
  if(!Array.isArray(rows))return [];
  if(!scope.grade&&!scope.classNo)return rows.slice();
  return rows.filter(row=>{
    const gc=recordGradeClass(row);
    if(scope.grade&&gc.grade&&gc.grade!==scope.grade)return false;
    if(scope.classNo&&gc.classNo&&String(Number(gc.classNo))!==String(Number(scope.classNo)))return false;
    return true;
  });
}
function dashboardGradeScope(){
  const profile=currentUserProfile();
  const scope=currentTeacherAccessScope();
  const grade=String(profile.grade||scope.grade||state?.settings?.neis?.grade||1);
  return {grade,label:`${grade}학년 전체`};
}

  const scope=dashboardGradeScope();
  if(!Array.isArray(rows))return [];
  return rows.filter(row=>{
    const gc=recordGradeClass(row);
    return !scope.grade||!gc.grade||String(gc.grade)===String(scope.grade);
  });
}

  return row?.[1]?String(Number(row[1])):'';
}
function resolveDashboardStudent(row){
  const students=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.students)?readonlyCache.students:[];
  const ids=[row?.studentId,row?.studentID,row?.id,row?.studentNo,row?.studentNumber].map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const name=String(row?.name||row?.studentName||row?.displayName||'').trim();
  const gc=recordGradeClass(row);
  return students.find(student=>{
    const studentIds=[student?.id,student?.studentId,student?.studentID,student?.studentNo,student?.studentNumber].map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if(ids.some(id=>studentIds.includes(id)))return true;
    if(name&&name===String(student?.name||student?.studentName||student?.displayName||'').trim()){
      const studentGc=recordGradeClass(student);
      return (!gc.grade||!studentGc.grade||gc.grade===studentGc.grade)&&(!gc.classNo||!studentGc.classNo||String(Number(gc.classNo))===String(Number(studentGc.classNo)));
    }
    return false;
  })||null;

  const gc=recordGradeClass(student||row);
  const classLabel=student?.className||row?.className||(gc.grade&&gc.classNo?`${gc.grade}-${Number(gc.classNo)}`:'');
  return {...row,studentRef:student,studentNo,name,classLabel};

function dormProgramActivityMarkup(student,termMode,bundle){
  const termStats=dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode),cohort=dormCohortAverage(termMode);
  const unified=studentActivityView(student);
  const allAfter=unified.afterPrograms||[];
  const night=allAfter.filter(p=>/야간심화|심화수업/.test(`${p.afterType||''} ${p.title||''}`));
  const after=allAfter.filter(p=>!night.includes(p));
  const uniqueTitles=rows=>[...new Set(rows.map(p=>String(p.actualTitle||p.title||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
  const attendance=(readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||[]).filter(r=>studentMatches(r,student));
  const programRows=termStats.programs.map(p=>{
    const report=p.report||resolveStudentProgramReport(p,student,termStats.reports);
    const submitted=Boolean(report&&report.submitted!==false);return `<li class="${submitted?'has-report':''}"><b>${escapeHtml(p.recordTitle||p.actualTitle||p.title||'프로그램')}</b>${submitted?`<button type="button" class="state positive dorm-report-open" data-dorm-report-id="${escapeHtml(report.id||'')}" data-dorm-report-student="${escapeHtml(student.id||'')}">보고서 제출 · 보기</button>`:`<span class="state warn">보고서 미제출</span>`}</li>`;
  }).join('');
  return `<div class="dorm-activity-grid"><article><h4>${escapeHtml(dormTermDefinition(termMode)?.label||'현재')} 프로그램·보고서</h4><div class="dorm-cohort-compare"><span><small>본인 프로그램</small><b>${termStats.programs.length}</b></span><span><small>학사생 평균</small><b>${cohort.programs.toFixed(1)}</b></span><span><small>본인 보고서율</small><b>${Math.round(termStats.reportRate)}%</b><em>${termStats.submitted}/${termStats.reportTargets?.length||0}</em></span><span><small>학사생 평균</small><b>${Math.round(cohort.reportRate)}%</b><em>대상 ${cohort.reportTargets.toFixed(1)}</em></span></div><ul>${programRows||'<li class="empty-inline">해당 학기 프로그램 자료 없음</li>'}</ul></article><article><h4>방과후·야간심화 근황</h4><p><b>방과후</b> ${escapeHtml(uniqueTitles(after).join(' · ')||'참여 기록 없음')}</p><p><b>야간심화</b> ${escapeHtml(uniqueTitles(night).join(' · ')||'참여 기록 없음')}</p><p><b>야자 출석기록</b> ${attendance.length}건</p></article></div>`;
}
function dormInseoulGap(student){
  const row=admissionManagementDataset('inseoul').find(x=>String(x.student?.id||'')===String(student.id||''));
  const standard=row?.standard||{internal:uepManagementRule('inseoul').internal,mockText:uepManagementRule('inseoul').mockText};
  const actual=row?.base?.actual9;
  const internalGap=actual==null?null:Math.max(0,(actual-Number(standard.internal))/Number(standard.internal)*100);
  const levels=(row?.mock?.mainLevels||[]).filter(Number.isFinite);
  const within3=levels.filter(v=>v<=3).length,within2=levels.filter(v=>v<=2).length;
  const mockScore=levels.length?Math.min(100,((Math.min(within3,3)+Math.min(within2,2))/5)*100):null;
  const mockGap=mockScore==null?null:100-mockScore;
  const overall=[internalGap,mockGap].filter(v=>v!=null).length?Math.max(...[internalGap,mockGap].filter(v=>v!=null)):null;
  const gaps=[];
  if(actual==null)gaps.push('내신 9등급 환산자료 확인 필요');
  else if(actual>standard.internal)gaps.push(`내신 ${actual.toFixed(2)} → 핵심기준 ${Number(standard.internal).toFixed(2)} 이내 보완`);
  else gaps.push(`내신 핵심기준 충족 (${actual.toFixed(2)})`);
  if(levels.length<3)gaps.push('국·수·영 모의고사 자료 보완');
  else{
    levels.forEach((v,i)=>{if(v>3)gaps.push(`${['국어','수학','영어'][i]||'주요영역'} ${v}등급 → 3등급 이내`);});
    if(within2<2)gaps.push(`국·수·영 2등급 이내 ${within2}개 → 2개 필요`);
    if(within3===3&&within2>=2)gaps.push('모의고사 인서울 핵심기준 충족');
  }
  return {row,standard,actual,internalGap,mockGap,overall,gaps};
}
function dormComprehensiveCounselMarkup(student,termMode,bundle){
  const gap=dormInseoulGap(student),career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle),stats=dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode),cohort=dormCohortAverage(termMode);
  const selected=(bundle.selected||[]).filter(r=>String(r.final||'Y')!=='N');
  const points=[...gap.gaps];
  if(!selected.length)points.push('희망계열에 맞는 선택과목 이수계획 확인 필요');
  if(stats.programs.length<cohort.programs)points.push(`계열 연계 프로그램: 본인 ${stats.programs.length}개 / 학사생 평균 ${cohort.programs.toFixed(1)}개`);
  if(stats.reportRate+1<cohort.reportRate)points.push(`보고서 제출률: 본인 ${Math.round(stats.reportRate)}% / 학사생 평균 ${Math.round(cohort.reportRate)}%`);
  points.push(`진로희망 ${career.hopeTrack} · ${career.hopeMajor}와 선택과목·탐구활동의 연속성 확인`);
  return `<section class="dorm-counsel-priority"><header><div><small>SEOUL · CORE GAP</small><h4>인서울 핵심관리 괴리 최우선</h4></div><strong>${gap.overall==null?'자료 확인':gap.overall<=0?'핵심기준 충족':`최대 괴리 ${gap.overall.toFixed(0)}%`}</strong></header><div class="dorm-gap-metrics"><span><small>내신 핵심기준</small><b>9등급 ${Number(gap.standard.internal).toFixed(2)} 이내</b></span><span><small>현재 내신</small><b>${gap.actual==null?'-':gap.actual.toFixed(2)}</b><em>${gap.internalGap==null?'자료 없음':gap.internalGap===0?'충족':`괴리 +${gap.internalGap.toFixed(0)}%`}</em></span><span><small>모의 핵심기준</small><b>국·수·영 2개≤2, 전체≤3</b></span><span><small>모의 충족 괴리</small><b>${gap.mockGap==null?'-':`${gap.mockGap.toFixed(0)}%`}</b></span></div><ol>${points.map(p=>`<li>${escapeHtml(p)}</li>`).join('')}</ol><p class="dorm-holistic-note"><b>학생부종합전형 관점</b> 성적만으로 순위를 매기지 않고 진로희망–선택과목–계열연계 프로그램–보고서 탐구의 연결성과 지속성을 함께 상담합니다.</p></section>`;
}
