function dormComprehensiveCounselMarkup(student,termMode,bundle){
  const gap=dormInseoulGap(student),career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle),stats=dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode),cohort=dormCohortAverage(termMode);
  const selected=(bundle.selected||[]).filter(r=>String(r.final||'Y')!=='N');
  const points=[...gap.gaps];
  if(!selected.length)points.push('희망계열에 맞는 선택과목 이수계획 확인 필요');
  if(stats.programs.length<cohort.programs)points.push(`계열 연계 프로그램: 본인 ${stats.programs.length}개 / 학사생 평균 ${cohort.programs.toFixed(1)}개`);
  if(stats.reportRate+1<cohort.reportRate)points.push(`보고서 제출률: 본인 ${Math.round(stats.reportRate)}% / 학사생 평균 ${Math.round(cohort.reportRate)}%`);
  points.push(`진로희망 ${career.hopeTrack} · ${career.hopeMajor}와 선택과목·탐구활동의 연속성 확인`);
  return `<section class="dorm-counsel-priority"><header><div><small>SEOUL · CORE GAP</small><h4>인서울 핵심관리 괴리 최우선</h4></div><strong>${gap.overall==null?'자료 확인':gap.overall<=0?'핵심기준 충족':`최대 괴리 ${gap.overall.toFixed(0)}%`}</strong></header><div class="dorm-gap-metrics"><span><small>내신 핵심기준</small><b>9등급 ${Number(gap.standard.internal).toFixed(2)} 이내</b></span><span><small>현재 내신</small><b>${gap.actual==null?'-':gap.actual.toFixed(2)}</b><em>${gap.internalGap==null?'자료 없음':gap.internalGap===0?'충족':`괴리 +${gap.internalGap.toFixed(0)}%`}</em></span><span><small>모의 핵심기준</small><b>국·수·영 2개≤2, 전체≤3</b></span><span><small>모의 충족 괴리</small><b>${gap.mockGap==null?'-':`${gap.mockGap.toFixed(0)}%`}</b></span></div><ol>${points.map(p=>`<li>${escapeHtml(p)}</li>`).join('')}</ol><p class="dorm-holistic-note"><b>학생부종합전형 관점</b> 성적만으로 순위를 매기지 않고 진로희망–선택과목–계열연계 프로그램–보고서 탐구의 연결성과 지속성을 함께 상담합니다.</p></section>`;
}

function dormSeoulUniversityRows(student){
  const seoul=/서울대|연세대|고려대|서강대|성균관대|한양대|중앙대|경희대|한국외대|서울시립대|건국대|동국대|홍익대|숙명여대|이화여대|광운대|국민대|숭실대|세종대|서울과기대/;
  const base=admissionBase(student.id), actual=base.actual9;
  const cuts=(readonlyCache?.admissionCuts||[]).filter(x=>seoul.test(String(x.university||''))&&Number.isFinite(Number(x.cut)));
  const grouped=new Map();
  cuts.forEach(row=>{const key=String(row.university||'').trim();if(!key)return;const diff=actual==null?null:actual-Number(row.cut);const old=grouped.get(key);if(!old||Math.abs(diff??99)<Math.abs(old.diff??99))grouped.set(key,{...row,diff});});
  return [...grouped.values()].sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(a.diff??99)-Math.abs(b.diff??99)).slice(0,9);
}
function dormUniversityFitMarkup(student,bundle){
  const rows=dormSeoulUniversityRows(student), career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle), gap=dormInseoulGap(student);
  const selectedCount=(bundle.selected||[]).filter(r=>String(r.final||'Y')!=='N').length;
  const holistic=selectedCount>=6?'적합':selectedCount>=3?'보완':'확인';
  const rules=readonlyCache?.admissionMinimums||[];
  const html=rows.map(row=>{const rule=rules.find(r=>String(r.university||'')===String(row.university||''));const latest=latestMockSnapshot(student.id), min=rule?minimumCheck(rule,admissionBase(student.id,latest.exam||'6월모의고사').mock):null;let state='자료확인';if(row.diff!=null)state=row.diff<=0?'가능권':row.diff<=.45?'경계':row.diff<=.9?'도전':'상향';const cls=state==='가능권'?'good':state==='경계'?'warn':state==='도전'?'warn':'bad';const internal=row.diff==null?'-':row.diff<=0?'충족':`+${row.diff.toFixed(2)}`;const mock=min?.result|| (gap.mockGap===0?'핵심충족':'보완');const point=row.diff==null?'입결 연결 확인':row.diff<=0?'학종 서류 연속성 강화':row.diff<=.45?'내신 경계 관리':row.diff<=.9?'내신·모의 동시 보완':'상향 지원 관찰';return `<div class="dorm-univ-row"><b>${escapeHtml(row.university)}</b><span class="fit ${cls}">${state}</span><span><small>내신</small>${escapeHtml(internal)}</span><span><small>모의/최저</small>${escapeHtml(mock)}</span><span><small>학종·계열</small>${holistic}</span><em>${escapeHtml(point)}</em></div>`;}).join('');
  return `<section class="dorm-university-fit"><header><div><small>SEOUL · UNIVERSITY FIT</small><h4>인서울 대학별 적합도</h4></div><span>${escapeHtml(career.hopeTrack)} · ${escapeHtml(career.hopeMajor)}</span></header><div class="dorm-univ-head"><span>대학</span><span>현재판정</span><span>내신</span><span>모의/최저</span><span>학종·계열</span><span>핵심 보완</span></div>${html||'<div class="empty-inline">연결된 인서울 대학 입결 자료가 없습니다.</div>'}<small class="dorm-univ-note">대학별 입결·수능최저 연결자료를 활용한 상담 참고값이며 합격 가능성을 확정하지 않습니다.</small></section>`;
}
function openDormReportOverlay(reportId,studentId){
  const student=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>String(s.id||'')===String(studentId||''));
  const report=findConnectedReport(reportId,student);if(!report)return toast('연결된 보고서 원문을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  document.querySelector('.dorm-report-overlay')?.remove();
  const layer=document.createElement('div');layer.className='dorm-report-overlay';
  layer.innerHTML=`<div class="dorm-report-dialog"><header><div><small>STUDENT REPORT · DORMITORY</small><h3>${escapeHtml(report.programName||report.reportGroup||'학생 보고서')}</h3></div><button type="button" data-dorm-report-close>×</button></header><div class="dorm-report-scroll">${reportPreviewMarkup(report,student)}</div></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(layer);layer.querySelector('[data-dorm-report-close]').onclick=()=>layer.remove();layer.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===layer)layer.remove();});
  layer.querySelector('#printCurrentReport')?.addEventListener('click',()=>printReportDocument(report,student));
  layer.querySelector('#excelCurrentReport')?.addEventListener('click',()=>exportReportExcel(report,student));
  layer.querySelectorAll('[data-report-tag-detail]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>openReportTagDetail(button.dataset.reportTagDetail));
}

}
function reportSubmissionLabel(report){
  const date=submissionDateText(report?.finalSubmittedAt||report?.submittedAt);
  const count=Math.max(1,Number(report?.submissionCount)||1);
  return `${date} (${count}차 제출)`;
}

function selectionComparisonsForStudent(student){
  return (readonlyCache?.selectionComparisons||[]).filter(x=>(x.studentId&&String(x.studentId)===String(student?.id))||(x.studentNo&&String(x.studentNo)===String(student?.studentNo)));
}
function selectionComparisonMarkup(student){
  const rows=selectionComparisonsForStudent(student);
  if(!rows.length)return `<section class="selection-compare-panel"><header><div><small>PRE-CONSULTATION ↔ MAIN APPLICATION</small><h3>사전상담 ↔ 본신청 비교</h3></div><span>비교자료 없음</span></header><p class="selection-compare-help">기존 Google Form 사전상담과 리로스쿨 본신청 자료가 함께 연결되면 학기별 변경 내용을 표시합니다.</p></section>`;
  const changed=rows.filter(x=>x.status!=='일치').length;
  return `<section class="selection-compare-panel"><header><div><small>PRE-CONSULTATION ↔ MAIN APPLICATION</small><h3>사전상담 ↔ 본신청 비교</h3><p>학생이 상담 단계에서 생각했던 선택과 실제 본신청이 어떻게 달라졌는지 확인합니다.</p></div><span>${changed?`변경 ${changed}개 학기`:'전체 일치'}</span></header><div class="selection-compare-grid">${rows.sort((a,b)=>curriculumTermOrder(a.term)-curriculumTermOrder(b.term)).map(x=>`<article class="selection-compare-card ${x.status==='일치'?'same':'changed'}"><div class="selection-compare-title"><b>${escapeHtml(x.term)}</b><em>${escapeHtml(x.status)}</em></div><div class="selection-compare-columns"><div><small>사전상담</small><p>${x.preSubjects.map(s=>`<span>${escapeHtml(s)}</span>`).join('')||'<i>없음</i>'}</p></div><div><small>본신청</small><p>${x.mainSubjects.map(s=>`<span>${escapeHtml(s)}</span>`).join('')||'<i>없음</i>'}</p></div></div>${x.status!=='일치'?`<div class="selection-change-summary">${x.removed.length?`<span class="removed">빠짐 · ${escapeHtml(x.removed.join(' · '))}</span>`:''}${x.added.length?`<span class="added">추가 · ${escapeHtml(x.added.join(' · '))}</span>`:''}</div>`:'<div class="selection-change-summary"><span class="same">사전상담과 본신청이 같습니다.</span></div>'}</article>`).join('')}</div></section>`;
}

function selectionErrorHistoryMarkup(student){
  const rows=(readonlyCache?.selectionErrorTransitions||[]).filter(x=>String(x.studentId||'')===String(student?.id||'')||String(x.studentNo||'')===String(student?.studentNo||''));
  if(!rows.length)return `<section class="selection-error-history"><header><div><small>VALIDATION HISTORY</small><h3>사전신청 오류 이력</h3></div><span>특이사항 없음</span></header><p>사전 Google Form 신청 당시 발견된 문·이과 혼합·과학 위계·학기간 중복 오류 이력입니다.</p></section>`;
  const bad=rows.filter(x=>/지속|신규|재검토/.test(x.status)).length;
  return `<section class="selection-error-history"><header><div><small>VALIDATION HISTORY</small><h3>사전신청 오류 이력</h3><p>상담 당시 오류가 실제 본신청에서 해결되었는지 집중 확인합니다.</p></div><span>${bad?`확인 필요 ${bad}건`:'모두 해결'}</span></header><div class="selection-error-history-grid">${rows.map(x=>`<article class="${/해결/.test(x.status)?'resolved':/본신청 전/.test(x.status)?'waiting':'warning'}"><b>${escapeHtml(x.category)}</b><em>${escapeHtml(x.status)}</em><p>${escapeHtml(x.mainMessage||x.preMessage||'세부내용 확인')}</p></article>`).join('')}</div></section>`;
}
function selectionSubmissionInfo(bundle){
  const rows=(bundle?.selected||[]).filter(x=>x.finalSubmittedAt||x.submittedAt||x.submissionCount||x.modifiedAfterNotice);
  const stamp=x=>{const raw=x.finalSubmittedAt||x.submittedAt||"";const serial=Number(raw);if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000)return Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+serial*86400000;const parsed=Date.parse(String(raw).replace(/오전/g,"AM").replace(/오후/g,"PM"));return Number.isFinite(parsed)?parsed:0;};
  const row=rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>stamp(b)-stamp(a)||(Number(b.submissionCount)||0)-(Number(a.submissionCount)||0))[0]||{};
  const count=Math.max(1,...rows.map(x=>Number(x.submissionCount)||1));
  const rawDate=row.finalSubmittedAt||row.submittedAt||"";
  const parsedText=submissionDateText(rawDate);
  const y=(new Date()).getFullYear();
  const m=String(parsedText).match(/(\d{4})[.\/-](\d{1,2})[.\/-](\d{1,2})/);
  const afterNotice=Boolean(m && `${m[1]}-${String(m[2]).padStart(2,"0")}-${String(m[3]).padStart(2,"0")}`>`${y}-08-03`);
  return {date:parsedText,count,modifiedAfterNotice:afterNotice||rows.some(x=>Boolean(x.modifiedAfterNotice))};
}
function outputReportRows(){
  const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
  const studentMap=new Map();
  students.forEach(s=>{studentMap.set(String(s.id),s);studentMap.set(String(s.studentNo),s);});
  return (readonlyCache?.programReports||[]).map(report=>{
    const student=studentMap.get(String(report.studentId||report.studentNo))||{};
    const rawClass=String(student.className||String(report.studentNo||"").slice(1,2)||"");
    const classNo=(rawClass.match(/[1-9]/)||[""])[0];
    return {...report,classNo,number:String(student.number||String(report.studentNo||"").slice(-2)||"").replace(/^0/,""),studentName:report.name||student.name||"",studentRef:student};
  }).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.classNo)-Number(b.classNo)||Number(a.number)-Number(b.number)||String(a.programName).localeCompare(String(b.programName)));
}

function printRecordPackage(student,type){
  if(!student)return;
  const bundle=studentRecordBundle(student);
  let body=`<header class="print-head"><small>UNHO EDUCATION PLATFORM · 생활기록부 교사용 자료</small><h1>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</h1><p>진로희망: ${escapeHtml(student.career||"-")}</p>${studentPrintBadgesMarkup(student)}</header>`;
  if(type==="evidence"){
    const selectedPrograms=bundle.programs.filter(p=>p.kind==="selected");
    const reportCommon=bundle.programs.filter(p=>p.kind==="common"&&(bundle.reports||[]).some(r=>String(r.programId||"")===String(p.programId||p.id).replace(/^common-/,"")));
    body+=`<section class="print-section"><h2>학생 활동현황</h2><table><thead><tr><th>일자</th><th>영역</th><th>활동</th><th>역할·근거</th></tr></thead><tbody>${bundle.activities.map(a=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(a.date||"")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(a.area||a.type||"")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(a.title||"")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(a.role||a.evidence||a.note||"")}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></section><section class="print-section"><h2>선택활동 참여 프로그램</h2>${selectedPrograms.map(p=>`<div class="record-box"><b>${escapeHtml(p.recordTitle||p.title)}</b> ${escapeHtml(recordDateText(p))}<br>${escapeHtml(p.memo||"")}</div>`).join("")||"자료 없음"}</section><section class="print-section"><h2>보고서가 있는 공통활동</h2>${reportCommon.map(p=>`<div class="record-box"><b>${escapeHtml(p.recordTitle||p.title)}</b> ${escapeHtml(recordDateText(p))}</div>`).join("")||"자료 없음"}</section><section class="print-section"><h2>SDGs 연계 성장 프로파일</h2>${bundle.programs.filter(p=>p.sdgs).map(p=>`<div class="record-box"><b>SDGs ${escapeHtml(p.sdgs)}</b> · ${escapeHtml(p.recordTitle||p.title)}<br>${escapeHtml(p.sdgsEvidence||"")}</div>`).join("")||"확정된 SDGs 자료 없음"}</section>`;
  }else{
    const areas=[["autonomy","자율활동 특기사항"],["career","진로활동 특기사항"],["behavior","종합의견(행동발달 특기사항)"]];
    body+=areas.map(([area,label])=>{const draft=localStorage.getItem(recordDraftKey(student.id,area))||"";const final=existingFinalRecord(bundle,area);return `<section class="print-section"><h2>${label}</h2><h3>초안 · ${draft.length}자</h3><div class="record-box draft">${escapeHtml(draft||"저장된 초안 없음")}</div><h3>최종본 · ${(final?.text||"").length}자</h3><div class="record-box final">${escapeHtml(final?.text||"확정된 최종본 없음")}</div></section>`}).join("");
  }
  openPrintDocument(`${student.studentNo}_${student.name}_생활기록부`,body);
}

      const base=admissionBase(student.id);

    }else{
      const all=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(r=>r.studentId===student.id);const internal=all.filter(r=>r.scoreType==='내신');const mock=all.filter(r=>r.scoreType==='모의고사');
      preview=`<div class="preview-student-head"><b>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</b><span>성적 종합</span></div><div class="preview-metrics"><span><small>내신 기록</small><b>${internal.length}</b></span><span><small>모의고사 기록</small><b>${mock.length}</b></span><span><small>진로희망</small><b>${escapeHtml(student.career||'-')}</b></span></div>`;
    }
  }
  return `${outputClassStudentPicker('반과 학생을 선택한 뒤 성적메뉴 또는 입시메뉴의 종합화면을 출력합니다.')}<div class="output-choice-cards two"><button class="${mode==='score-summary'?'active':''}" data-output-score-mode="score-summary"><b>성적 종합</b><small>성적 메뉴의 학생별 종합화면</small></button><button class="${mode==='admission-summary'?'active':''}" data-output-score-mode="admission-summary"><b>입시 종합</b><small>입시 메뉴의 학생별 종합화면</small></button></div>${student?outputPreviewShell(`${student.studentNo} ${student.name} · ${mode==='admission-summary'?'입시 종합':'성적 종합'}`,'출력 전 미리보기',preview,`<button class="btn primary" data-output-score-print>${mode==='admission-summary'?'입시 종합':'성적 종합'} 인쇄</button>`):''}`;
}
function printScorePackage(student,mode){
  if(!student)return;
  const all=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(r=>r.studentId===student.id);
  const internal=all.filter(r=>r.scoreType==="내신");
  const mock=all.filter(r=>r.scoreType==="모의고사");
  const gradeRows=internal.filter(r=>Number.isFinite(Number(r.level)));
  const avg=gradeRows.length?(gradeRows.reduce((s,r)=>s+Number(r.level),0)/gradeRows.length).toFixed(2):"-";
  const mockExams=[...new Set(mock.map(r=>r.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b));
  const latestMock=mockExams.at(-1)||"-";
  const subjects=[...new Set(mock.map(r=>r.subject).filter(Boolean))];
  const summary=`<div class="score-print-summary"><div><small>최근 내신 평균</small><b>${avg}</b><span>등급 평균</span></div><div><small>내신 자료</small><b>${internal.length}</b><span>과목·시험 기록</span></div><div><small>최근 모의고사</small><b>${escapeHtml(latestMock)}</b><span>${mock.length}개 성적</span></div><div><small>조회범위</small><b>${mode==="combined"?"종합":mode==="mock"?"모의":"내신"}</b><span>개인별 누적</span></div></div>`;
  const internalExams=[...new Set(internal.map(r=>r.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b));
  const internalSubjects=[...new Set(internal.map(r=>r.subject).filter(Boolean))];
  const internalTable=`<section class="print-section"><h2>내신 누적 성적</h2><table class="score-wide"><thead><tr><th>과목</th>${internalExams.map(e=>`<th>${escapeHtml(e)}<small>등급 / 원점수</small></th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${internalSubjects.map(subject=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(subject)}</td>${internalExams.map(exam=>{const r=internal.find(x=>x.subject===subject&&x.exam===exam);return `<td>${r?`<b>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.level))}등급</b><small>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.rawScore))}점 · ${escapeHtml(r.achievement||"")}</small>`:"-"}</td>`}).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></section>`;
-----
  const grade = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(grade) && grade >= 1 && grade <= 9 ? `grade-${grade}` : "grade-na";
}
function gradeBadgeMarkup(value, suffix = "등급") {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">-</span>`;
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">${escapeHtml(String(value))}</span>`;
  return `<span class="grade-badge ${gradeToneClass(number)}"><b>${scoreDisplay(number,2)}</b><small>${suffix}</small></span>`;
}
function mockGradeMiniMarkup(studentId, exam) {
  const order = ["국어", "수학", "영어", "한국사"];
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => row.studentId === studentId && row.scoreType === "모의고사" && row.exam === exam);
  if (!rows.length) return `<span class="mock-grade-empty">자료 없음</span>`;
  const sorted = rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=order.indexOf(a.subject), bi=order.indexOf(b.subject);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi);
    return String(a.subject).localeCompare(String(b.subject),"ko");
  });
  return `<div class="mock-grade-mini-strip">${sorted.map(row=>`<span><small>${escapeHtml(row.subject)}</small><b class="${gradeToneClass(row.level)}">${escapeHtml(String(row.level || "-"))}</b></span>`).join("")}</div>`;
}
function scoreTableMarkup(rows, mode = scoreMode) {
  const coreOrder = mode === "mock" ? ["국어","수학","영어","한국사"] : ["공통국어1","공통수학1","공통영어1","한국사1","통합사회1","통합과학1","한문","과학탐구실험1","체육1","음악"];
  const subjects = [...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=coreOrder.indexOf(a), bi=coreOrder.indexOf(b);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?999:ai)-(bi<0?999:bi);
    return a.localeCompare(b,"ko");
  });
  const groups = new Map();
  rows.forEach(row=>{
    const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
    const key=`${row.studentId}|${period}|${row.exam}`;
    if(!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key,{studentId:row.studentId,studentNo:row.studentNo,name:row.name,period,exam:row.exam,items:new Map()});
    groups.get(key).items.set(row.subject,row);
  });
  const grouped=[...groups.values()].sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.studentNo)) || a.period.localeCompare(b.period) || scoreExamOrder(a.exam)-scoreExamOrder(b.exam) || String(a.exam).localeCompare(String(b.exam),"ko"));
  const cell=(row)=>{
    if(!row) return `<td class="empty-score-cell">-</td>`;
    const trend=scoreTrend(row,readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]);
    const trendMarkup=trend.text!=="-"?`<em class="trend ${trend.cls}">${trend.text}</em>`:"";
    if(row.scoreType==="모의고사") return `<td><div class="score-cell-primary">${gradeBadgeMarkup(row.level)}${trendMarkup}</div><small>원점수 ${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)} · 표준 ${scoreDisplay(row.standardScore)}${row.percentile!==""?` · 백분위 ${scoreDisplay(row.percentile)}`:""}</small></td>`;
    const gradeValue = row.level === "산출제외" ? (row.achievement || "산출제외") : row.level;
    return `<td><div class="score-cell-primary">${gradeBadgeMarkup(gradeValue, Number.isFinite(Number(gradeValue))?"등급":"")}${trendMarkup}</div><small>원점수 ${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)} · 석차 ${escapeHtml(String(row.rank||"-"))}/${escapeHtml(String(row.enrollment||"-"))}${row.achievement?` · ${escapeHtml(row.achievement)}`:""}</small></td>`;
  };
  const title = mode === "mock" ? "모의고사 과목별 등급" : "내신 과목별 등급";
  return `<div class="data-card score-matrix-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>${title}</strong></div><small>전체 누적 또는 선택 학기를 표시합니다.</small></div><div class="score-matrix-scroll"><table class="score-matrix-table"><thead><tr><th class="student-col">학생</th><th class="exam-col">학기·시험</th>${subjects.map(subject=>`<th>${escapeHtml(subject)}</th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${grouped.map(group=>`<tr><th class="student-col"><b>${escapeHtml(group.studentNo)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(group.name)}</small></th><th class="exam-col"><b>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(group.period).replace("학년도 ","· "))}</b><small>${escapeHtml(group.exam)}</small></th>${subjects.map(subject=>cell(group.items.get(subject))).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div></div>`;
}

function scoreDisplay(value, digits = 1) {
-----
  semesterSource.forEach(row=>{const year=Number(row.schoolYear);const grade=Number.isFinite(year)?Math.max(1,Math.min(3,year-firstYear+1)):1;const sem=String(row.semester||"1").match(/[12]/)?.[0]||"1";const key=`${grade}-${sem}`;if(!semesterAverages.has(key))semesterAverages.set(key,[]);if(Number.isFinite(Number(row.level)))semesterAverages.get(key).push(Number(row.level));});

function runScoreQuery() {
  const scope = $("#scoreScope")?.value || "class";
  const classNo = $("#scoreClass")?.value || "";
  const studentId = $("#scoreStudent")?.value || "";
  const exam = $("#scoreExam")?.value || "";
  const target = $("#scoreQueryResult");
  let rows = readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || [];
  if (scoreMode === "internal") rows = rows.filter(x => x.scoreType === "내신");
  if (scoreMode === "mock") rows = rows.filter(x => x.scoreType === "모의고사");
  if (scorePeriod !== "all") rows = rows.filter(x=>scorePeriodKey(x)===scorePeriod);
  if (scope === "individual") {
    if (!studentId) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>학생을 선택하세요.</b><span>반을 먼저 선택한 뒤 학생을 고르고 조회하세요.</span></div>`; return; }
    rows = rows.filter(x => x.studentId === studentId);
  } else {
    if (!classNo) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>반을 선택하세요.</b><span>반을 선택한 뒤 조회 버튼을 눌러야 성적이 표시됩니다.</span></div>`; return; }
    const ids = new Set((readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(s => classNumberOf(s) === String(classNo)).map(s=>s.id));
    rows = rows.filter(x => ids.has(x.studentId));
  }
  if (scoreMode === "combined" && scope !== "individual") { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>종합성적은 개인별로 조회하세요.</b><span>반과 학생을 선택하면 학사상담 대시보드가 표시됩니다.</span></div>`; return; }
  if (scoreMode === "combined" && scope === "individual") {
    const student = (readonlyCache?.students || []).find(item=>item.id===studentId);
    if (!student) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>학생자료를 찾지 못했습니다.</b><span>학생정보 연결 상태를 확인하세요.</span></div>`; return; }
    target.innerHTML = combinedScoreDashboardMarkup(student, rows); return;
  }
  if (exam) rows = rows.filter(x=>x.exam===exam);
  if (!rows.length) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>조회 결과가 없습니다.</b><span>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(scorePeriod))}의 반·시험 자료를 확인하세요.</span></div>`; return; }
  let averageRows=rows, averageLabel='5등급제 평균';
  if(scoreMode==='internal'&&scope==='individual'){
    const periods=[...new Set(rows.map(scorePeriodKey).filter(Boolean))].sort();
-----
  const latestInternalExam = ["학기말고사","2차고사","1차고사"].find(exam=>summaryInternal.some(row=>row.exam===exam)) || "내신";
  const latestInternalRows=summaryInternal.filter(row=>row.exam===latestInternalExam);
  const latestInternalAverage=scoreAverage(latestInternalRows.length?latestInternalRows:summaryInternal);
  const mockGradeCard=(exam)=>{const examRows=mock.filter(row=>row.exam===exam).sort((a,b)=>{const ai=coreMockOrder.indexOf(a.subject),bi=coreMockOrder.indexOf(b.subject);return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi)||String(a.subject).localeCompare(String(b.subject),"ko");});return `<span class="mock-grade-metric"><small>${escapeHtml(exam)}</small><div>${examRows.map(row=>`<i title="${escapeHtml(row.subject)}">${escapeHtml(String(row.subject).replace("통합","").slice(0,2))}${gradeBadgeMarkup(row.level)}</i>`).join("")||"<em>-</em>"}</div><em>과목별 등급</em></span>`;};

  const baseFirst=admissionBase(student.id,firstMockExam), baseSecond=admissionBase(student.id,secondMockExam);
  const rules=readonlyCache?.admissionMinimums||[];
  const cutRows=(readonlyCache?.admissionCuts||[]).map(cut=>({...cut,diff:baseSecond.actual9==null?null:baseSecond.actual9-Number(cut.cut)})).filter(cut=>cut.diff==null||Number.isFinite(cut.diff)).sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(a.diff??99)-Math.abs(b.diff??99));
  const uniqueCuts=[],seenCuts=new Set();for(const cut of cutRows){const key=`${cut.university}|${cut.unit}|${cut.name}`;if(seenCuts.has(key))continue;seenCuts.add(key);uniqueCuts.push(cut);if(uniqueCuts.length>=7)break;}
  const targetRows=uniqueCuts.map(cut=>{const rule=rules.find(item=>item.university===cut.university&&(!item.unit||!cut.unit||item.unit===cut.unit))||rules.find(item=>item.university===cut.university);const firstCheck=rule?minimumCheck(rule,baseFirst.mock):null;const secondCheck=rule?minimumCheck(rule,baseSecond.mock):null;const internalOk=cut.diff==null?"판단대기":cut.diff<=0?"충족":cut.diff<=.7?"도전":"상향";const internalClass=internalOk==="충족"?"good":internalOk==="도전"?"warn":"bad";return `<div class="consult-target-row consult-target-grid"><b>${escapeHtml(cut.university)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(cut.unit||"-")}</span><span>${escapeHtml(cut.name||"-")}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(cut.cut,2)}</span><span class="consult-status ${internalClass}">${internalOk}</span><span>${escapeHtml(rule?.minimum||"최저자료 없음")}</span><span class="consult-status ${firstCheck?.result==="충족"?"good":firstCheck?.result==="미충족"?"bad":"warn"}">${escapeHtml(firstCheck?.result||"-")}</span><span class="consult-status ${secondCheck?.result==="충족"?"good":secondCheck?.result==="미충족"?"bad":"warn"}">${escapeHtml(secondCheck?.result||"-")}</span></div>`;}).join("")||`<div class="consult-empty">연결된 대학 입결 자료가 없습니다.</div>`;

  const cumulativeTables = `<section class="consult-cumulative-scores"><header><div><small>ALL RECORDS · ${escapeHtml(periodLabel)}</small><h4>누적 성적 전체보기</h4></div><span>학기 전환 버튼으로 범위를 바꿀 수 있습니다.</span></header>${internal.length?scoreTableMarkup(internal,"internal"):""}${mock.length?scoreTableMarkup(mock,"mock"):""}</section>`;
  const actualNineText=baseSecond.actual9==null?"산출 자료 없음":`${baseSecond.actual9.toFixed(2)}등급`;
  const targetRuleMarkup=`<div class="consult-admission-rule"><span><small>현재 9등급 환산 내신</small><b>${escapeHtml(actualNineText)}</b><em>${escapeHtml(baseSecond.preferred||"최근 내신")} 기준</em></span><p><b>판정 규칙</b> 과목별 석차÷수강자 백분위를 9등급 경계(4·11·23·40·60·77·89·96%)로 환산해 평균합니다. 대학 목표 내신 이하이면 ‘충족’, 목표보다 0.7등급 이내 높으면 ‘도전’, 그보다 높으면 ‘상향’으로 표시합니다.</p></div>`;
  return `<section class="consult-dashboard"><header class="consult-student-head"><div><small>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} · ${escapeHtml(student.className)} ${escapeHtml(student.number||"")}번 · function runScoreQuery() {
  const scope = $("#scoreScope")?.value || "class";
  const classNo = $("#scoreClass")?.value || "";
  const studentId = $("#scoreStudent")?.value || "";
  const exam = $("#scoreExam")?.value || "";
  const target = $("#scoreQueryResult");
  let rows = readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || [];
  if (scoreMode === "internal") rows = rows.filter(x => x.scoreType === "내신");
  if (scoreMode === "mock") rows = rows.filter(x => x.scoreType === "모의고사");
  if (scorePeriod !== "all") rows = rows.filter(x=>scorePeriodKey(x)===scorePeriod);
  if (scope === "individual") {
    if (!studentId) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>학생을 선택하세요.</b><span>반을 먼저 선택한 뒤 학생을 고르고 조회하세요.</span></div>`; return; }
    rows = rows.filter(x => x.studentId === studentId);
  } else {
    if (!classNo) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>반을 선택하세요.</b><span>반을 선택한 뒤 조회 버튼을 눌러야 성적이 표시됩니다.</span></div>`; return; }
    const ids = new Set((readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(s => classNumberOf(s) === String(classNo)).map(s=>s.id));
    rows = rows.filter(x => ids.has(x.studentId));
  }
  if (scoreMode === "combined" && scope !== "individual") { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>종합성적은 개인별로 조회하세요.</b><span>반과 학생을 선택하면 학사상담 대시보드가 표시됩니다.</span></div>`; return; }
  if (scoreMode === "combined" && scope === "individual") {
    const student = (readonlyCache?.students || []).find(item=>item.id===studentId);
    if (!student) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>학생자료를 찾지 못했습니다.</b><span>학생정보 연결 상태를 확인하세요.</span></div>`; return; }
    target.innerHTML = combinedScoreDashboardMarkup(student, rows); return;
  }
  if (exam) rows = rows.filter(x=>x.exam===exam);
  if (!rows.length) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>조회 결과가 없습니다.</b><span>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(scorePeriod))}의 반·시험 자료를 확인하세요.</span></div>`; return; }
  let averageRows=rows, averageLabel='5등급제 평균';
  if(scoreMode==='internal'&&scope==='individual'){
    const periods=[...new Set(rows.map(scorePeriodKey).filter(Boolean))].sort();
    const latestPeriod=periods.at(-1)||'';
    const periodRows=latestPeriod?rows.filter(row=>scorePeriodKey(row)===latestPeriod):rows;
    const preferredExam=['학기말고사','2차고사','1차고사'].find(name=>periodRows.some(row=>row.exam===name));
-----
    .filter(x=>x.stage!=='general'&&(filter==='all'||x.stage===filter))
    .sort((a,b)=>order[a.stage]-order[b.stage]||(a.base.actual9??99)-(b.base.actual9??99)||String(a.student.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.student.studentNo)));
}
function admissionManagementMarkup(group){
  const fallbackRule=uepManagementRule(group);
  const dataset=admissionManagementDataset(group),standard=dataset[0]?.standard||{name:fallbackRule.label,internal:fallbackRule.internal,mockText:fallbackRule.mockText};
  const managed=dataset.filter(x=>x.stage!=='general');
  const counts={all:managed.length,core:dataset.filter(x=>x.stage==='core').length,growth:dataset.filter(x=>x.stage==='growth').length,interest:dataset.filter(x=>x.stage==='interest').length};
  const rows=admissionManagementRows(group,admissionManagementFilter);
  const stageLabel={all:'전체 관리 대상',core:'핵심관리',growth:'성장관리',interest:'관심관리'}[admissionManagementFilter]||'전체 관리 대상';
  const filters=`<div class="admission-manage-filters growth-filters"><button data-admission-manage-filter="all" class="${admissionManagementFilter==='all'?'active':''}">전체 <b>${counts.all}</b></button><button data-admission-manage-filter="core" class="${admissionManagementFilter==='core'?'active':''}">핵심관리 <b>${counts.core}</b></button><button data-admission-manage-filter="growth" class="${admissionManagementFilter==='growth'?'active':''}">성장관리 <b>${counts.growth}</b></button><button data-admission-manage-filter="interest" class="${admissionManagementFilter==='interest'?'active':''}">관심관리 <b>${counts.interest}</b></button></div>`;
  const stageName={core:'핵심관리',growth:'성장관리',interest:'관심관리'};
  return `<div class="admission-manage-note growth-note"><b>${standard.name} 성장관리 기준</b> · 내신과 모의고사 기준을 함께 충족하면 핵심관리, 양쪽에 근거가 있으나 모의 일부기준만 충족하면 성장관리, 내신 또는 모의 한 종류만 충족하면 관심관리로 분류합니다. 내신은 실제 9등급 평균 포함</small></span><span><b>${rows.filter(x=>x.mockCore).length}</b><small>모의 핵심기준 충족</small></span><span><b>${rows.filter(x=>x.prep.after||x.prep.night||x.prep.selected).length}</b><small>학습·프로그램 이력</small></span><span><b>${rows.filter(x=>!x.prep.night&&!x.prep.after).length}</b><small>심화학습 추천 검토</small></span></div><div class="data-card"><div class="data-head admission-manage-grid"><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>반</span><span>관리단계</span><span>내신(5/9등급)</span><span>최근 모의고사</span><span>선정 이유</span><span>관리</span></div>${rows.map((x,i)=>`<div class="data-row admission-manage-grid growth-row" data-growth-student-id="${escapeHtml(x.student.id||'')}"><b>${escapeHtml(x.student.studentNo)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(x.student.name)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(/반$/.test(String(x.student.className||''))?x.student.className:`${x.student.className||''}반`)}</span><span><em <small>5등급</small></b><strong>${x.base.actual9==null?'-':x.base.actual9.toFixed(2)} <small>9등급</small></strong><em function recordEvidenceArticle(row){
  return `<article><input type="checkbox" data-record-evidence="${escapeHtml(row.id)}" ${recordWriterSelected.has(row.id)?"checked":""}><div><b>${escapeHtml(row.title)}</b><small>${escapeHtml([row.kind==="activity"?"활동":row.report?"보고서 제출":"프로그램 참여",row.program&&row.program.kind==="common"&&recordProgramArea(row.program)==="career"?recordDateText({...row.program,endDate:row.program.endDate||weekdaysBetween(row.program.date,"").at(-1)}):row.date].filter(Boolean).join(" · "))}</small></div>${row.report?.id?`<button class="link-button" data-open-student-report="${escapeHtml(row.report.id)}">보고서 보기</button><button class="link-button adopt" data-record-adopt-report="${escapeHtml(row.report.id)}">최종 보고서 채택</button>`:""}</article>`;
}
function recordEvidenceListMarkup(rows,area){
  if(!rows.length)return '<div class="empty-inline">선택할 근거자료가 없습니다.</div>';
  if(area!=="behavior")return rows.map(recordEvidenceArticle).join("");
  const order=["자율활동","진로활동","교육과정 내 봉사","교육과정 외 봉사"];
  return order.map(label=>{const items=rows.filter(row=>recordBehaviorCategory(row)===label);return `<section class="record-evidence-group behavior-${label.includes("진로")?"career":label.includes("봉사")?"volunteer":"autonomy"}"><header><b>${label}</b><span>${items.length}건</span></header>${items.map(recordEvidenceArticle).join("")||'<div class="empty-inline">연결 자료 없음</div>'}</section>`;}).join("");
}


function currentRecordStudent(){
  return (readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>s.id===recordStudentId)||null;
}
function careerDateOverride(program,student=currentRecordStudent()){
  if(!program||!student||recordProgramArea(program)!=="career"||program.kind!=="common")return null;
  const activityId=String(program.programId||program.id||"").replace(/^common-/,"");
  const classNo=recordStudentClass(student);
  const key=recordCareerDateKey(activityId,classNo);
  try{
    const value=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||"null");
    if(!value)return null;
    const range=semesterRangeForProgram(program);
    if(value.date&&(value.date<range.start||value.date>range.end)){
      if(value.confirmed||value.status==="edited"||value.status==="confirmed"){
        return {...value,rangeWarning:true,source:`${value.source||"담임 저장"} · 현재 프로그램 학기범위와 다름`};
      }
      localStorage.removeItem(key);
      return null;
    }
    return value;
  }catch{
    localStorage.removeItem(key);
    return null;
  }
}
function recordDateStatus(program,student=currentRecordStudent()){
  const override=careerDateOverride(program,student);
  if(override)return override;
  return {status:"range",date:"",period:"",source:"주간 운영기간",confirmed:false};
}
function dateKeyLocal(value){
  const d=new Date(value);if(Number.isNaN(d.getTime()))return "";
-----
    const groupedVolunteerMarkup=(rows,label)=>{
      const groups=new Map();
      rows.forEach(x=>{const key=activityName(x);if(!groups.has(key))groups.set(key,[]);groups.get(key).push(x);});
      return [...groups.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[0].localeCompare(b[0],'ko')).map(([name,items])=>{const hours=items.reduce((s,x)=>s+(Number(x.hours)||0),0);const dates=[...new Set(items.map(x=>x.date).filter(Boolean))].sort();return `<article><b>${escapeHtml(name)}</b><small>${escapeHtml([`${items.length}회`,hours?`${hours}시간`:"",dates.length?dates.join(' · '):""].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</small><p>${escapeHtml([...new Set(items.map(x=>x.organization||x.role||x.note).filter(Boolean))].join(' · ')||label)}</p></article>`;}).join('');
    };
    const makeActivityCard=([label,test,sizeClass])=>{
      const seen=new Set();
      const rows=bundle.activities.filter(test).filter(x=>{const key=x.id||[x.studentNo,x.title,x.date,x.hours].join("|");if(seen.has(key))return false;seen.add(key);return true;});
      const totalHours=rows.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+(Number(x.hours)||0),0);
      let body='';
      if(label==="교육과정 내 봉사"&&rows.length){
        const series=["자율·자치활동","진로활동","동아리활동"];
        body=`<div class="inside-volunteer-three-columns">${series.map(seriesName=>{const items=rows.filter(x=>insideVolunteerSeries(x)===seriesName);return `<section><header><b>${seriesName}</b><span>${items.length}건</span></header>${items.length?groupedVolunteerMarkup(items,seriesName):'<div class="empty-inline">연결 자료 없음</div>'}</section>`;}).join('')}</div>`;
      }else if(/봉사/.test(label)){
        body=groupedVolunteerMarkup(rows,label);
      }else{
        body=rows.map(x=>`<article><b>${escapeHtml(x.title||x.type||label)}</b><small>${escapeHtml([x.date,x.role,x.organization,Number(x.hours)?`${Number(x.hours)}시간`:""].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')||activityTypeLabel(x))}</small><p>${escapeHtml(x.note||x.evidence||x.status||'근거자료 확인')}</p></article>`).join('');
      }
      return `<section class="record-history-group ${sizeClass||""}"><header><h3>${label}</h3><span>${rows.length}건${/봉사/.test(label)&&totalHours?` · ${totalHours}시간`:""}</span></header>${rows.length?body:'<div class="empty-inline">연결 자료 없음</div>'}</section>`;
    };
    const officeCard=makeActivityCard(groupDefs[0]);
    const clubCard=makeActivityCard(groupDefs[1]);
    const scholarshipCard=makeActivityCard(groupDefs[2]);
    const outsideCard=makeActivityCard(groupDefs[3]);
    const insideCard=makeActivityCard(groupDefs[4]);
    const career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle);
    const careerValues=[career.hopeTrack,career.hopeMajor,career.aptitude].filter(v=>v&&v!=="-");
    const careerCard=`<section class="record-history-group record-career-profile compact"><header><h3>진로희망</h3><span>${careerValues.length?"등록":"0건"}</span></header>${careerValues.length?`<div class="career-support-strip compact record-career-strip"><span><small>진로희망계열</small><b>${escapeHtml(career.hopeTrack)}</b></span><span><small>진로희망전공</small><b>${escapeHtml(career.hopeMajor)}</b></span><span><small>대학적성데이터</small><b>${escapeHtml(career.aptitude)}</b></span></div>`:'<div class="empty-inline">연결 자료 없음</div>'}</section>`;
    return `<div class="record-history-grid record-history-three-columns record-history-layout-v700"><div class="record-history-small-stack">${officeCard}${careerCard}</div><div class="record-history-small-stack">${clubCard}${scholarshipCard}</div><div class="record-history-volunteer-stack">${outsideCard}</div><div class="record-history-inside-full">${insideCard}</div></div>`
  }
  if(recordMode==='sdgs') return sdgsDashboard(student,bundle);
  if(recordMode==='curriculum'){
    const byTerm=new Map();bundle.selected.forEach(x=>{const key=x.term||x.semester||'학기 미분류';if(!byTerm.has(key))byTerm.set(key,[]);byTerm.get(key).push(x);});
    const errors=selectionErrorsForStudent(student);const errorPanel=errors.length?`<section class="selection-error-panel"><header><div><small>ERROR REVIEW</small><h3>선택과목 오류검토</h3></div><span>${errors.length}건 확인 필요</span></header>${errors.map(x=>`<article><b>${escapeHtml(x.type)}</b><em>${escapeHtml(x.severity)}</em><p>${escapeHtml(x.detail||[x.subject,x.terms].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</p><small>${escapeHtml([x.subject,x.terms,x.status].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</small></article>`).join('')}</section>`:`<section class="selection-safe-panel"><b>✓ 선택과목 검증 정상</b><span>현재 선택과목 기준 문·이과 교차지원·과학과목 위계·학기간 중복과목 오류가 없습니다.</span></section>`;
    const profilePanel=`<section class="curriculum-career-profile subtle"><header><div><small>CAREER REFERENCE</small><h3>선택과목 상담 참고정보</h3></div><span>05_대학적성데이터</span></header>${careerSupportMarkup(student,bundle,true)}</section>`;
    const submit=selectionSubmissionInfo(bundle);
    return `<div class="curriculum-summary"><b>${escapeHtml(student?.name||'학생')} 선택과목</b><span>${bundle.selected.length}과목</span><small>최종 신청 ${escapeHtml(submit.date)} · ${submit.count}차 신청 · 8/3 안내 후 ${submit.modifiedAfterNotice?'수정함':'추가 수정 없음'}</small></div>${profilePanel}${errorPanel}<div class="curriculum-term-grid">${['2학년 1학기','2학년 2학기','3학년 1학기','3학년 2학기'].map(term=>{const rows=byTerm.get(term)||[];return `<section><header><h3>${escapeHtml(term)}</h3><span>${rows.length}과목</span></header>${rows.map(x=>`<article><b>${escapeHtml(x.subject)}</b><small>${escapeHtml([x.group,x.track,x.major].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</small><em>${escapeHtml(x.status||'상담용 신청')}</em></article>`).join('')||'<div class="empty-inline">신청 없음</div>'}</section>`;}).join('')}</div>${curriculumMessagePanel(student,bundle)}`;
  }
  return recordWriterWorkspace(student,bundle);
}

  const classCards=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1)).map(no=>`<button data-record-class="${no}" class="${String(recordClassNo)===no?"active":""}"><b>${no}반</b><span>${allStudents.filter(s=>recordStudentClass(s)===no).length}명</span></button>`).join("");
  const options=visibleClassStudents.map(s=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(s.id)}" ${s.id===student?.id?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(s.name)}</option>`).join("");
  const personalToolbar=`<div class="record-student-toolbar personal-only"><div class="record-class-cards">${classCards}</div><label>${recordClassNo}반 학생<select id="recordStudentSelect">${options}</select></label><button class="btn primary" data-open-student-dashboard="${escapeHtml(student?.id||"")}">학생 개인 대시보드</button></div>`;
  let content="";
  if(recordMode==="sdgs"){
    content=personalToolbar+recordPersonalContent(student,bundle);
  }else if(recordQueryMode==="class"){
    content=`<div class="record-query-bar"><div class="record-query-switch"><button data-record-query="class" class="active">반별 조회</button><button data-record-query="student">개인별 조회</button></div><div class="record-class-cards">${classCards}</div>${recordMode==='curriculum'?`<button type="button" class="btn ${recordCurriculumErrorOnly?'primary':'secondary'}" data-curriculum-error-only>${recordCurriculumErrorOnly?'오류학생만 보는 중':'오류학생만 보기'}</button><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-generate-bulk-curriculum-message>오류학생 일괄 문구 생성</button>`:''}</div><div class="record-class-summary"><div><small>${recordMode==="activities"?"ACTIVITY":recordMode==="curriculum"?"CURRICULUM":"FINAL RECORD"}</small><h3>${recordClassNo}반 한 번에 보기</h3><p>${recordMode==='curriculum'?'오류 배지를 누르면 해당 학생의 신청내역과 안내문을 확인할 수 있습니다.':'학생을 누르면 개인 상세로 전환됩니다.'}</p></div><span>${visibleClassStudents.length}명</span></div>${recordClassTable(visibleClassStudents)}${recordMode==='curriculum'?`<section class="curriculum-message-panel curriculum-bulk-panel" id="curriculumBulkPanel" hidden><header><div><small>BULK MESSAGE</small><h3>${recordClassNo}반 오류학생 일괄 안내문</h3></div><span>${visibleClassStudents.filter(s=>selectionErrorsForStudent(s).length).length}명</span></header><textarea id="curriculumBulkMessageText" readonly></textarea><div class="curriculum-message-actions"><button type="button" class="btn primary" data-copy-bulk-curriculum-message>전체 복사</button><a class="btn secondary" href="${escapeHtml(SUBJECT_REAPPLICATION_FORM_URL)}" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">수강신청 구글폼 열기</a></div><p>현재 반의 오류학생 안내문을 학번·이름 구분선과 함께 한 번에 생성합니다.</p></section>`:''}`;
  }else{
    content=`<div class="record-query-bar"><div class="record-query-switch"><button data-record-query="class">반별 조회</button><button data-record-query="student" class="active">개인별 조회</button></div></div>${personalToolbar}${recordPersonalContent(student,bundle)}`;
  }
  return `<div class="module-page records-v0601"><div class="record-main-tabs">${tabs.map(([id,label])=>`<button data-record-mode="${id}" class="${recordMode===id?"active":""}"><b>${label}</b><small>${id==="activities"?"학생 활동 사실 확인":id==="sdgs"?"진로·전공·탐구주제·사회적 가치 연계":id==="curriculum"?"학기별 선택과목":"학생 성장 자료"}</small></button>`).join("")}</div>${content}</div>`;
}
